Operad theory, the formalism for systems built by iterated substitution, suggests a complementary diagnostic: a model's direct answer to a compositional query should agree with the answer it produces by composing a stated decomposition of the same query. We instantiate this idea as operadic consistency (OC), a per-question signal. Across twelve instruction-tuned LLMs (4B to 671B parameters, open-weights and closed-source) on four multi-hop QA datasets, OC is strongly correlated with accuracy on every dataset (Pearson $r \in [0.86, 0.94]$, all $p \leq 0.0004$), and is the only signal we evaluate with $r \geq 0.85$ uniformly across all four datasets. Chain-of-thought self-consistency (CoT-SC; Wang et al., 2023) matches OC on HotpotQA and DROP ($r = 0.93, 0.87$) but drops to $r \approx 0.45$ on MuSiQue and StrategyQA. At the per-question level, OC contributes information beyond CoT-SC and semantic entropy on every dataset (cluster-robust $p \leq 10^{-16}$ for the OC coefficient), and the conclusion is robust to additionally controlling for constructed decomposition-aware baselines ($p \leq 10^{-13}$). The same signal yields selective-prediction improvements (accuracy at fixed coverage) over a tuned CoT-SC baseline at the equal-cost $K = 3$ budget (AUARC lifts of +0.086 to +0.096 and AUROC lifts of +0.092 to +0.164; 95% CIs exclude zero on every cell). On five frontier thinking models, where the decomposition is extracted from the model's own chain of thought, the same equal-cost comparison gives positive selective-prediction point-estimate lift on all 16 (dataset, budget, metric) cells tested, with 95% CIs excluding zero on 12 of the 16.

</details>


### [140] [HyperTool: Beyond Step-Wise Tool Calls for Tool-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13663)
*Yaxin Du,Yifan Zhou,Yujie Ge,Jiajun Wang,Xianghe Pang,Shuo Tang,Tuney Zheng,Bryan Dai,Jian Yang,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HyperTool，一种将多步工具调用折叠为单一代码块执行的新接口，解决LLM智能体中"执行粒度不匹配"问题，在MCP-Universe上将准确率从15.69%提升至35.29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强LLM智能体采用逐步原子化工具调用，每一步调用、观察和值传递都暴露在主推理轨迹中，导致"执行粒度不匹配"：局部确定性的工具工作流被展开为重复的模型可见决策，消耗上下文窗口并迫使模型在轨迹中管理低级数据流。

Method: 提出HyperTool——一个统一可执行的MCP风格工具接口。模型通过一个代码块调用HyperTool，该代码块可以按原始schema调用现有工具、操作返回值、本地传递中间结果，将确定性工具子程序折叠为单次外部调用。同时合成了跨工具组合任务的HyperTool格式轨迹，并在真实MCP环境中验证。

Result: 在MCP-Universe基准上，HyperTool将Qwen3-32B的平均准确率从15.69%提升至35.29%，Qwen3-8B从9.93%提升至33.33%，并在平均准确率上超越GPT-OSS和Kimi-k2.5。

Conclusion: HyperTool通过改变模型可见的工具执行单元，将确定性多步工具调用封装为单一调用，显著提升了多步工具使用能力，证明了执行粒度重构是提升LLM智能体工具使用效率的有效路径。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents commonly rely on step-wise atomic tool calls, where each invocation, observation, and value transfer is exposed in the main reasoning trace. This creates an \emph{execution-granularity mismatch}: locally deterministic tool workflows are unfolded into repeated model-visible decisions, consuming context and forcing the model to manage low-level dataflow in the trace. We introduce \textbf{HyperTool}, a unified executable MCP-style tool interface that changes the model-visible unit of tool execution. A model invokes HyperTool with a code block that can call existing tools through their original schemas, manipulate returned values, and pass intermediate results locally, folding deterministic tool subroutines into a single outer call. To train models to use this interface, we synthesize HyperTool-format trajectories from cross-tool compositional tasks and verify them in real MCP environments. On MCP-Universe, HyperTool improves average accuracy from 15.69\% to 35.29\% on Qwen3-32B and from 9.93\% to 33.33\% on Qwen3-8B, and surpass GPT-OSS and Kimi-k2.5 on average accuracy, showing that our HyperTool can substantially improve multi-step tool use.

</details>


### [141] [Influcoder: Distilling Decoders' Gradient Influence Rankings into an Encoder for Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13668)
*Dimitri Kachler,Damien Sileo,Pascal Denis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Influcoder是一种快速、低成本的大规模数据归因方法，基于影响函数范式，旨在高效识别训练数据中哪些样本对LLM的特定输出行为产生了条件性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力增长，高质量数据集 curation 需求上升。现有基于影响函数的数据归因方法虽然有效，但处理速度慢、存储开销大，无法在大规模数据集上实际部署。因此需要一种快速且紧凑的替代方案。

Method: 提出Influcoder方法，一种快速且成本效益高的大规模影响函数数据归因方法（具体技术细节摘要中未展开）。

Result: Influcoder实现了在大规模数据集上快速、低成本地执行基于影响函数的数据归因（具体实验指标摘要中未给出）。

Conclusion: Influcoder有效解决了传统影响函数方法在速度和存储上的瓶颈，使大规模数据归因变得切实可行，有助于高质量数据集的筛选与 curation。

Abstract: With the growth of LLMs' (Large Language Models) capabilities, there has been an increasing push to curate high quality datasets by filtering samples in the training data. In general, Data Attribution (DA) methods aim to estimate how individual samples in a training dataset can precondition a model to generate certain outputs. As an example, one might be interested in which samples in the data could be the source of toxic behavior after training the LLM. Many methods quantify this conditioning through the paradigm of influence functions. While methods of this family are effective in its function, they lack the necessary processing speed and storage compactness to be practically implemented on large datasets. We propose a method, Influcoder, as a quick and cost-effective approach to influence-based Data Attribution at scale.

</details>


### [142] [Learning to Reason by Analogy via Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13680)
*Zilin Xiao,Qi Ma,Chun-cheng Jason Chen,Xintao Chen,Avinash Atreya,Hanjie Chen,Vicente Ordonez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RA-RFT 是一种后训练框架，通过推理感知检索（而非语义相似度检索）来检索类比演示，并结合强化微调，使语言模型学会类比推理。在数学推理基准上，该方法显著优于标准强化微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG依赖词汇或语义相似度检索，不适用于复杂推理任务：语义相似的问题可能需要完全不同的解法，而表面不同的问题可能共享相同的推理模式。因此需要一种能够按推理收益而非语义重叠来检索上下文的方法。

Method: 提出RA-RFT框架：1）使用gold-relevance蒸馏训练一个检索器，使其按预期推理收益而非语义相似度对上下文排序；2）用检索到的类比演示，通过强化微调方法（如GRPO）微调策略模型，使模型在可验证结果奖励下利用推理轨迹进行学习。

Result: 在AIME 2025数学推理基准上，RA-RFT consistently优于标准强化微调。Qwen3-1.7B的average@32准确率提升7.1个百分点，Qwen3-4B提升2.8个百分点。推理感知检索能挖掘出互补的求解策略，为不同问题提供差异化的推理支架。

Conclusion: 推理感知检索是一条与奖励设计或训练课程设计正交的改进路径，两者可互补。RA-RFT证明了将类比推理机制融入检索增强生成流程的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has become a standard mechanism for grounding language models in external knowledge, yet conventional retrieval based on lexical or semantic similarity is poorly suited for complex reasoning tasks: a semantically similar problem may demand an entirely different solution strategy, while a superficially different problem may share the same underlying reasoning pattern. We propose Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RA-RFT), a post-training framework that teaches language models to reason by analogy. RA-RFT uses gold-relevance distillation to train a retriever that ranks contexts by expected reasoning benefit rather than semantic overlap, and then fine-tunes the policy model via reinforcement fine-tuning methods with retrieved analogous demonstrations, so the model learns to leverage reasoning traces under verifiable outcome rewards. We further analyze the diversity of retrieved contexts and find that reasoning-aware retrieval surfaces complementary solution strategies that provide distinct reasoning scaffolds for individual problems. Across challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, RA-RFT consistently outperforms standard reinforcement fine-tuning methods. For example, it improves AIME 2025 average@32 accuracy by 7.1 and 2.8 points over GRPO for Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B respectively -- suggesting that reasoning-aware retrieval is a complementary axis of improvement and orthogonal to advances in reward design or training curricula.

</details>


### [143] [EvoArena: Tracking Memory Evolution for Robust LLM Agents in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13681)
*Jundong Xu,Qingchuan Li,Jiaying Wu,Yihuai Lan,Shuyue Stella Li,Huichi Zhou,Bowen Jiang,Lei Wang,Jun Wang,Anh Tuan Luu,Caiming Xiong,Hae Won Park,Bryan Hooi,Zhiyuan Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvoArena是一个模拟环境动态演化的LLM Agent评测基准，涵盖终端、软件和社交三个领域。作者同时提出EvoMem，一种基于补丁的记忆范式，通过结构化的更新历史帮助Agent推理环境演化。实验表明当前Agent在EvoArena上表现不佳（平均准确率仅39.6%），而EvoMem在EvoArena及GAIA、LoCoMo等标准基准上均带来一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent评测大多假设静态环境，而真实部署场景中环境是动态变化的，Agent需要持续对齐不断更新的知识、技能和行为。当前缺乏对Agent在演化环境中持续适应能力的系统性评估基准。

Method: 提出EvoArena基准，将环境变化建模为终端、软件和社交三个领域中一系列渐进式更新序列。提出EvoMem记忆范式，以"补丁"形式将记忆演化记录为结构化更新历史，使Agent能够通过记忆变更来推理环境演化。该记忆机制可插入多种Agent架构。

Result: 当前Agent在EvoArena上平均准确率仅39.6%。EvoMem在EvoArena上带来平均1.5%的提升，在GAIA和LoCoMo标准基准上分别提升6.1%和4.8%。在需要连续完成相关演化子任务链的场景中，链级准确率提升3.7%。机制分析表明EvoMem改善了记忆中的证据捕获，更好地保留了完整的环境演化状态。

Conclusion: 在Agent评估和记忆设计中建模环境演化对于可靠部署至关重要。EvoArena为动态环境下的Agent评测提供了标准化平台，EvoMem通过结构化记忆演化有效提升了Agent对动态环境的适应能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have achieved strong performance on a wide range of benchmarks, yet most evaluations assume static environments. In contrast, real-world deployment is inherently dynamic, requiring agents to continually align their knowledge, skills, and behavior with changing environments and updated task conditions. To address this gap, we introduce EvoArena, a benchmark suite that models environment changes as sequences of progressive updates across terminal, software, and social domains. We further propose EvoMem, a patch-based memory paradigm that records memory evolution as structured update histories, enabling agents to reason about environmental evolution through changes in their memory. Experiments show that current agents struggle on EvoArena, achieving an average accuracy of 39.6% across evolving terminal, software, and social-preference domains. EvoMem consistently improves performance, yielding an average gain of 1.5% on EvoArena and also improving standard benchmarks such as GAIA and LoCoMo by 6.1% and 4.8%. Beyond individual tasks, EvoMem further improves chain-level accuracy by 3.7% on EvoArena, where success requires completing a consecutive sequence of related evolutionary subtasks. Mechanistic analysis shows that EvoMem improves evidence capture in the memory, indicating better preservation of complete evolving environment states. Our results highlight the importance of modeling evolution in both evaluation and memory for reliable agent deployment.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Identifiability Without Gaussianity: Symbolic World Models and Near-Infinite Temporal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12471)
*Seth Dobrin,Łukasz Chmiel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明，联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）仅在世界潜在动态服从高斯稳态过程时才能实现线性可辨识性（即线性恢复真实潜变量）。作者将此称为"高斯边界"，并证明对于任何非高斯物理系统，统计世界模型的表示误差随时间单调增长。进一步，作者提出物理根基符号架构（PGSA），证明其可在任意物理机制下实现精确线性可辨识性，且每步误差仅受数值精度限制，从而获得"近无限时间一致性"。统计世界模型在非高斯系统中无法达到该性质。四项定理的代数核心在Lean 4中形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出JEPA类架构的可辨识性受限于高斯假设，其时间一致性存在根本性上限。该限制并非世界模型的固有属性，而是统计对齐机制的产物。需提出一种超越高斯边界的架构，以实现任意物理系统上的长期一致世界模型。

Method: 提出物理根基符号架构（PGSA），通过符号化扎根于世界动力学的因果生成器，而非统计对齐。从理论上证明了三个核心结论：普适线性可辨识性、误差仅受数值精度约束、近无限时间一致性。同时证明统计世界模型在非高斯系统中无法达成该性质。四项定理的代数核心使用Lean 4与Mathlib4进行形式化验证。

Result: PGSA在所有物理机制下实现精确线性可辨识性；其每步表示误差仅由数值精度界定；可在无限多步转移中保持时间一致性。相反，任何统计世界模型在非高斯系统中均无法获得近无限时间一致性，与模型容量或数据量无关。

Conclusion: 符号化扎根于世界动力学的因果生成器，是实现近无限时间一致性的充分条件；在非高斯机制下，这也是唯一条件。该结论从根本上区分了统计对齐与符号扎根两条技术路径的能力边界。

Abstract: Klindt, LeCun, and Balestriero (arXiv:2605.26379) proved that Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) achieve linear identifiability, the linear recovery of the world's true latent variables, if and only if the world's latent dynamics follow a Gaussian, stationary process. This Gaussian boundary implies a fundamental limit on temporal consistency: for any non-Gaussian physical system, the representation error of a statistical World Model grows monotonically with time. We prove that this limit is an artifact of the statistical alignment mechanism, not a property of World Models in general. We introduce the Physics-Grounded Symbolic Architecture (PGSA) and prove three results: (1) a PGSA achieves exact linear identifiability for all physical regimes, regardless of the latent distribution; (2) the per-step error of a PGSA is bounded by numerical precision alone; and (3) as a direct consequence, a PGSA maintains temporal consistency for an unbounded number of transitions, a property we term near-infinite temporal consistency. We further prove that statistical World Models cannot achieve this property for any non-Gaussian system, regardless of model capacity or the volume of training data. The algebraic cores of four of the theorems are formalized in Lean 4 with Mathlib4 v4.31.0 (zero sorry placeholders); the Klindt et al. converse is taken as an external premise. The contrast establishes that symbolic grounding in the causal generator of the world's dynamics is the sufficient condition and, in non-Gaussian regimes, the only condition for near-infinite temporal consistency.

</details>


### [145] [Epistemic Uncertainty Is Not the Reducible Kind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12646)
*Robin Young*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文指出标准预测不确定性分类法在定义与度量之间存在外延不一致性：互信息度量将所有不确定性归为认知不确定性，但实际无法通过增加数据消除。作者提出应将认知不确定性细分为样本可约减和机制可约减两类，并证明集成分歧跟踪的是训练过程而非真正的认知项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的预测不确定性分类法（将认知不确定性定义为"可通过收集更多数据消除的部分"，并用互信息来度量）在直觉上被广泛接受，但作者发现定义与度量之间存在未被察觉的外延不一致性，需要重新审视这一基础框架。

Method: 通过显式构造反例（explicit construction）证明互信息度量将所有不确定性归为认知类，但数据量增加无法使其归零；进一步将不确定性分解为三部分——偶然不确定性、样本可约减认知不确定性和机制可约减认知不确定性，并推导了观测价值的精确恒等式；分析集成分歧在一致训练和插值极限下的渐近行为，并通过有限样本证伪检验和种子扫描实验进行验证。

Result: 分布内数据永远无法减少机制不可约减的不确定性，且通常会增加它；集成分歧在一致训练下塌缩至低于真实值的零，在插值极限下等于超参数缩放的初始化噪声。

Conclusion: 预测不确定性的标准二分法存在根本缺陷，可约减性应理解为（不确定性，获取类）对的属性，应替代为三分法：偶然不确定性、样本可约减认知不确定性、机制可约减认知不确定性。集成分歧作为认知不确定性的代理指标并不可靠。

Abstract: The standard taxonomy of predictive uncertainty defines epistemic uncertainty as the part removable by collecting more data, while the standard measure identifies it with a mutual-information term. We prove the definition and the measure are extensionally inconsistent. On an explicit construction, the measure assigns all uncertainty to the epistemic class, yet no quantity of training data reduces it. Reducibility is instead a property of the pair (uncertainty, acquisition class), and the dichotomy resolves into three parts: aleatoric, sample-reducible epistemic, and mechanism-reducible epistemic uncertainty. An exact identity for the value of an observation shows that in-distribution data never reduces mechanism-irreducible uncertainty and generically increases it. Ensemble disagreement, the deployed epistemic estimate, tracks the training procedure rather than the epistemic term. It collapses to zero beneath a positive truth under consistent training, and equals hyperparameter-scaled initialization noise under interpolation. A finite-sample falsification test and seed-swept experiments confirm the theory.

</details>


### [146] [Prediction-Powered Causal Inference by Automatic Debiased Machine Learning and Semi-Supervised Riesz Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12892)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了"预测驱动的因果推断"（PPCI）框架，在半监督设定下利用无标签辅助协变量来提高因果/结构参数的估计效率。作者推导了效率影响函数与效率界，并将其与去偏机器学习（DML）结合，提出EE-DML-PPCI和TMLE-DML-PPCI两种估计量，两者均达到效率界，方差小于仅用有标签数据的估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际场景中，有标签数据（含结局与协变量）获取成本高昂，而无标签辅助协变量往往丰富且廉价。传统因果推断仅利用有标签数据，其估计效率存在理论上限。本文旨在利用无标签辅助数据突破这一效率界，从而获得更小渐近方差的因果/结构参数估计量。

Method: 1. 首先在半监督设定下推导效率影响函数（EIF）与半参数效率界，证明辅助协变量可降低渐近方差。2. 将EIF与去偏机器学习（DML）框架结合：基于估计方程的方法称为EE-DML-PPCI，基于目标学习的方法称为TMLE-DML-PPCI。3. 由于EIF同时也是Neyman正交得分，依赖于Riesz表示元和回归函数，作者专门开发了半监督广义Riesz回归方法，并给出其收敛速率保证。

Result: 两种估计量（EE-DML-PPCI和TMLE-DML-PPCI）的渐近方差均达到所推导的效率界，且小于仅使用有标签数据可达到的效率界。半监督广义Riesz回归具有收敛速率保证。

Conclusion: PPCI框架成功将预测驱动方法与因果推断结合，通过利用无标签辅助协变量实现了对因果/结构参数更高效的半参数估计。该框架兼具理论优雅性（效率界推导）和实用灵活性（与DML的兼容），为半监督因果推断提供了系统的方法论基础。

Abstract: This study investigates semiparametric efficient estimation of causal and structural parameters in a semi-supervised setting. In our setting, unlabeled auxiliary regressors are available in addition to labeled observations consisting of outcomes and regressors. Our goal is to construct estimators of causal and structural parameters whose asymptotic variances are smaller than those of estimators constructed using only labeled data. We refer to this framework as prediction-powered causal inference (PPCI). We first derive the efficient influence function and the efficiency bound, which imply that the use of auxiliary regressors can attain a smaller asymptotic variance than the efficiency bound attainable from labeled observations alone. Then, by combining the efficient influence function with the debiased machine learning (DML) framework, we propose methods that we call DML-PPCI. If we construct an estimating-equation estimator, we refer to the method as EE-DML-PPCI; if we construct a targeted-learning estimator, we refer to the method as TMLE-DML-PPCI. The asymptotic variances of both estimators match our derived efficiency bound. In the construction of the estimators, estimation of the efficient influence function plays an important role. In our study, the efficient influence function is also a Neyman orthogonal score, which depends on the Riesz representer and the regression function. For Riesz representer estimation, we develop semi-supervised generalized Riesz regression with convergence rate guarantees.

</details>


### [147] [Robust State-Conditional Feature-Weighted Jump Models for Temporal Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13146)
*Federico P. Cortese,Alessio Farcomeni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种鲁棒的、特征加权的跳跃模型用于时间依赖聚类，结合Tukey双权损失函数实现抗异常值能力，并通过惩罚项促进时间平滑转移，同时允许各状态下特征权重可变，以识别状态特异性相关特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列聚类中，现有方法对异常值敏感，且无法有效区分不同状态下各特征的重要性差异，需要一种既能稳健处理异常值、又能自适应赋予特征权重的动态聚类方法。

Method: 构建特征加权跳跃模型，采用Tukey双权损失函数增强鲁棒性，引入惩罚项约束状态转移的平滑性，并通过额外参数控制各状态下特征权重的变异程度，使模型能为每个状态分配特异性的特征重要性。

Result: 模拟实验表明，该方法能够准确恢复真实聚类序列并可靠识别相关特征，尤其在存在异常值时显著优于竞争方法。两个实证应用验证了模型的有效性：一是1998-2000年科索沃冲突相关凶杀案数量分析，二是1949-2024年12个欧洲国家宏观经济表现分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒特征加权跳跃模型在时序聚类中兼具抗异常值能力和特征选择灵活性，在模拟和实证中均表现优异，适用于需要动态识别状态及其关键驱动特征的应用场景。

Abstract: We propose a robust feature-weighted jump model for time-dependent clustering. A penalty is used to encourage smoothness of transitions over time, while robustness is achieved through the use of a Tukey's biweight loss function. An additional parameter controls the variability of feature weights across states, allowing the model to assign state-specific relevance to each feature. We illustrate in simulation how the method accurately recovers the true cluster sequence and reliably identifies relevant features, outperforming competing approaches, particularly in the presence of outliers. We conclude with two empirical applications, one on the number of conflict-related homicides in Kosovo in the period 1998-2000, and another on macroeconomic performance of twelve European countries in the period 1949-2024.

</details>


### [148] [ProtoX-AD: Self-Explainable Time Series Anomaly Detection and Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13277)
*Aitor Sánchez-Ferrera,Elisabeth Wetzer,Kristoffer Wickstrøm,Michael Kampffmeyer,Robert Jenssen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ProtoX-AD 是一种基于原型的自监督时间序列异常检测框架，在保持与黑盒方法相当检测性能的同时，通过可解释的原型提供对异常特征的语义解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于自监督分类的时间序列异常检测方法虽然性能优异，但缺乏可解释性，无法揭示被标记异常的具体特征和类型，这限制了其在需要解释性的实际场景中的应用。

Method: 提出 ProtoX-AD 框架，在自监督学习范式下同时学习变换感知的潜在表征和可解释的原型（prototypes），通过原型匹配机制实现异常检测，并利用原型提供对异常模式的解释；同时支持系统性分析变换设计对检测性能和解释质量的影响。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，ProtoX-AD 的检测性能与黑盒同类方法相当，且相比现有的可解释基线方法，能提供更一致、语义更有意义的解释。

Conclusion: ProtoX-AD 成功地在自监督时间序列异常检测中实现了性能与可解释性的兼顾，为构建可信赖的异常检测系统提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in time series anomaly detection (TSAD) have highlighted the effectiveness of self-supervised classification-based approaches. These methods apply transformations to normal training samples, training a classifier to recognize transformation-specific patterns that help identify anomalies through increased classification errors. Despite their strong performance, a significant challenge is their lack of explainability, as they provide limited insight into the characteristics of flagged anomalies. To address this limitation, we propose ProtoX-AD, a prototype-based self-explainable framework for self-supervised TSAD. ProtoX-AD learns transformation-aware latent representations alongside interpretable prototypes, enabling both accurate anomaly detection and the identification of distinct anomalous profiles through prototype-based explanations. Additionally, it allows for systematic analysis of how transformation design impacts detection performance and explainability. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that ProtoX-AD achieves detection performance comparable to its black-box counterparts while offering more consistent and semantically meaningful explanations than existing explainable baselines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aitorzan3/ProtoX-AD.

</details>


### [149] [Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees for Stratified Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13295)
*Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees for Stratified Classification Cristian Buoncompagni,Stefano Pellegrino,Giulia Vannucci,Roberta Siciliano*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概率机器学习框架——同时潜在预算树（SLBT），用于在存在分层因素（如时间、空间或人口统计变量）的情况下构建分类树，并通过模型驱动的分裂规则实现对条件异质性的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可解释人工智能时代，单棵决策树因其易解释性重新受到关注。然而，标准的树生长过程并未针对条件分裂规则进行优化，尤其是在存在分层控制变量或潜在混杂因素时，现有方法难以有效捕捉不同组别间的异质性结构。

Method: 提出基于模型的分裂规则：将子节点解释为同时混合模型（如同时潜在预算模型及其约束版本）的潜在成分，并拟合到父节点。混合参数驱动观测值按不同组别分配到子节点，而潜在预算参数则更新各控制变量水平的响应类别特征。参数通过最小二乘法估计，并借鉴神经网络的视角。同时提供交互式可视化、视觉剪枝、决策树选择过程，并针对不平衡响应类别分布提出相应处理措施。

Result: 将该方法应用于肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）疾病进展中的性别差异研究，验证了其有效性。SLBT 库及相关树算法已在 GitHub 仓库中开源。

Conclusion: SLBT 框架为存在分层因素的分类任务提供了一种兼具可解释性与统计严谨性的树模型解决方案，能够揭示不同组别下的条件异质性，并具有实际应用的潜力。

Abstract: In the era of Explainable Artificial Intelligence, there is a renewed focus on single trees for their ease of interpretation. This paper introduces Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees, a probabilistic machine learning framework for classification trees in the presence of a stratification factor such as a temporal, spatial, or demographic variable, acting as a control variable or potential confounder. Standard tree growth procedures are not designed to optimize a conditional split rule. A model-based split rule is proposed in which child nodes are interpreted as latent components of a simultaneous mixture model, such as the Simultaneous Latent Budget Model and its constrained versions, fitted to the parent node. Mixing parameters drive the observations, differently for each group, to the child nodes whereas latent budgets parameters update the response classes profile of each level of the control variable. Parameters are estimated by least squares considering a neural network perspective of the model. An informative tree structure can be interactively visualized with interpretation aids on the node and the paths, including visual pruning and decision tree selection procedure. Suitable measures are proposed to handle an unbalanced response class distribution. The proposed methodology is applied to investigate gender-related differences in disease progression of Amyotrophic Lateral Sclerosis. The SLBT library with the various tree-based algorithms is available in the linked GitHub repository.

</details>


### [150] [Majority-of-Three is Optimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13614)
*Divit Rawal,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文给出了一个简短证明：三个独立一致分类器的多数投票在可实现PAC设置下是最优学习器，从而简化了先前投票学习器的算法结构和概率分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简化投票学习器的算法结构与概率分析，同时证明最简单的投票方案（三个独立一致分类器的多数投票）在可实现PAC学习中具有最优性。

Method: 采用简短证明，基于可实现PAC（Probably Approximately Correct）学习框架，分析三个独立一致分类器的多数投票机制。

Result: 证明了三个独立一致分类器的多数投票在可实现PAC设置下是最优学习器；该证明同时简化了S. Hanneke的算法以及K. Green Larsen对Bagging的分析。

Conclusion: 最简单的三分类器多数投票方案即可达到PAC学习的最优性，为投票学习器的理论和实践提供了更简洁的基础。

Abstract: We give a short proof that the majority vote of three independent consistent classifiers is an optimal learner in the realizable PAC setting. This proves optimality for the simplest voting scheme, while simplifying both the algorithmic structure and the probabilistic analysis of previous voting learners, including the algorithm of S. Hanneke and the analysis of bagging by K. Green Larsen.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Inferring resource selection and utilization distributions from irregular and error-prone animal tracking data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12566)
*Fanny Dupont,Brett T. McClintock,Jan-Ole Fischer,Marianne Marcoux,Nigel E. Hussey,Marie Auger-Méthé*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Langevin扩散模型的单阶段框架，利用拉普拉斯近似在TMB中同时处理测量误差和不规则采样，解决了传统两步法在栖息地选择推断中的偏差问题，并在仿真和独角鲸数据上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统栖息地选择分析方法假设规则采样且测量误差可忽略，但海洋系统中这些假设常被违反。常规的两步法（先正则化/滤波再建模）无法传播滤波阶段的不确定性，导致有偏估计。此外，现有状态空间模型难以处理依赖潜在真实位置的栖息地协变量，亟需一种能统一解决测量误差和时间不规则性的高效框架。

Method: 采用栖息地驱动的Langevin扩散模型（Habitat-driven Langevin diffusion）作为运动建模基础，天然适应不规则采样；通过拉普拉斯近似（Laplace approximation）同时对潜在真实位置进行积分，并沿潜在路径整合栖息地协变量，构建单阶段推断框架；利用TMB（Template Model Builder）高效实现，支持依赖潜在变量的协变量，通过最大似然估计进行快速推断。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该方法显著优于两步法：即使在较大测量误差和缺失数据下，也能准确恢复栖息地选择参数，并获得更精确的利用分布和轨迹重建。在独角鲸（Monodon monoceros）遥测数据的应用中，两步法将栖息地选择系数大幅收缩至零附近，而统一方法恢复了更强的信号。

Conclusion: 该框架为栖息地选择推断中长期存在的测量误差和时间不规则性挑战提供了计算高效的解决方案，适用于广泛的物种和环境类型。

Abstract: Habitat selection and space use are fundamental to understanding animal distribution. Traditional methods for quantifying habitat preferences from telemetry data assume regular sampling and negligible measurement error. However, these assumptions are routinely violated in marine systems. Practitioners typically regularize and filter the data before fitting models, but these two-step procedures do not propagate uncertainty from the filtering stage and can yield biased estimates. Habitat-driven Langevin diffusion models offer an elegant alternative, naturally accommodating irregular sampling. However, incorporating measurement error via a state-space formulation is challenging because habitat covariates depend on the latent true locations. We address this using the Laplace approximation to simultaneously integrate over true locations and account for habitat covariates along latent paths, yielding a single-stage framework efficiently implemented in Template Model Builder (TMB). By doing so, we provide the first TMB implementation capable of handling covariates that depend on latent variables, allowing inference via fast and efficient maximum likelihood estimation. Simulations show that our approach outperforms the two-step method, recovering habitat-selection parameters even under substantial measurement error and missing data, with more accurate utilization distributions and trajectory reconstructions. Applied to narwhal (Monodon monoceros) telemetry data, the two-step method substantially shrinks the habitat selection coefficient towards zero, while our unified approach recovers a much stronger signal. Our framework offers a computationally efficient solution to long-standing challenges of measurement error and temporal irregularity in habitat selection inference, applicable across a wide range of taxa and environments.

</details>


### [152] [Extending Prais-Winsten Regression to Panel Data with Higher-Order Autoregressive Errors: A Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12596)
*Ariel Linden*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文将Prais-Winsten AR(k)广义最小二乘变换扩展到面板数据，在Beck-Katz面板校正标准误框架下实现了`xtpraisk`命令，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟与Driscoll-Kraay估计量(`xtscc`)进行比较。结果表明：`xtpraisk`在所有条件下均保持接近名义水平的I类错误率和置信区间覆盖率，且检验功效高于`xtscc`；而`xtscc`在短序列长度下存在系统性标准误低估和I类错误膨胀问题，尤其在自回归阶数增加时更为严重。因此，当存在持续性高阶自相关且序列长度较短至中等时，`xtpraisk`更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面板数据中序列自相关的处理通常采用Newey-West型非参数估计（如Driscoll-Kraay的`xtscc`），但该方法在短序列、高阶自相关条件下表现不佳。Prais-Winsten变换作为一种参数化GLS方法，理论上在效率上具有优势，但此前缺乏将其推广至面板且系统比较两者有限样本表现的研究。

Method: (1) 将Prais-Winsten AR(k) GLS变换推广至面板数据，在Beck-Katz PCSE框架下实现Stata命令`xtpraisk`；(2) 设计大规模蒙特卡洛模拟，涵盖自回归阶数AR(1)–AR(3)、三种自相关情景、三种面板规模（N）、六种序列长度（T）、五种效应量，每种条件重复2,000次；(3) 比较`xtpraisk`与`xtscc`的I类错误率、检验功效、置信区间覆盖率、标准误校准及偏差。

Result: `xtpraisk`在所有条件下检验功效均高于`xtscc`，同时保持接近名义水平的I类错误率、置信区间覆盖率和标准误校准；`xtscc`在短T下标准误系统性低估、I类错误膨胀，且随AR阶数增加而恶化；两者均基本无偏；AR阶数误设不损害`xtpraisk`的推断性能；截面相关和面板规模对两种估计量的相对表现影响甚微。

Conclusion: 当统计效率和有效推断均为优先目标时，推荐使用`xtpraisk`，尤其是在持续性高阶自相关和短至中等序列长度的面板数据中。该研究为应用研究者提供了明确的估计量选择指导，并将`xtpraisk`确立为`xtscc`的重要替代方案。

Abstract: We extend the Prais-Winsten AR(k) generalized least squares (GLS) transformation to panel data within the Beck-Katz panel-corrected standard error (PCSE) framework and implement the method in the community-contributed Stata package xtpraisk. As the panel extension of Prais-Winsten, xtpraisk is the natural comparator to xtscc, the panel extension of Newey-West and implementation of the Driscoll-Kraay estimator. We conduct a Monte Carlo simulation to validate the statistical properties of xtpraisk and compare its finite-sample performance with xtscc. The simulation spans autoregressive orders 1-3, three autocorrelation scenarios, three panel sizes, six series lengths, and five effect sizes, with 2,000 replications per condition. Across all conditions, xtpraisk achieved higher power than xtscc while maintaining near-nominal Type I error rates, confidence interval coverage, and standard error calibration. In contrast, xtscc exhibited systematic standard error underestimation and inflated Type I error at short series lengths, with both deficiencies worsening as autoregressive order increased. Both estimators were essentially unbiased. Misspecification of the autoregressive order did not degrade xtpraisk's inferential performance, and cross-panel correlation and panel size had negligible effects on the relative performance of either estimator. The results indicate that xtpraisk is preferable when both statistical efficiency and valid inference are priorities, particularly under persistent higher-order autocorrelation and short to moderate series lengths.

</details>


### [153] [Computationally tractable robust differentially private mean estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12654)
*Kelly Ramsay*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种名为"气球均值"(balloon mean)的差分隐私均值估计器，通过在马氏球上迭代裁剪实现，兼具计算可行性和对异常值的鲁棒性，在污染数据场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私均值估计器通常在计算可行性和鲁棒性之间难以兼顾。需要一种既能抵抗重尾分布和异常值污染，又计算上易于处理、调参简单的差分隐私均值估计方法。

Method: 提出"气球均值"估计器，核心是在不断扩展的马氏球（balloons）上执行迭代裁剪过程。该方法满足零集中差分隐私（zCDP），仅依赖少量可解释的调优参数。

Result: 理论上给出了重尾和污染椭圆模型下的统计性能与鲁棒性保证。大量模拟实验表明，气球均值对重尾数据和污染数据具有鲁棒性，在污染场景下显著优于现有差分隐私均值估计器。

Conclusion: 气球均值成功在计算可处理性、差分隐私保障和统计鲁棒性之间取得平衡，是一种实用的隐私保护均值估计方法。

Abstract: We develop a new, differentially private mean estimator called the balloon mean. The main features of the balloon mean are that it is computationally tractable and enjoys robustness to outlying observations. It is based on an iterative clipping procedure over expanding Mahalanobis balls, or ``balloons.'' The method satisfies zero-concentrated differential privacy and depends on a small number of interpretable tuning parameters. We provide theoretical guarantees under heavy-tailed and contaminated elliptical models, characterizing its statistical performance and robustness to outliers. Extensive simulations demonstrate that the balloon mean is robust to heavy-tailed and contaminated data, and outperforms existing differentially private mean estimators in contaminated settings.

</details>


### [154] [Restricted Multivariate Spatial Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12677)
*Jihyeon Kwon,Harrison Quick*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个框架来量化多元条件自回归（MCAR）模型的信息量，并引入一种控制方法，通过重参数化确保模型对各亚组贡献可比，避免过度精确估计。以美国县级心脏病死亡数据（按种族和性别分层）进行实证演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BYM CAR模型在小区域健康事件建模中广泛应用，但近期研究揭示其可能过度信息量导致估计过度精确。MCAR作为多元扩展，通过共享风险因素和跨亚组借用信息，预期会更"信息化"，但其信息量水平此前未被量化。作者旨在填补这一空白，并提供控制MCAR信息量的方法。

Method: 1. 提出扩展先前工作的MCAR模型信息量度量框架；2. 在计算高效框架内对MCAR模型进行重参数化，以控制模型信息量，使其对各亚组的贡献可比；3. 将MCAR与BYM CAR在信息量和过度平滑方面进行对比；4. 使用按种族和性别分层的县级心脏病死亡数据验证受限MCAR的优势。

Result: 1. MCAR模型通过借用跨亚组的额外信息，确实比BYM CAR更具信息量；2. 提出的重参数化方法能有效控制MCAR的信息量水平；3. 受限MCAR在避免过度平滑和过度精确估计方面优于标准MCAR；4. 实证分析（县级心脏病死亡数据）展示了受限MCAR在跨种族和性别亚组中平衡信息共享的优势。

Conclusion: MCAR模型较BYM CAR更具信息量，但存在过度信息化的风险。作者提出的限制性MCAR框架，通过重参数化控制信息量，确保跨亚组贡献可比，在保持多结局/亚组联合建模优势的同时，避免了过度精确估计的问题，对小区域健康事件建模具有重要方法论意义。

Abstract: When modeling health events in small areas, the conditional autoregressive (CAR) framework of Besag, York, and Mollié (BYM) is widely used. For multiple outcomes, the multivariate CAR (MCAR) extension accommodates dependence among diseases that share risk factors, in addition to spatial dependence, and can also jointly model demographic subgroups for a single disease, allowing information to be borrowed across related populations. However, recent studies have shown that the BYM CAR model can be overly informative, leading to excessively precise estimates. While the MCAR model is expected to be more informative due to additional information shared across subgroups, its level of informativeness has not been previously quantified. We propose a framework to measure MCAR model informativeness as an extension of prior work and introduce a method to control it, ensuring the model contributes comparably to each subgroup. We achieve this through a reparameterization of the MCAR model within a computationally efficient framework. We demonstrate how the MCAR model compares with the BYM CAR model in terms of informativeness and oversmoothing and highlight the advantages of the restricted MCAR model using county-level heart disease death data stratified by race and sex.

</details>


### [155] [Bayesian machine learning approach for recurrent events studies using Soft Bayesian Additive Regression Trees (SBART)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12701)
*MengXing Chen,Debajyoti Sinha,Antonio Linero*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出RecSBART——一种基于软贝叶斯加性回归树（SBART）的非参数方法，用于复发性事件数据的建模。该方法将条件强度函数分解为时间常数基线、个体脆弱性随机效应和非参数协变量效应三部分，通过两层数据增广策略实现高效计算。模拟和真实数据均表明其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学研究中广泛存在复发性事件数据（如反复住院），现有方法在捕捉协变量与时间之间的非线性关系及未知交互效应方面存在局限，亟需一种兼具高预测精度和平滑估计能力的非参数方法。

Method: 提出RecSBART方法，将非齐次泊松过程的条件强度函数建模为三项乘积：时间常数基线、服从伽玛分布的个体脆弱性随机效应、以及基于SBART的非参数组分（捕捉协变量与时间的非线性效应及交互作用）。SBART采用软决策树集成，生成平滑估计。计算上通过两层数据增广方案——将泊松似然转化为潜变量正态似然，再嵌入SBART的贝叶斯后验采样框架，实现Gibbs采样器中的高效推断。

Result: 模拟研究表明，即使在建模假设不完全成立时，RecSBART在累积强度函数估计上的精度仍优于现有方法。在结直肠癌患者反复住院的贝叶斯分析中，RecSBART能够揭示并解释协变量间的复杂非线性关系。

Conclusion: RecSBART为复发性事件数据提供了一种灵活、准确且可解释的非参数贝叶斯建模框架，有效解决了传统方法难以处理复杂协变量效应的痛点，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Recurrent event data frequently arise in biomedical studies, where individuals may experience multiple recurrences of the same type of events, such as recurrent hospitalizations. This article introduces a nonparametric method for recurrent events under a Bayesian ensemble learning framework, called Soft Bayesian Additive Regression Trees (SBART), which combines multiple soft decision trees to achieve high predictive accuracy and a smooth estimator of the underlying intensity of the recurrent events. The proposed model represents the conditional intensity function of the non-homogeneous Poisson process as the product of a time-constant baseline, a subject-specific frailty random effect, and a nonparametric component capturing potentially nonlinear covariate effects and unknown interactions among covariates and time. A two-layer data augmentation scheme is employed to efficiently incorporate the SBART component within our computational algorithm. Simulation studies demonstrate that our method, called RecSBART in short, achieves superior accuracy in estimating cumulative intensity compared to existing approaches, even when our modeling assumptions are not true. With the Bayesian analysis of a study of recurrent hospitalizations of colorectal cancer patients, we further demonstrate our RecSBART method's ability to reveal and interpret the underlying complex relationships among covariates in a recurrent events study.

</details>


### [156] [Discrepancy Modeling with Intermediate Variables: A New Framework for Robust Gaussian Process Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12857)
*Henry Shaowu Yuchi,Michael Grosskopf,Aman Sharma,Nicolas Schunck,Jared O'Neal,Matt Menickelly,Stefan M. Wild*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的高斯过程校准框架，通过利用中间变量进行差异建模，结合结构化中间变量选择、离散化缩放高斯随机过程(S-GaSP)约束差异项、以及空间填充设计选择约束点，实现仿真器和差异的联合建模，在核物理结合能应用中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机实验的标准高斯过程校准框架通常忽略大量产生的中间变量，而不完美模型的校准若不利用这些中间变量则极具挑战性；同时，单独拟合仿真器和差异模型会引发可识别性问题。因此需要一种能够联合利用中间变量进行差异建模的鲁棒校准方法。

Method: 提出一个集成三项核心策略的鲁棒高斯过程校准框架：(1) 结构化中间变量选择过程，用于筛选对差异建模有用的中间变量；(2) 离散化缩放高斯随机过程(S-GaSP)作为差异项的约束先验，以正则化差异模型；(3) 空间填充设计策略选取约束点。三者协同实现仿真器与差异的联合建模。

Result: 在核物理结合能应用上验证了框架的有效性，所提方法在预测性能上优于多种基线方法，同时提供了有原则的不确定性量化，并缓解了可识别性风险。

Conclusion: 通过结构化的中间变量选择、S-GaSP先验约束和空间填充约束点设计，所提出的联合建模框架有效解决了标准校准中忽略中间变量和分步拟合导致的可识别性问题，在不完美模型的校准中展现出显著的预测性能提升和可靠的不确定性量化能力。

Abstract: Gaussian processes are widely used for surrogate modeling in computer experiments, which often produce numerous intermediate variables that are not explicitly used in standard calibration frameworks. Calibration of imperfect models can be challenging without leveraging these variables, while fitting the emulator and the discrepancy models separately also poses identifiability issues. In this work, we propose a robust Gaussian process calibration framework that leverages intermediate variables for discrepancy modeling. The framework integrates a structured intermediate variable selection process, a discretized scaled Gaussian stochastic process (S-GaSP) to constrain the discrepancy term, and a space-filling design strategy for selecting constraint points. This enables joint modeling of the emulator and discrepancy, improving predictive performance, providing principled uncertainty quantification, and alleviating identifiability risks. We demonstrate its efficacy on a nuclear physics application involving binding energies, where it outperforms baseline approaches.

</details>


### [157] [Volterra--Wiener--Kunchenko Orthogonalization: From Wiener--Hermite to Distribution-Matched Volterra Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12884)
*Serhii Zabolotnii*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文构建了分布匹配的Volterra-Wiener-Kunchenko (VWK)基，通过在有向Gram-Schmidt正交化下，将单项式在L²(P)中正交化，用作有限记忆Volterra辨识的任意多项式混沌坐标。核心贡献在于Volterra估计视角：证明了阶-2误设定惩罚定理，表明自归一化对角估计器在方差匹配高斯基下的超额风险由偏度系数控制；实验显示VWK经验Gram矩阵条件数远优于幂基；并用Lean 4形式化证明了任意N的二项式Krawtchouk行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限记忆Volterra辨识在非高斯输入下，单项式参数化是病态的；Wiener-Hermite展开仅对高斯白噪声输入去除病态性。需要构造一种适用于任意输入分布的良态正交基，使得从数据中进行Volterra辨识时，Gram矩阵条件良好，且估计器具有可控的误设定风险。

Method: 作者采用分布匹配策略：在输入分布P诱导的L²(P)空间中，对单项式序列进行有向Gram-Schmidt正交化，构造VWK基（本质上是经典正交多项式）。将其作为广义多项式混沌（gPC）和任意多项式混沌（aPC）框架下的坐标系统。理论分析包括：(1) 阶-2误设定惩罚定理，推导方差匹配高斯基下自归一化对角估计器的超额L²(P)风险，揭示偏度系数δ=μ₃/σ²的关键作用；(2) 条件数实验，对比总体匹配Gram（恒等矩阵）与有限样本设计Gram；(3) Lean 4形式化证明二项分布Krawtchouk多项式的显式行。估计方面，比较全最小二乘（基不变）与对角交叉相关/正则化坐标拟合。

Result: (1) 理论上：对称输入（偏度为零）下超额风险消失；偏度越大，误设定惩罚越重。(2) 数值上：n=2000时，中心化指数分布输入的VWK经验Gram条件数远优于幂基Gram，尽管随多项式阶数升高而退化。(3) 形式化验证：成功在Lean 4中证明了任意N参数的二项分布Krawtchouk行公式。(4) 方法定位：全最小二乘在固定跨度下是基不变的，因此VWK的优势体现在稳定对角交叉相关和正则化坐标拟合，而非宣称普遍预测优越性。

Conclusion: VWK基通过分布匹配正交化，为非高斯输入下的Volterra辨识提供了一个良态的坐标系统。其理论保证依赖于矩分析和有限记忆假设，且仅限于乘积输入律。核心实用价值在于改善Gram矩阵条件数，从而稳定对角估计和正则化回归，而非取代全最小二乘的预测能力。

Abstract: The monomial parameterization of finite-memory Volterra identification is ill-conditioned under non-Gaussian input, and the Wiener--Hermite expansion removes this ill-conditioning only for Gaussian white-noise input. We construct the distribution-matched Volterra--Wiener--Kunchenko (VWK) basis by oriented Gram--Schmidt orthogonalization of monomials in $L^2(P)$ and use it as an arbitrary-polynomial-chaos coordinate system for finite-memory Volterra identification from data, following the generalized polynomial chaos of Xiu and Karniadakis (2002) and the data-driven arbitrary polynomial chaos of Oladyshkin and Nowak (2012). The basis itself is classical; the contribution is the Volterra-estimation reading. First, an order-2 misspecification-penalty theorem shows that a self-normalized diagonal estimator in the variance-matched Gaussian basis incurs an excess $L^2(P)$ risk governed by the skew coefficient $δ=μ_3/σ^2$, vanishing exactly for symmetric inputs. Second, conditioning experiments separate the constructional fact that the population matched Gram is the identity from the finite-sample design Gram: at $n=2000$, the centered-exponential empirical VWK Gram remains far better conditioned than the power Gram, although it degrades with degree. Third, a machine-checked Lean 4 proof establishes the Binomial$(N,p)$ Krawtchouk row for arbitrary $N$. Full least squares over a fixed span is basis-invariant, so VWK stabilizes diagonal cross-correlation and regularized coordinate fits rather than claiming universal prediction superiority. The analysis is moment-based, finite-memory, and restricted to product input laws.

</details>


### [158] [Diagnostics-guided variance-inflated Fay-Herriot estimation from non-probability samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13025)
*Andrius Čiginas*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种诊断引导的方差膨胀Fay-Herriot估计量，用于基于非概率数据的小区域估计。该方法通过域级诊断指标识别IPW估计量可靠性较差的区域，并在FH模型观测方程中引入混合方差膨胀成分，使弱信息域向回归均值进行更强收缩。基于立陶宛企业真实数据的验证表明，该方法相比校准IPW显著降低了估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非概率数据源在小区域估计中日益重要，但逆概率加权(IPW)产生的域估计量可靠性在不同域间差异显著。标准Fay-Herriot平滑虽然能跨域借力，却将输入的面积级方差估计视为充分刻画了不确定性。当某些域覆盖弱、权重不稳定或辅助变量平衡差时，仅凭估计方差无法捕捉选择偏差风险，可能导致误导性推断。因此需要一种能够识别并应对这些弱信息域的方法。

Method: 提出诊断引导的方差膨胀FH估计量：(1) 从校准IPW域估计量出发；(2) 通过一组域级诊断指标（涵盖覆盖度、权重稳定性、辅助平衡等）总结各域IPW估计的可靠性；(3) 在FH观测方程中引入混合方差膨胀成分——诊断指标越差的域，其观测方差被膨胀越多，从而在模型中向面积级回归均值进行更强收缩。该方法实现了对弱信息域的自适应平滑。

Result: 基于立陶宛商业企业伪真实总体的真值已知验证显示，所提方法相比校准IPW估计量在估计误差上有显著降低，证实了诊断引导的方差膨胀策略能有效提升小区域估计精度，尤其对IPW可靠性较差的域改善明显。

Conclusion: 该方法将域级诊断信息系统地融入FH建模框架，通过方差膨胀机制实现了对IPW估计质量的差异化处理，为非概率样本下的小区域估计提供了一种更稳健的解决方案。未来可进一步拓展至其他非概率数据场景及更复杂的抽样设计。

Abstract: Non-probability data sources are increasingly considered in small area estimation, but inverse probability weighting (IPW) gives model-dependent domain estimators whose reliability may vary substantially across domains. Standard Fay-Herriot (FH) smoothing borrows strength across domains, yet it uses the supplied area-level variance estimates as if they fully described the uncertainty of the input estimators. This can be misleading when some domains have weak coverage, unstable weights, or poor auxiliary balance, since these features may indicate selection-bias risk not captured by the estimated variance alone. We propose a diagnostics-guided variance-inflated FH estimator for finite-population domain totals. The method starts from calibrated IPW domain estimators, summarizes their reliability through a small set of domain diagnostics, and introduces a mixture variance-inflation component in the FH observation equation. Domains whose diagnostics indicate weaker IPW information are thereby smoothed more strongly toward the area-level regression mean. A truth-known validation based on a pseudo-real population of Lithuanian business enterprises shows a substantial reduction in estimation error relative to calibrated IPW.

</details>


### [159] [Calibrating simplified vine copulas with a noise contrastive estimation approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13213)
*Michael Denis Kraus,David Huk,Claudia Czado*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于噪声对比估计（NCE）的校正策略，通过观察特定的校正因子对简化藤Copula模型进行局部调整，在保持计算可处理性的前提下，有效改善简化假设被违反时的模型拟合精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藤Copula的简化假设（即条件pair copula不依赖于具体的条件值）虽极大便利了估计，但在条件依赖性显著变化的应用中会导致模型误设。现有方法要么放弃简化假设而面临计算困难，要么缺乏有效的局部校正手段。作者希望在保持简化藤计算优势的同时，通过数据驱动的方式对其进行局部校正。

Method: 将已拟合的简化藤Copula作为噪声模型，采用噪声对比估计（NCE）框架，把密度估计重构为二分类任务（区分真实数据与噪声样本）。通过该监督学习方法，为每个观测样本导出校正因子，进而获得校正后的对数似然估计，实现对简化藤模型的局部调整。

Result: 模拟研究表明：当简化假设被违反时，所提校正方法能提供合理有效的调整，显著提升模型精度；当简化模型本身已充分时，校正保持中性，不会引入额外偏差。两个真实数据应用进一步验证了该方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 基于NCE的校正策略是一种有前景的工具，能在不放弃简化藤Copula计算可处理性的前提下增强其建模能力，为处理复杂相依结构提供了灵活且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Vine copulas provide a flexible framework for modeling complex multivariate dependence structures using only bivariate building blocks. Their practical success relies heavily on the simplifying assumption, which restricts conditional pair copulas to be independent of the specific conditioning values. While this assumption greatly facilitates estimation, it may lead to model misspecification in applications with pronounced varying conditional dependence. We propose a novel calibration strategy for simplified vine copula models based on observation-specific correction factors. These factors are derived using noise contrastive estimation (NCE), a supervised learning technique for density estimation that reframes the problem as a binary classification task with an easily sampled noise distribution. Treating the fitted simplified vine copula as the noise model, the NCE approach yields corrected log-likelihood estimates for individual observations, thereby locally adjusting the simplified vine toward the underlying data-generating dependence structure. Simulation studies demonstrate that the proposed calibration provides sensible and effective adjustments, improving model accuracy when the simplifying assumption is violated while remaining neutral when the simplified model is adequate. Two real-data applications further illustrate the practical benefits of the method. The results highlight NCE-based calibration as a promising tool to enhance simplified vine copula models without abandoning their computational tractability.

</details>


### [160] [Least Absolute Deviations Estimation for Sinusoidal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13242)
*Zehaan Naik,Debasis Kundu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究正弦回归模型中的鲁棒参数估计，采用最小绝对偏差（LAD）框架替代传统最小二乘法。提出一种模块化坐标下降算法，利用目标函数的部分凸性：幅度参数通过加权中位数计算更新，频率参数通过周期图启发的网格搜索加局部细化估计。理论上证明了估计量的强一致性和渐近正态性，并在合成数据和多个真实时间序列上验证了方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典正弦回归参数估计主要依赖最小二乘法，对重尾噪声和离群点高度敏感。需要一种对非高斯噪声具有鲁棒性的替代方法，同时保持计算可行性和统计推断的理论保证。

Method: 作者将估计问题形式化为LAD目标函数的直接最小化。提出模块化坐标下降算法，利用目标函数在幅度参数上的部分凸性：幅度参数通过加权中位数计算更新（相比传统单纯形优化方法大幅提升计算效率），频率参数通过周期图启发的网格搜索后进行局部细化。理论上在温和正则条件下建立了估计量的强一致性和渐近正态性。

Result: 理论上证明了所提LAD估计量在温和条件下的强一致性和渐近正态性。实验方面，在合成数据集以及Mauna Loa大气CO₂数据、航空客运数据和英国驾驶员死亡数据等真实时间序列上，验证了该方法在非高斯噪声环境下相比最小二乘方法的显著鲁棒性优势。

Conclusion: 所提方法为正弦信号估计提供了一种简单、可解释且鲁棒的替代方案，有效克服了最小二乘法对异常值的敏感性，兼具理论保证和计算实用性。

Abstract: We study robust parameter estimation in sinusoidal regression models within a least absolute deviations (LAD) framework. While classical approaches rely predominantly on least-squares formulations, they are known to be sensitive to heavy-tailed noise and outliers. We formulate the estimation problem as direct minimization of the LAD objective and propose a simple, modular coordinate descent algorithm that exploits the partial convexity of the objective: amplitude parameters are updated via weighted median computations, leading to substantial computational improvements over traditional simplex-based optimization methods, while frequency parameters are estimated via a periodogram-inspired grid search with local refinement. We establish strong consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed estimator under mild regularity conditions. Empirically, we demonstrate the method's effectiveness on both synthetic datasets and real-world time series, including the Mauna Loa atmospheric CO2 data, air passenger data, and UK drivers' deaths data, where robustness to non-Gaussian noise is essential. The proposed approach provides a simple, interpretable, and robust alternative to least-squares-based methods for sinusoidal signal estimation.

</details>


### [161] [Causal invariance in graphical models with latent variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13281)
*Marco Borriero,Monia Lupparelli,Giovanni M. Marchetti,Veronica Vinciotti*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了在存在未观测（隐）变量的情况下，如何利用因果不变性原理识别目标变量的观测父节点。作者刻画了隐变量存在时观测变量上的诱导图结构，并形式化给出了多变量高斯目标下检验因果不变性的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果不变性原理利用跨实验环境的因果效应稳定性来识别目标变量的父节点。然而，当部分父节点不可观测时，观测变量上的诱导图可能不再是有向无环图（DAG），且可能不唯一，这使得因果推断变得复杂。现有方法对此类配置下的不变性保持条件缺乏严格的形式化刻画。

Method: 针对隐变量父节点的相关配置，作者首先刻画了观测变量上的诱导图结构；随后，形式化定义了在隐变量存在时因果不变性得以保持的条件，并在此基础上，针对多变量高斯目标变量，推导并建立了检验因果不变性的充分必要条件。

Result: （1）对于隐变量父节点的相关配置，完整刻画了观测变量上的诱导图；（2）给出了因果不变性在隐变量场景下仍可用于识别观测父节点的形式化条件；（3）为多变量高斯目标建立了检验不变性的充要条件。

Conclusion: 即使存在未观测的父节点，在满足特定条件时，因果不变性原理仍然可用于识别观测父节点。本文为这一场景提供了严格的理论基础，特别是针对高斯情形给出了可操作的检验条件，扩展了因果不变性方法在含隐变量场景下的适用性。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to identify causal relationships among variables from observational or interventional data, typically represented by a directed acyclic graph (DAG). The causal invariance principle enables the identification of the causal parents of target variables by exploiting the stability of causal effects across different experimental settings. When some parents are unobserved, however, the induced graph over the observed variables may no longer be a DAG, and it may not be unique, complicating causal inference. For relevant configurations of latent parents, we characterize the induced graph and formalize the conditions under which causal invariance is preserved for the identification of the observed parents. Necessary and sufficient conditions for testing such invariance are formally established for a multivariate Gaussian target.

</details>


### [162] [Semiparametric Bayesian inference for causal mediation in cluster randomized trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13305)
*Woojung Bae,Michael Daniels,Joseph Hogan,Rajesh Vedanthan,Stavroula Chrysanthopoulou*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对聚类随机试验（CRT）中因果中介分析的稳健推断框架，采用参数贝叶斯模型对结局和中介变量建模，并创新性地引入基于相似性加权的贝叶斯自助法（SWBB）来估计不确定性，从而在聚类数量较少时也能获得有效的间接和直接效应估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在聚类随机试验中进行因果中介分析面临两大挑战：中介变量在聚类层面测量，以及聚类数量有限。标准推断方法依赖大样本渐近假设，在小样本情况下会导致有偏的方差估计和无效的置信区间，亟需一种适用于有限样本的稳健推断方法。

Method: 1) 采用参数贝叶斯模型对结局和中介变量进行建模，保证计算效率和可解释性；2) 提出一种新颖的相似性加权贝叶斯自助法（SWBB），通过定义聚类间的"距离"度量，使模型能够从"更近"的聚类中借取更多信息，避免对限制性参数假设的依赖；3) 将观测数据模型与因果假设相结合，估计自然直接效应和自然间接效应。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提方法在多种场景下均能达到名义覆盖率；通过对肯尼亚一项聚类随机试验的中介分析，验证了方法的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 该方法为聚类随机试验中的因果中介分析提供了一种在小样本条件下依然稳健的推断框架，兼具计算效率与统计可靠性，具有广泛的实践应用前景。

Abstract: Cluster randomized trials (CRTs) are frequently used to evaluate interventions, yet conducting causal mediation analysis in these settings remains challenging, particularly when the mediator is measured at the cluster level and the number of clusters is small. Standard inference methods often rely on asymptotic assumptions that fail in finite-sample settings, leading to biased variance estimation and invalid confidence intervals. In this paper, we propose a robust inference framework for causal mediation analysis in CRTs. We utilize parametric Bayesian models for the outcome and mediator to ensure computational efficiency and interpretability. Crucially, to quantify uncertainty, we specify a novel similarity-weighted Bayesian bootstrap (SWBB) with a `distance' metric between clusters; this avoids the need for restrictive parametric assumptions and allows the model to borrow more information from `closer' clusters. By combining observed data models with causal assumptions, our approach accurately estimates natural direct and indirect effects even with limited clusters. Simulation studies demonstrate that our method achieves nominal coverage probability across diverse scenarios. We illustrate the practical utility of our approach by assessing mediation in a CRT in Kenya.

</details>


### [163] [Disclosure risk in a geo-spatial setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13327)
*Peter-Paul de Wolf*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的针对专题地图统计披露的风险度量方法，该方法不受可塑性面积单元问题（MAUP）的影响，且直接关联目标人口的局部密度，并能处理多个单元关联到同一位置的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统计信息披露风险与效用度量大多忽略地图的空间特性；少数考虑空间特性的风险度量又受到可塑性面积单元问题（MAUP）的困扰——区域划分的微小变化（如网格的轻微平移）即可能导致风险评估结果的显著波动。因此，需要一种对区域划分方式稳健的空间风险度量。

Method: 作者提出了一种与MAUP无关的新型空间风险度量。该度量直接基于目标人口的局部密度，并能够处理多个统计单元对应于同一地理位置的情形。此外，该度量可进一步扩展，以纳入地图缩放级别和输出分辨率对（感知）风险的影响。方法通过一个包含模拟但逼真的企业位置数据集进行了实证验证。

Result: 利用模拟的企业位置数据集对所提出的风险度量进行了行为分析，展示了该方法在不同条件下的表现。具体定量结果未在摘要中详述，但验证了该度量对区域划分变化的稳健性。

Conclusion: 所提出的风险度量成功规避了MAUP问题，能够更真实地反映专题地图在统计披露中的空间风险，并具备良好的可扩展性（可适配缩放与分辨率因素），为专题地图的统计信息披露风险-效用平衡提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Using thematic maps to publish statistical information has become a popular visualization. As is the case with all statistical publications, thematic maps also have to deal with the balance between disclosure risk and utility. However, most risk and utility measures do not take into account the spatial character of a map. Some of the proposed spatial risk measures suffer from the Modifiable Areal Unit Problem (MAUP): slightly changing regional classifications may influence the risk. Indeed, even a small translation of for example a grid may influence that risk. We propose a new risk measure that does not suffer from MAUP. Moreover, our risk is directly related to the local density of the (target) population and takes into account that often multiple units may be connected to a single location. We show the behavior of our risk measure using an example dataset of fake but realistic locations of enterprises. Our risk measure can be adapted to take into account the effect on the (perceived) risk of zooming in or out and the effect of the used resolution.

</details>


### [164] [Smoothed-KL Reweighting: A Principled Account and Matching Rule for SNR-Based Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13433)
*Lei Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文从原理上推导了 Crowson 等人(2024)的 Soft-Min-SNR 权重，通过将 spread divergence 应用于每个时间步的局部匹配高斯代理，得到闭式权重 w(t,λ)=σ²/(σ²+λ)。该权重在方差保持调度下等价于 Soft-Min-SNR 的常数倍，并与 Min-SNR-γ 在 γ≈1/λ 时渐近一致，建立了软/硬重加权族之间的桥梁。此外，局部几何分析预测了高 SNR 阶段迭代效率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型训练中，Soft-Min-SNR 权重由 Crowson 等人(2024)以启发式方式提出，缺乏严格的理论推导。同时，Kingma & Gao(2023)仅从数据增强 ELBO 角度统一了单调对数 SNR 加权族，而本文旨在从分布平滑的互补视角，为 Soft-Min-SNR 提供原理性推导，并建立其与 Min-SNR 硬加权族的理论联系。

Method: 核心方法：将 Zhang 等人(2018)的 spread divergence 应用于扩散模型每个时间步的局部匹配高斯代理——该散度在对两个分布与高斯核卷积后再取 KL 散度——导出闭式权重 w(t,λ)=σ²/(σ²+λ)。具体分析分三支：(1) 证明方差保持调度下该权重等于 Soft-Min-SNR(γ'=(1+λ)/λ)的常数倍；(2) 通过渐近展开建立与 Min-SNR-γ 在 γ≈1/λ 时的等价关系；(3) 局部几何分析以 w³ 缩放 SGD 难度代理，预测高 SNR 阶段加速。实验在 CIFAR-10(线性和余弦调度)和 CelebA-64(余弦调度)上验证。

Result: 理论层面，成功将 Soft-Min-SNR 从启发式权重转化为具有严格推导的公式，并建立了软/硬重加权族的交叉对应规则。实验层面：(1) 匹配规则在 CIFAR-10 和 CelebA-64 上均成立；(2) 跨数据集切面轨迹验证中，|Ours - Min-SNR| 在 CelebA-64 seed-42 轨迹的 7 个中间检查点上平均相差 0.45 FID，约比任一方与 DDPM 的差距紧 3 倍；(3) 局部几何预测部分验证：CIFAR-10 线性调度下收敛速度比 DDPM 快约 21%，但该优势未迁移至余弦调度或 CelebA-64，三者最终 FID 相近。总体结论：最终 FID 持平，迭代效率数据集依赖，理论匹配规则成立。

Conclusion: 本文为 Soft-Min-SNR 提供了基于 spread divergence 的原理性推导，揭示其与 Min-SNR 家族的内在联系，从"平滑双方分布"而非仅平滑数据侧的视角补充了 Kingma & Gao 的统一框架。实证表明所推导的权重在最终生成质量上与现有方法持平，并在特定设置下具有迭代效率优势，但该优势依赖于数据集和调度策略。

Abstract: We give a principled derivation of the Soft-Min-SNR weight of Crowson et al. (2024). The spread divergence of Zhang et al. (2018) convolves both compared distributions with a Gaussian kernel before taking the Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence; applied to the per-sample local matched-Gaussian surrogate at each timestep, it yields the closed-form weight w(t,lambda) = sigma^2 / (sigma^2 + lambda). Three consequences follow. First, for variance-preserving schedules, w(t,lambda) equals a constant multiple of Soft-Min-SNR with gamma' = (1+lambda)/lambda, deriving a validated heuristic rather than introducing a new weight. Second, the same weight matches Min-SNR-gamma at leading order under gamma approximately 1/lambda, giving a cross-walk between the soft and hard reweighting families. Third, a local-geometry analysis scales an SGD-difficulty proxy by w^3 at high-SNR timesteps. Complementary to the objective-level account of Kingma & Gao (2023), who unified monotonic-in-log-SNR weightings as ELBOs of noise-augmented data, ours smooths both compared distributions rather than only the data side. Empirically, the matching rule holds on CIFAR-10 (linear and cosine) and CelebA-64 (cosine), with trajectory-wide confirmation on the cross-dataset cut: |Ours - Min-SNR| averages 0.45 FID across seven intermediate checkpoints on the seed-42 CelebA-64 trajectory, roughly 3x tighter than either reweighter's gap to DDPM. The local-geometry prediction is partially borne out: Ours converges about 21% earlier than DDPM at mid-training FID thresholds on CIFAR-10's linear schedule, where high-SNR damping headroom is largest, but this iteration-efficiency advantage does not transfer to cosine or CelebA-64, where all three methods reach similar final FIDs. Overall: final-FID parity with dataset-dependent iteration efficiency, plus a principled matching rule across the Min-SNR family.

</details>


### [165] [Valid Inference with Synthetic Data via Task Exchangeability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13629)
*Lezhi Tan,Tijana Zrnic*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了在科学研究中使用合成数据的统计原则，核心是一个称为"任务可交换性"（task exchangeability）的技术条件。当研究者能够识别出与当前任务可交换的历史真实数据任务时，便可获得可证明的有效性保证。作者开发了任务可交换性下的有效推断方法及其扩展，并在硅样本民意调查和AI自动评分评估中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据在社会科学（LLM生成的"硅样本"）、AI评估（LLM-as-a-judge）和蛋白质组学等领域日益普及。合成数据有助于扩大研究规模、加速发现，但同时也存在偏差、噪声和模型误设等根本性隐患。目前缺乏一套具有可证明有效性保证的统计框架来规范合成数据在科学研究中的使用。

Method: 作者提出"任务可交换性"这一核心技术条件：研究者需识别出具有真实数据的历史任务，并确保当前任务与这些历史任务在适当的数学意义下可交换。基于此条件，构建了有效统计推断方法，并进一步提出超越严格可交换性的扩展方案，以提供更广泛的保证。

Result: 在任务可交换性条件下，所提方法能够提供可证明的有效性保证。通过在民意调查（硅样本）和AI评估（自动评分器）两个应用场景中的实证演示，验证了该框架的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 任务可交换性为在科学研究中安全使用合成数据提供了一条具有理论保证的统计路径，使研究者能够在利用合成数据扩大研究规模的同时，对推断的有效性保持信心。

Abstract: There is a proliferation of work arguing for the use of synthetic data in scientific research. For example, social scientists are arguing for the use of LLM-generated "silicon samples" in pilot studies; AI evaluations increasingly rely on "LLM-as-a-judge" outputs; and proteomics research is accelerated by generative models that produce synthetic protein structures. These developments raise an intriguing possibility: synthetic data may help researchers ask more questions, run more studies, and accelerate discovery. But they also raise a fundamental concern: synthetic data can be biased, noisy, and misspecified. In this work, we propose statistical principles for using synthetic data in scientific research with provable validity guarantees. The key insight is a new technical condition that we call task exchangeability. Informally, this is a requirement that the researcher can identify historical tasks, for which real data is available, such that their current task of interest is exchangeable with the historical tasks in an appropriate mathematical sense. We develop methods for valid inference under task exchangeability, together with extensions that provide guarantees even beyond exchangeability. We demonstrate the framework on public opinion surveys with silicon samples and AI evaluation with autoraters.

</details>


### [166] [When Representative Samples Produce Worse Outcomes: Scale-up Decisions and Testing in Small-Budget RCTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13531)
*Hannah Li,Hongseok Namkoong,Isaac Scheinfeld*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"代表性样本总是最优"的传统观点，证明在小规模试点随机对照试验中，当显著性检验决定后续研究时，最优样本构成取决于预算规模：大预算时代表性样本最优，小预算时从单一同质子群体中抽样最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型随机对照试验常用于筛选干预措施，以决定是否进行大规模后续研究。然而，资源受限条件下招募代表性样本往往困难，且在统计显著性检验作为决策门槛时，代表性样本是否总是最优尚未被严格检验。错失有效干预或扩大有害干预的代价巨大，因此优化试点试验设计至关重要。

Method: 作者构建了一个理论框架，将试点RCT设计建模为一个优化问题：在给定预算约束下，选择样本构成以最大化下游期望结果改善。通过数学证明，分别分析了小预算和大预算两种极限情形下的最优抽样策略，并将小预算结果推广到使用RCT与显著性检验决定是否接受任何非适应性下游收益的更一般情境。

Result: 在大预算极限下，最优试点设计收敛于目标人群的代表性样本。但在小预算情形下，最优策略是从单一同质子群体中抽样，该子群体的选择取决于抽样成本以及设计者对异质性处理效应的先验信念。这一小预算结果具有一般性，可推广至其他受约束实验预算的情境。

Conclusion: 试点试验的最优样本构成并非一成不变，而是随预算规模发生相变：预算充足时应追求代表性，预算拮据时反而应聚焦单一同质群体。这对资源受限的试点研究设计具有重要启示，挑战了"代表性优先"的普遍认知。

Abstract: Small randomized controlled trials are often used to screen interventions before running larger follow-up studies. This is a critical phase of experimentation, as missing effective interventions or scaling up harmful ones can be very costly. A common proposal to mitigate these errors is to recruit samples that are representative of the target population, but this is often challenging in resource-constrained pilots. We challenge the narrative that representative samples are always superior by showing that when statistical significance testing determines whether interventions receive further study, the pilot trial composition that maximizes the downstream expected improvement in outcomes depends critically on its budget size. In the large-budget limit, the optimal pilot design converges to a sample that is representative of the target population. However, in the small-budget regime, the pilot designer maximizes expected impact by sampling only from a single homogeneous sub-population, chosen in a manner that depends on sampling costs and the designer's prior beliefs about heterogeneous treatment effects. Our proof of the small-budget result applies more generally when an RCT and significance test are used to decide whether to receive any non-adaptive downstream payoff, a result that may be applicable to other settings with constrained experimentation budgets.

</details>


### [167] [Smoothed Rank-Based Regression Estimation Using Wilcoxon Score Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13593)
*Feridun Tasdan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的基于秩的回归估计量，通过用平滑经验累积分布函数得到的平滑秩替代传统Wilcoxon秩-得分回归中的整数秩。该估计量继承了经典秩回归的稳健性，同时在重尾误差分布和处理结值（ties）方面表现出更高的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Wilcoxon秩回归虽然稳健，但使用离散的整数秩导致目标函数不可微，且在存在结值（ties）和重尾误差时效率有待提升。作者希望在不牺牲稳健性的前提下，通过平滑化技术改进秩回归的估计效率。

Method: 采用连续、非递减的核分布函数H替代经典指示函数，构建平滑经验累积分布函数，进而生成平滑秩。将这些平滑秩代入Wilcoxon得分函数，得到新的斜率参数估计量。在此基础上推导了回归系数的Wald型假设检验，并证明其渐近正态性。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟对比了所提估计量与OLS、经典Wilcoxon秩回归以及Theil-Sen估计量在正态、拉普拉斯、柯西和污染正态等误差分布下的表现。结果表明，所提估计量在所有场景下的相对效率均达到或超过经典秩回归，在存在离群值和重尾误差时增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 基于平滑秩的Wilcoxon回归估计量成功地在保持经典秩回归稳健性的同时提升了效率，特别是在处理结值和重尾分布方面具有明显优势，是一种有前景的稳健回归替代方法。

Abstract: This article proposes an improved rank based regression estimator obtained by replacing the ordinary integer ranks in the Wilcoxon rank-score regression procedure with smoothed ranks derived from a smoothed empirical cumulative distribution function. The smoothed ranks are computed via a continuous, nondecreasing kernel distribution function H that provides a differentiable approximation to the classical indicator function used in standard rank regression. Substituting these smoothed ranks into the Wilcoxon score function yields a new estimator for the slope parameter(s) of the simple and multiple linear regression model. We show that the proposed estimator inherits the robustness properties of classical rank regression while providing improved efficiency under heavy tailed error distributions and better handling of tied observations. A Wald type hypothesis test for the regression coefficients is derived and its asymptotic normality is established. A Monte Carlo simulation study compares new estimator with the ordinary least-squares (OLS) estimator, the classical Wilcoxon rank regression estimator, and the Theil and Sen estimator under several error distributions including the normal, Laplace, Cauchy, and contaminated normal. The proposed estimator achieves relative efficiencies at or above those of classical rank regression uniformly across all scenarios considered, with notable gains in the presence of outliers and heavy-tailed errors.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将幻觉检测重新定义为快速变化检测问题，基于CUSUM框架分析因果循环标注器的检测延迟，发现其检测延迟为11-13个token（远优于线性基线的31个token），但理论下界仅约1.3个token，差距主要源于所学评分函数仅捕获了特征所携带散度的1/4.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统幻觉检测评估使用全token AUC等分类指标，无法反映流式监测中"从幻觉发生到警报触发"的反应延迟这一关键性能维度。作者旨在引入序贯分析的视角，将检测延迟作为可测量、可优化的核心指标。

Method: 首先，将忠实/幻觉状态的切换建模为一阶马尔可夫过程，在RAGTruth上验证后纳入经典变点检测理论框架，推导Lorden下界（虚警率0.01下约1.3个token）。然后将因果循环标注器解释为带学习增量的CUSUM过程，通过控制虚警率的实验测量其检测延迟。最后利用Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理进行分解分析，将延迟差距归因于评分函数的信息捕获效率和有限时域效应。

Result: 因果循环标注器在匹配虚警率下检测延迟为11-13个token，显著优于线性逐token基线（31个token）。分解表明，延迟优势主要来自更好的逐token评分质量，而非时间累积效应。与理论下界相比仍存在数量级差距：所学评分仅实现特征散度的1/4.5，且该缺陷无法通过重校准消除，其余差距归因于有限时域效应。

Conclusion: 分类指标掩盖了检测延迟的结构特征，序贯分析使其可测量。当前检测器与理论最优之间仍有显著差距，改进方向在于提升评分函数对特征散度的捕获效率。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>


### [169] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Boltzmann注意力机制，将标准注意力推广为基于能量的Ising模型，通过引入可学习的成对耦合项来显式建模注意力决策间的交互作用，在字符级语言建模和括号匹配任务上均优于标准softmax注意力，且优势随序列长度增加而增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制仅通过单个query-key相似度计算相关性，softmax归一化虽引入位置间竞争，但缺乏对注意力决策间合作或拮抗交互结构的显式参数化建模能力，限制了模型捕捉位置间共注意力结构的上限。

Method: 提出Boltzmann注意力：将注意力模式建模为受Ising模型支配的能量基分布。在常规数据依赖的局部场（local fields）基础上，增加可学习的成对耦合项（pairwise couplings），使注意力分布服从Boltzmann分布。训练时可采用精确Boltzmann计算或 diabatic quantum annealing 采样策略进行近似。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配任务上，Boltzmann注意力在标准Transformer架构中一致优于softmax注意力；序列长度增加时优势更显著。四项消融实验证实改进来源于可学习的成对耦合项。此外，diabatic quantum annealing在保持与精确计算可竞争性能的同时，提供了一种实用的训练方法。

Conclusion: 显式的跨位置交互建模为基于注意力的序列建模提供了原则性的增强方案。Ising形式的引入不仅提升了建模能力，还为量子计算采样策略开辟了自然路径，展示了量子退火在注意力机制训练中的潜力。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [170] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ReCal（Reward Calibration）框架，通过分层奖励分解与分布感知优化来校准强化学习中路由策略的学习信号，解决多目标聚合带来的信用分配模糊和样本间奖励变异性导致的优化偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的 LLM 路由方法将正确性、格式行为等多目标聚合为单一标量奖励，导致信用分配模糊和优化信号冲突；同时，不同实例的奖励信号变异性大，容易引入优化偏差，使模型偏向简单样本而忽视信息量大的困难样本。

Method: 提出 ReCal 框架：(1) 分层奖励分解机制，对多目标奖励进行组件级优势估计，实现细粒度信用分配；(2) 分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知重加权和按数据集归一化来校准优化变异性，平衡不同难度样本的优化贡献。

Result: 在七个数据集上的实验表明，ReCal 在路由性能和训练稳定性上均持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: ReCal 框架通过奖励校准有效提升了 RL-based LLM 路由的质量与训练稳定性，为解决异构任务下的多目标强化学习路由问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [171] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: T2SP将时间序列转换为结构化符号程序（趋势、周期、显著事件），以训练无关的方式桥接时间序列与LLM的模态鸿沟，在编辑、描述和问答任务上相比原始字符串表示，持续提升性能、减少推理时间并降低失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在时间序列分析中存在模态不匹配问题：现有方法要么将原始数值序列化，要么在时间序列数据上微调LLM，这迫使LLM自行提取时序结构，导致长序列性能下降且计算开销大。需要一种更优的时间序列表示方法，使现成的LLM能直接利用其推理能力。

Method: 提出T2SP（Time-Series-to-Structured-Program），一种确定性、免训练的方法。将时间序列分解为趋势（trends）、周期成分（periods）和显著事件（salient events）三类结构要素，并以结构化符号程序（program-friendly format）表达，与LLM原生训练的文本/代码模态对齐。核心思想是将时序结构提取从模型端转移到表示端。

Result: 在编辑（editing）、描述（captioning）和问答（question answering）三个推理任务上评估，T2SP相比原始字符串表示，一致地提升了性能，缩短了推理时间，并降低了失败率。

Conclusion: T2SP提供了一种有效的时间序列与LLM之间的接口，使现成LLM无需微调即可利用其原生推理能力进行时间序列理解，验证了将结构化程序作为中间表示的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [172] [A Two-Parameter Weibull Framework for Diagnosing Transformer Weight Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18898)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 以Weibull分布作为Transformer权重矩阵的诊断框架，发现FFN和W_o（传输类）的k值高度集中（CV=0.51%），W_q/W_k（选择类）偏离Weibull族且程度受存储方式影响，λ随训练增长并与sqrt(η/λ_wd)正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚合统计量无法解析Transformer内部各组件、各层、各训练步的细粒度权重动态，需要一个架构无关、有理论锚点的诊断框架。

Method: 采用极值理论中的Weibull分布（两参数族），对Transformer中每个权重矩阵独立拟合。以初始化时高斯权重对应的HalfNormal分布给出k≈1.20作为锚点，通过中间80%概率图拟合（protocol贯穿全文），实现逐组件、逐层、逐检查点的诊断。

Result: 1）传输类（FFN模块+W_o）：终端k中位数在[1.186,1.204]窄带，跨12模型7架构CV=0.51%，对激活函数、归一化位置、模型规模(70M-14B)鲁棒；2）选择类（W_q,W_k）：偏离Weibull族，程度由存储方式决定——分离存储k∈[0.76,0.99]（深层），GQA模型k∈[1.10,1.16]（轻度），Pythia合并W_qkv处于过渡区并随训练预算T/τ单调变化；3）λ在训练中显著增长，在Pythia族内与sqrt(η/λ_wd)正相关（Pearson r=0.94）。两参数独立：k标记功能类别，λ标记训练进度。

Conclusion: Weibull分布为Transformer权重动态提供了简洁、可解释、架构无关的诊断语言。k参数揭示模块功能类别归属，λ参数追踪训练进度，二者信息独立，验证了Fan et al.(2025)的方向一致性。

Abstract: We apply the Weibull distribution -- a two-parameter family from extreme-value theory -- as a diagnostic framework for element-wise weight magnitude distributions in transformers. At initialization, i.i.d. Gaussian weights give |w| ~ HalfNormal, yielding k ~ 1.20 via middle-80% probability-plot fit (the protocol used throughout this work). This anchor makes k a principled, architecture-independent measuring stick for training dynamics; fitting each weight matrix independently at every layer at every checkpoint enables per-component, per-layer, and per-step diagnostics that aggregate statistics cannot resolve.
  Applying this framework to 12 model entries spanning 7 architectural families (Pythia, OLMo-1/2, LLaMA-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5/3) reveals three findings. First, FFN modules and the attention output projection W_o -- the Transmission Class -- fall in a narrow k band: median terminal k in [1.186, 1.204] across 12 entries (cross-family CV = 0.51%), shared across SwiGLU/GeLU activations, Pre-LN/QK-Norm placements, and 70M-14B sizes. Second, the attention input projections W_q, W_k -- the Selection Class -- depart from the Weibull family, with severity shaped by storage: separately-stored Q/K (OLMo-1, OLMo-2) yields k in [0.76, 0.99] (deep); GQA models yield k in [1.10, 1.16] (mild); Pythia's merged W_qkv occupies a transitional zone tracking training budget T/tau monotonically. Third, lambda grows substantially during training and scales with sqrt(eta/lambda_wd) within the Pythia family (Pearson r = 0.94, three Transmission kinds), directionally consistent with Fan et al. (2025). The two parameters carry independent information: k labels the functional class, lambda labels training progress.
  We release npm-weibull-py v0.4 (Python library) and DATABASE_v9_1 at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public .

</details>


### [173] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），通过基于查询点与支持点之间平方距离的方式，解决了Christoffel函数异常检测方法在高维数据下矩阵求逆的指数级复杂度瓶颈，在ADBench基准上超越14个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Christoffel函数异常检测方法虽具有强数学基础和计算轻量优势，但其核心矩阵的大小随数据维度指数增长，导致即使中等维度数据也难以处理，亟需解决其维度可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），基于查询点与支持点之间的平方距离构建，替代原有的多维多项式优化框架，从而规避了高维矩阵求逆的指数级复杂度，同时保留了原方法的on-off支持二分行为和精确支持形状捕获两大理论性质。

Result: 在ADBench基准数据集上进行广泛实验，UCF在平均精度（Average Precision）上一致超越14个最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: UCF成功突破了Christoffel函数在异常检测中的可扩展性瓶颈，为异常检测领域提供了一种鲁棒、理论完备且普遍适用的新方法，拓展了异常检测工具箱。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

</details>


### [174] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SRC提出了一种分支级别的模仿学习框架，通过在固定时间窗口内让学生自主探索，再由教师仅在局部进展中断时定位并纠正首个有害偏差，从而在干预时机与代理自主性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于专家轨迹的交互式Web代理训练中，专家干预时机难以把握：延迟干预导致错误累积使状态不可恢复，过早干预则使代理过度依赖专家策略，陷入单一刚性轨迹的局部最优。

Method: 提出Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC)：学生先在固定时间窗口内执行一段推测性轨迹段，然后教师仅在局部进展受阻时定位首个有害偏差并进行回滚修正；成功通过的轨迹经硬验证器筛选后存入轻量级的质量-多样性档案库，用于下一动作的监督微调。

Result: 在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集了977条验证器通过的轨迹和9,183个下一动作样本；固定时间窗口评审相比逐步评审在恢复能力与查询成本的权衡上表现更优，同时保留了通过验证器的多样化解法。

Conclusion: SRC通过推测性分支评审和回滚修正，有效解决了专家干预时机问题，在保持解决方案多样性的同时提升了数据采集效率和代理性能。

Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive.
