The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

</details>


### [175] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Chemotherapy Pharmacokinetics: Benchmarking the Clinical Estimator and Exposing Parameter Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12658)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PINNs can match the standard clinical NLS baseline on linear pharmacokinetics, but their true value emerges in nonlinear regimes where the closed-form estimator fails: they expose structural non-identifiability and naturally absorb sparse tissue measurements, recovering parameters that NLS cannot even attempt.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在化疗药代动力学中，血浆药物浓度可常规测量，但决定肿瘤杀伤和毒性的组织浓度无法直接观测。传统临床标准（NLS）依赖双指数解析解，仅在线性两室模型下有效；一旦引入非线性（如饱和消除），解析解不复存在，NLS会悄无声息地返回无意义的参数。需要一种统一框架来处理非线性、非恒定的部分观测问题，并诚实地报告可辨识性。

Method: 以物理信息神经网络（PINN）为核心，在线性两室模型和Michaelis-Menten饱和消除模型两个PK问题上，与临床标准NLS（非线性最小二乘拟合双指数解析解）和纯数据驱动MLP进行基准对比。PINN将ODE系统作为软约束嵌入损失函数，可在单次训练中同时输出血浆和组织曲线，并支持异质观测（血浆连续采样+稀疏组织采样）的统一融合。

Result: 线性两室模型：PINN与近最优的NLS差距仅一个小常数因子，且同时给出组织曲线；纯MLP在组织预测上误差约10倍。Michaelis-Menten模型：NLS因双指数形式误指定而返回无意义参数；PINN揭示该模型仅从血浆数据不可辨识（k12→0）。加入两个稀疏组织观测后，PINN在5个种子下将k21恢复至真值1%以内，Vmax和Km在1个标准差内，k12方向正确（0.02→0.82）但偏低约2σ——而NLS的双指数形式根本无法做此估计。

Conclusion: PINN的优势不在于击败NLS，而在于提供统一方案：在标准问题上匹配教科书估计器，在非线性/非恒定问题上暴露结构可辨识性，并将异质测量吸收进单一损失函数。这使其成为生物学中已知动力学但部分不可测场景下有吸引力的工具。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are an attractive tool for partial-observation problems in biology, where the governing dynamics are known but some compartments cannot be measured. Chemotherapy pharmacokinetics (PK) is a clean instance: drug concentration in plasma is routinely measured, but concentration in tissue -- which determines tumour kill and off-target toxicity -- is not. We benchmark a PINN against the standard clinical baseline (nonlinear least-squares on the analytical biexponential plasma solution, hereafter NLS) and a physics-agnostic neural baseline (a data-only MLP) on two PK problems. On the linear two-compartment problem, NLS is near-optimal; the PINN matches it to within a small constant factor while also producing the tissue curve in a single training pass, whereas the data-only MLP fails on tissue by roughly 10x. On a Michaelis-Menten extension (saturable elimination), the biexponential closed form no longer exists, so NLS is mis-specified and silently returns meaningless rate constants. The PINN instead exposes a deeper fact: the Michaelis-Menten two-compartment model is non-identifiable from plasma alone, and the PINN reports this honestly by converging to a basin with k12 -> 0. Adding two sparse tissue observations largely resolves identifiability: across five seeds the PINN recovers k21 to within 1% of truth and Vmax, Km to within one standard-deviation bar, while k12 moves in the correct direction (0.02 -> 0.82) but remains ~2 sigma below truth -- a recovery the closed-form NLS estimator cannot attempt at all, because its biexponential ansatz describes only plasma. Our claim is not that PINNs beat NLS. It is that PINNs offer a uniform recipe that ties the textbook estimator on the textbook problem, exposes structural identifiability that
  the textbook estimator hides, and absorbs heterogeneous measurements within a single loss.

</details>


### [176] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于状态的预测性维护方法，假设部件磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列，仅选取最近观测转换为表格数据后利用AutoML进行分类，在Scania Component X数据集上相比SOTA降低了成本并简化了建模流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车车队的基于状态预测性维护面临三大挑战：(1) 车辆生成的数据量巨大；(2) 通过传感器数据检测故障本身具有复杂性；(3) 难以找到成本效益最优的实施方案。现有方法在这些方面仍有改进空间。

Method: 核心假设是将被监测部件的磨损状态建模为单调非递减时间序列。具体方法为：仅从时间序列中选取最近期的观测值，将其转换为表格格式，然后利用面向表格数据的机器学习模型（含AutoML自动建模）进行分类预测。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上，所提方法相比当前最优（SOTA）方法降低了维护成本，同时通过AutoML简化了建模过程。

Conclusion: 基于单调非递减时间序列假设、结合最近观测表格化与AutoML分类的PdM方法，能够在降低维护成本的同时保持建模简洁性，验证了该方法在工业场景中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

</details>


### [177] [How Useful is Causal Invariance for Domain Adaptation in Finite-Sample Settings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12680)
*Julia Kostin,Kasra Jalaldoust,Elias Bareinboim,Samory Kpotufe,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究因果知识在监督域适应（sDA）中的有限样本收益。以线性回归为起点，推导了匹配的上下界，证明有限样本增益由候选预测器间的目标风险边际（target-risk margins）和有限源估计误差共同决定。当边际相对目标样本量足够大时，自适应聚合可匹配最佳候选预测器并避免负迁移；反之则无法可靠利用因果结构获得更快速率。该理论在真实因果基准上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于因果的域泛化研究多聚焦于总体层面的因果不变性，但此类总体因果不变性在有限样本条件下能否带来实际增益尚未被充分探索。特别是在监督域适应（sDA）场景中，我们通常可获取少量标注目标样本，如何利用（完全或部分）因果知识可证明地提升sDA性能是一个核心开放问题。

Method: 以线性回归为起始模型，将完全或部分因果知识形式化为一组不变或可能不变的特征子集，每个子集对应一个源训练的候选预测器。推导匹配的上下界，刻画有限样本增益的充分必要条件：当目标风险边际相对于目标样本量 $n_Q$ 足够大时，采用自适应聚合策略匹配最优候选预测器；当边际过小时，证明无算法能可靠利用候选集获得更快的有限样本收敛速率。进一步将边际与线性结构因果模型（SCM）中的结构偏移幅度建立联系。

Result: 得到了匹配的上下界，揭示有限样本增益的关键取决于：（1）候选预测器间的目标风险边际；（2）有限源估计误差。当边际足够大时，自适应聚合可避免负迁移；边际过小时，因果结构无法带来统计上显著的有限样本加速。在真实因果基准数据集上的实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 总体因果不变性的存在并不自动保证有限样本下的性能增益；目标风险边际的大小是决定能否有效利用因果结构的关键门槛。该发现为监督域适应中因果知识的实用价值提供了精细的理论刻画。

Abstract: Machine learning models often degrade when they are deployed on a target distribution that differs from the source distributions they were trained on. Recent work in causality-based domain generalization has shown how shared causal structure between domains can induce invariant predictors, e.g., models on a subset of features which have stable risk across structured domain shifts. However, the extent to which such population-level causal invariances can lead to gains in finite-sample settings remains underexplored. In particular, in practice we often have access to a few labeled target samples, a setting called supervised domain adaptation (sDA). In this paper, we explore when (full or partial) causal knowledge can provably improve supervised domain adaptation.
  As a first step, we study linear regression, where full or partial causal knowledge specifies a collection of invariant or possibly invariant feature subsets, each yielding a source-trained candidate predictor. We derive matching upper and lower bounds showing that finite-sample gains are governed by the target-risk margins separating the candidates, together with the finite-source estimation error. When these margins are sufficiently large relative to $n_Q$, an adaptive aggregation procedure can match the best candidate predictor while avoiding negative transfer relative to target-only learning. On the other hand, when the margins are too small, no algorithm can reliably exploit the candidate collection to obtain faster finite-sample rates. We further connect these margins to structural shift magnitude in linear SCMs and validate the theory on real-world causal benchmarks.

</details>


### [178] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DynamicPTQ，一种基于残差流动态特性的分阶段混合精度后训练量化策略。通过分析massive activations的层级涌现/消失模式（引入Jump Ratio和Historical Feature SNR两个指标），识别量化敏感层并仅对其激活使用8-bit精度，其余保持4-bit，在W4A4KV4下实现困惑度和零样本QA性能的持续提升，同时获得1.05-1.07倍吞吐增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法主要通过正交旋转或仿射缩放等静态变换平滑massive activations，但忽视了残差流跨层动态特性：massive activations沿网络深度呈阶段性涌现和消失，导致新注入的层级更新主导量化尺度、削弱历史残差信息，静态变换无法彻底解决这种动态量化不稳定性。

Method: 1) 提出Jump Ratio和Historical Feature SNR两个指标，刻画残差流中massive activations的动态行为及其对量化的影响；2) 基于该分析设计DynamicPTQ——动态后训练量化策略，从残差流动态中识别量化敏感层，仅对这些层分配8-bit激活精度，权重、KV缓存及其他激活保持4-bit；3) 可直接集成到QuaRot、SpinQuant、FlatQuant等强基线中。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上的实验表明，W4A4KV4量化下DynamicPTQ持续提升困惑度和零样本QA性能，同时实现1.05-1.07倍吞吐提升，且内存开销适度。

Conclusion: DynamicPTQ通过利用残差流动态选择性地对不同层使用混合精度，为鲁棒的低比特LLM推理提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR. This suggests that static transformation-based smoothing cannot fully resolve dynamic quantization instability caused by cross-layer residual changes. Based on this analysis, we propose DynamicPTQ, a Dynamic Post-Training Quantization policy for phase-aware mixed-precision activation quantization. DynamicPTQ identifies quantization-sensitive layers from residual-stream dynamics and assigns 8-bit activation precision only to these layers, while keeping weights, KV caches, and other activations in 4-bit precision. It can be directly integrated with strong PTQ baselines such as QuaRot, SpinQuant, and FlatQuant. Experiments on LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3 show that DynamicPTQ consistently improves perplexity and zero-shot QA performance under W4A4KV4 quantization, while achieving 1.05 to 1.07 times throughput improvement with modest memory overhead. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust low-bit LLM inference.

</details>


### [179] [Two-Layer Linear Auto-Regressive Models Estimate Latent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12691)
*Yahya Sattar,Sunmook Choi,Leo Maynard-Zhang,Yassir Jedra,Maryam Fazel,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了两层线性自回归模型在部分观测的线性动态系统数据上通过经验风险最小化训练时，其隐藏表示会自然逼近卡尔曼滤波，且在相似变换意义下与最优卡尔曼滤波的状态估计一致，并提供了有限样本下的预测误差、参数估计误差和隐状态恢复的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型在序列数据建模中广泛应用，但其学习到的隐表示究竟编码了什么信息、以及为何能有效工作，一直是一个开放的理论问题。作者旨在揭示自回归模型与经典状态估计（卡尔曼滤波）之间的内在联系。

Method: 作者基于三个关键洞察展开分析：(1) 证明卡尔曼滤波可由自回归模型以有界截断误差逼近；(2) 证明两层线性自回归模型的优化景观是良态的，即所有驻点要么是严格鞍点要么是全局极小值；(3) 利用经验风险最小化框架，给出有限样本下预测误差、参数估计误差和隐状态恢复的理论保证，并通过数值仿真验证。

Result: 理论证明两层线性自回归模型学到的隐藏表示在相似变换下与卡尔曼滤波的状态估计完全一致；优化景观不存在虚假局部极小值；提供了有限样本下的非渐近误差界。数值实验也验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 两层线性自回归模型在部分观测线性动态系统上训练时，会隐式地学会执行卡尔曼滤波，这为理解自回归模型的表示学习提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Auto-regressive models have emerged as powerful tools for sequential data, from language to video. Understanding how and why these models learn latent representations remains an open theoretical question. In this work, we demonstrate that when trained by empirical risk minimization on data from partially observed linear dynamical systems, two-layer linear auto-regressive models naturally learn to approximate Kalman filtering. In particular, we show that the learned hidden representation coincides, up to a similarity transformation, with the state estimates produced by the optimal (Kalman) filter, even though the model has no explicit knowledge of the underlying dynamics or state. The result follows from three main insights. First, we establish that the Kalman filter is well approximated by an auto-regressive model with bounded truncation error. Second, we show that despite non-convexity, the two-layer optimization landscape is benign, i.e., all stationary points are either strict saddles or global minima. Finally, as our main contributions, we provide finite-sample guarantees on prediction error, parameter estimation error, and latent state recovery. Numerical simulations support the theoretical results and demonstrate that the latent representations of auto-regressive models recover state estimates.

</details>


### [180] [A Stationary (and Therefore Compatible) Representation is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12488)
*Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Simone Ricci,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了d-Simplex固定分类器学习到的平稳表示满足兼容性的形式化定义，并提出结合交叉熵损失与对比损失的凸组合来捕获高阶依赖，在模型序贯微调与替换场景下实现了无需重新处理图库的最先进检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型更新后，新旧特征表示之间往往不兼容，导致需要重新提取所有图库图像的特征（回采），计算代价高昂。作者旨在实现"学习兼容表示"——即模型更新后特征可互换使用，无需回采，从而保证检索服务不中断。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明d-Simplex固定分类器学习到的平稳表示（stationary representations）自然满足兼容性的形式化定义。2. 损失函数设计：提出将交叉熵损失与对比损失进行凸组合，在d-Simplex固定分类器框架下训练，既能对齐一阶统计量，又能捕获表示间的二阶/高阶依赖关系，且等价于在兼容性约束下的交叉熵学习。3. 实验场景：考虑序贯微调以及模型偶尔被替换为更优模型的现实场景。

Result: 在模型更新与替换场景下，所提方法实现了无需重新处理图库图像的无中断检索服务，同时性能持续提升，达到了最先进水平。

Conclusion: d-Simplex固定分类器是学习兼容表示的有效基础，结合交叉熵与对比损失的凸组合可进一步捕获高阶依赖，实现真正的"即插即用"式模型更新，为未来工作奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Learning compatible representations aims to learn feature representations that can be used interchangeably over time whenever a model undergoes updates. In this paper, we demonstrate that stationary representations learned by d-Simplex fixed classifiers imply compatibility as in its formal definition. This result establishes a foundation for future works and can be directly exploited in practical learning scenarios. We address the challenge of learning compatibility using $d$-Simplex fixed classifiers when the model is sequentially fine-tuned. Learning according to a d-Simplex fixed classifier with the cross-entropy loss aligns feature distributions at the first-order statistics. Consequently, it may not fully capture higher-order dependencies in the representation between model updates. To address this issue, we demonstrate that training the model using a $d$-Simplex fixed classifier through a convex combination of the cross-entropy loss and a contrastive loss not only captures higher-order dependencies, but is also equivalent to learning with the cross-entropy under the compatibility constraints. We confirm our findings with extensive experiments also considering a new scenario where a pre-trained model is sequentially fine-tuned and occasionally replaced with an improved model. We show that stationary representations enable uninterrupted retrieval services (without reprocessing gallery images) while improving performance during model updates and replacements, achieving state-of-the-art. Code at https://github.com/miccunifi/iamcl2r.

</details>


### [181] [Reliability of Probabilistic Emulation of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12997)
*Sam F. Greenbury,Radka Jersakova,Paolo Conti,Marjan Famili,Christopher Iliffe Sprague,Edwin Brown,Jason D. McEwen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了两种概率预测方法——生成模型（扩散/流匹配）与CRPS训练的确定性集成模型——在二维时空物理系统中的不确定性可靠性，发现CRPS集成在覆盖率和推理速度上普遍更优，并发布了AutoCast和AutoSim工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散/流匹配等生成模型和CRPS训练的集成模型在物理系统概率预测中均展现出良好精度，但两类方法所产生不确定性的可靠性尚未被系统评估。作者旨在填补这一空白，在匹配模型规模和计算预算的条件下进行严格对比。

Method: 在多种二维时空物理系统上构建统一评估框架：1）在原始空间和潜在空间中分别训练生成模型与CRPS集成模型；2）通过预测区间的经验覆盖率检验概率模拟的可靠性；3）同时评估预测精度和推理计算效率；4）涵盖单步预测和自回归 rollout 两种场景。

Result: CRPS训练的集成模型在单步预测和自回归 rollout 中均表现出更可靠的不确定性（更好的覆盖率），且推理速度显著更快。原始空间中训练的生成模型覆盖率与CRPS集成相当，但推理延迟大得多。CRPS集成在潜在空间中训练时覆盖率不会显著退化，而生成模型则不然。两类方法均展现出良好的预测精度。

Conclusion: CRPS训练的集成模型在不确定性可靠性、计算效率方面整体优于潜在空间生成模型，是更具实用性的概率预测方案。为促进后续研究，作者开源了AutoCast（模型实现）和AutoSim（数据集生成）两个模块化框架。

Abstract: Two dominant approaches have emerged for generating probabilistic forecasts of physical systems: generative models, such as diffusion or flow matching; and ensembles of deterministic models with stochasticity injected, trained using the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) loss. While both approaches have demonstrated strong predictive accuracy, the reliability of their uncertainties has not been systematically assessed. We address this gap by developing a framework to evaluate both approaches across diverse 2D spatiotemporal physical systems, under matched model size and computational budget. We assess the reliability of probabilistic emulation by inspecting the empirical coverage of predictive intervals, while also considering accuracy and computational efficiency metrics. CRPS-trained ensembles typically achieve more reliable uncertainties on both single-step prediction and autoregressive rollouts, demonstrating better coverage than the standard alternative of training generative models in a latent space. Moreover, the CRPS approach offers significantly faster inference. When generative models are trained in ambient rather than a compressed latent space, which is often infeasible for high-dimensional problems, they exhibit comparable coverage to CRPS-trained ensembles, though with substantially larger inference latency. In contrast, when CRPS-trained ensembles are trained in latent space they do not show a marked degradation in coverage with respect to ambient space. Both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles demonstrate good predictive accuracy. To facilitate future research and application, we release AutoCast, a modular framework implementing both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles, alongside AutoSim, a flexible dataset generation package for rapid prototyping.

</details>


### [182] [Robustness Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks with Abstraction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12490)
*Li-Jen Lin,Chih-Duo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向RNN的抽象-精化验证框架，通过对预激活区间进行分支划分来消除非线性松弛引入的主导误差，并引入SHAP引导的时间步选择策略以控制序列分割的组合代价，在CIFAR10和MNIST基准上验证成功率和鲁棒性边界均优于纯抽象方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN的局部鲁棒性验证面临核心难题：非线性松弛（如ReLU、tanh、sigmoid的线性上下界近似）引入的误差会通过循环连接随时间步累积传播，导致可扩展的线性边界传播方法过于保守，尤其是在大量预激活区间跨越零点时，无法认证实际鲁棒的输入。现有方法缺乏在精度与计算代价之间的有效平衡机制。

Method: 提出抽象-精化（abstraction-refinement）框架：对导致松弛误差主导的预激活区间进行分支划分——在每条精化分支上，ReLU变为精确表示，而tanh和sigmoid等平滑激活函数可获得显著更紧的线性包络。为控制长序列中分裂的组合爆炸问题，引入SHAP（Shapley Additive Explanations）引导的时间步选择策略：按隐藏状态对验证目标的贡献度进行排序，仅精化最关键的时间步并按时序顺序执行。

Result: 在CIFAR10和MNIST笔画基准数据集上的实验表明，相比纯抽象基线方法，验证成功率与鲁棒性边界的紧致性均有持续提升；同时揭示了ReLU模型与tanh模型之间明显的运行时权衡。

Conclusion: 通过有针对性的区间精化和SHAP引导的时间步选择，可在不大幅牺牲可扩展性的前提下显著提升RNN验证的精度，为不同激活函数的选择提供了实际的效率参考。

Abstract: Certified local robustness verification for recurrent neural networks (RNNs) is challenging because approximation errors introduced by nonlinear relaxations can propagate through recurrent connections and accumulate over time. As a result, scalable linear bound propagation methods often become overly conservative and fail to certify inputs that are in fact robust, especially when many pre-activation intervals cross zero. We propose an abstraction-refinement framework for RNN verification that partitions such intervals to remove the dominant relaxation error: on each refined branch, ReLU becomes exact, and smooth activations such as tanh and sigmoid admit substantially tighter linear envelopes. To control the combinatorial cost of splitting in long sequences, we introduce a SHAP-guided timestep selection strategy that ranks hidden states by their contribution to the verification objective and refines only the most critical timesteps in temporal order. Experiments on CIFAR10 and MNIST stroke benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in verification success and robustness-margin tightness over abstraction-only baselines, while exposing clear runtime trade-offs between ReLU and tanh models.

</details>


### [183] [Net-Ev$^2$: A Generative Simulator for Network Event Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12494)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Net-Ev² 是一个用于模拟扰动事件在网络中传播影响的生成式模拟器，通过结构引导掩码预训练和拓扑感知扩散过程，仅需自然语言事件输入即可生成保持网络拓扑的仿真结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时建模事件的结构化属性与非结构化语义，且无法在模拟网络事件演化时有效捕捉拓扑结构，限制了真实世界网络中扰动事件影响传播的模拟能力。

Method: 提出 Net-Ev² 两阶段框架：(1) 结构引导掩码预训练，(2) 拓扑感知扩散过程（在去噪过程中使用类 U-Net 的图下采样和上采样）。推理时仅需自然语言事件输入。同时引入多模态基准数据集 Net-Ev²-6.5M 和拓扑保真度评估指标 JL-MMD。

Result: 在四个大规模路网的多模态对齐数据集上，Net-Ev² 展现出最先进的性能和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: Net-Ev² 有效解决了网络事件演化模拟中结构化属性与语义联合建模的难题，为减少现实试错提供了新的生成式仿真方案。

Abstract: Reducing real-world trial and error has long been a central goal of decision making, and generative simulators advance this goal by modeling the evolution of future states. An even more challenging yet meaningful task is simulating how disturbance events (e.g., accidents) propagate their impacts across real-world networks. The existing approaches fall short of modeling both structured attributes and unstructured semantics of events, and capturing topological structures in simulating network event evolution. Therefore, we are motivated to propose Net-Ev$^2$ ($\underline{\textbf{Net}}$work $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$ent $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$olution), a novel generative simulator that jointly leverages event cues while preserving network topology in simulations. Specifically, the framework consists of two stages, namely structure-guided masked pre-training and topology-aware diffusion process, which is achieved by U-Net-like graph downsampling and upsampling during denoising. At inference time, Net-Ev$^2$ can generate simulations using natural-language event input only, with greater flexibility for practical usage. Furthermore, we introduce Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M, a multimodal benchmark of aligned event and network traffic data across four large-scale road networks, as well as a new topology-aware metric, namely JL-MMD, to evaluate topological fidelity in generated network dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization ability of Net-Ev$^2$. Code is made available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/Net-Ev-2.

</details>


### [184] [Towards More General Control of Diffusion Models Using Jeffrey Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13240)
*Raphaël Razafindralambo,Rémy Sun,Frédéric Precioso,Jes Frellsen,Pierre-Alexandre Mattei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Jeffrey guidance框架，利用Jeffrey条件化规则以最小扰动方式引导扩散模型朝向预设目标分布，在FID降低和公平性约束等任务上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型引导方法（如classifier guidance）表达能力有限，在复杂场景下目标分布往往是隐式定义的（仅通过采样规则或启发式能量函数），缺乏一个原则性框架来将扩散模型控制拓展到更广泛的应用。

Method: 提出Jeffrey guidance框架，基于Jeffrey条件化规则（Jeffrey's rule of conditioning）：通过更新边缘分布使其趋近于预设目标分布，同时保持条件结构不变，从而最小化对联合分布的扰动。该方法将引导问题形式化为在保持条件分布的前提下逼近目标边缘分布的优化问题。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和FFHQ数据集上，以Inception嵌入作为目标分布进行Jeffrey guidance，FID指标大幅降低；在CelebA-HQ上应用于公平性约束，成功引导无条件扩散模型实现属性间的独立。

Conclusion: Jeffrey guidance为扩散模型控制提供了一个原则性、表达能力更强的框架，能够处理标准引导方法无法表达的应用场景，在生成质量和公平性约束等任务上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: A key strength of diffusion models lies in their flexibility, since their outputs can be controlled at sampling time through guidance. However, beyond simple cases such as conditional sampling, the target distribution is often left implicit, defined only through a sampling rule or a heuristic energy function. To address this, we propose Jeffrey guidance, a principled framework that extends diffusion-model control to applications beyond what standard guidance can express. It leverages Jeffrey's rule of conditioning to update marginal distributions towards a prescribed target, preserving the conditional structure and minimally perturbing the joint distribution. We first demonstrate Jeffrey guidance by targeting a prescribed embedding distribution. With Inception embeddings as the target, this leads to substantial reductions in FID on both CIFAR-10 and FFHQ. We further apply Jeffrey guidance to fairness on CelebA-HQ, updating an unconditional diffusion model to enforce independence between attributes.

</details>


### [185] [$μ$VLA: On Recurrent Memory for Partially Observable Manipulation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12497)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Daniil Zelezetsky,Aydar Bulatov,Artem Pshenitsyn,Yuri Kuratov,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: μVLA 通过在预训练 VLA 骨干网中引入最小化循环记忆（可学习记忆 token 跨时间步传递并通过自注意力更新），在受控隔离实验中系统校准了骨干网内循环的能力边界，证明其在部分可观测任务上显著提升成功率，同时在全可观测任务上无性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强 VLA 模型将循环机制与检索、压缩、辅助损失、分层记忆等额外组件混合，导致循环本身的贡献无法被独立评估。本文旨在通过受控隔离实验，纯粹研究在强预训练 VLA 骨干网中引入循环的作用与边界。

Method: 在 OpenVLA-OFT 骨干网中引入一小组可学习的记忆 token，在时间步之间传递并通过自注意力更新，使用截断时间反向传播（TBPTT）端到端训练，无辅助损失、无架构变更。实例化为 μVLA 族，参数化控制记忆宽度 m、TBPTT 长度 K 和记忆更新规则（跨步梯度或分离 EMA），使循环成为唯一变化因素。

Result: 在 MIKASA-Robo 上，μVLA 将五个训练任务的平均成功率从 0.42 提升至 0.84（最强设置），保留任务上达 0.23（无记忆基线为 0.07）；需要不同记忆结构的任务上性能保持接近基线。在 LIBERO 上，最强循环变体达 96.2% 平均成功率，表明全可观测下无性能退化。

Conclusion: 该研究标定了骨干网内最小循环的能力包络：在记忆结构与任务匹配时，循环本身足够解决问题；当任务需要不同的记忆结构时，则需额外机制。这为未来 VLA 模型中循环机制的设计提供了清晰的消融基准。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions from the current observation, an assumption that fails under partial observability, where decisions depend on information no longer visible. Existing memory-augmented VLAs simultaneously introduce recurrence, retrieval, compression modules, auxiliary objectives, hierarchical memory, or task-specific architectural changes, so the contribution of recurrence itself remains entangled with surrounding machinery. We present a controlled isolation study of recurrence in a strong pretrained VLA backbone. Our formulation augments the transformer with a small set of learnable memory tokens carried across timesteps and updated through self-attention, trained end to end with truncated backpropagation through time, with no auxiliary losses and no architectural changes. We instantiate this as $μ$VLA, a family of OpenVLA-OFT variants parameterized by memory width m, TBPTT length K, and the memory update rule (cross-step gradients or a detached EMA), so that recurrence is the only varying factor. On MIKASA-Robo, $μ$VLA improves average success rate on five training tasks from 0.42 to 0.84 at the strongest setting and reaches 0.23 on held-out tasks with the same memory structure versus 0.07 for the memoryless baseline. On tasks requiring different memory structure, performance remains near baseline. On LIBERO, the strongest recurrent variant achieves 96.2% average success, indicating no regression under full observability. We interpret these results as a calibration of the capability envelope of minimal in-backbone recurrence, identifying the regime in which it is sufficient and the regime where additional memory structure is required. Demos and videos can be found in https://avanturist322.github.io/mu-vla/.

</details>


### [186] [Accelerating Speculative Diffusions via Block Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13426)
*Alexander Soen,Hisham Husain,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对扩散模型的高效推测采样方案，成功将LLM中的块验证（block verification）机制适配到连续扩散领域，并形式化了无需训练的Free Drafter启发式方法，实现了最高6.3%的额外加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码在LLM中已取得成功，但将其核心的推测采样机制（接受-拒绝方案）适配到连续扩散模型面临关键挑战：离散空间中简单的残差分布采样在连续空间中实现高效计算非常困难。现有扩散模型的推测方法要么使用计算低效的采样技术，要么依赖替代方案，未能忠实实现原始推测采样机制，也无法利用块验证带来的接受率提升。

Method: 作者提出了一种新的推测采样方案，在连续扩散空间中高效实现了原始的接受-拒绝机制。核心贡献包括：(1) 将LLM中的块验证（block verification）技术适配到扩散模型，该技术可证明性地提高草稿的接受率；(2) 形式化并分析了Free Drafter——一种无需训练的启发式自推测草稿生成器，利用扩散模型自身的中间预测作为草稿。

Result: 通过引入块验证，所提出的Free Drafter在现有推测方法的基础上实现了最高6.3%的额外加速，且无需任何额外训练，除已有的并行验证过程外几乎无额外开销。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将推测采样的原始机制高效迁移至连续扩散模型，并通过块验证显著提升了草稿接受率，为扩散模型的推理加速提供了一种实用且低成本的解决方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding speeds up LLM inference by using a draft model to generate tokens, with an acceptance-rejection scheme that ensures that the output matches the target distribution. Adapting this to continuous diffusions is difficult because speculative sampling requires drawing from a residual distribution. While straightforward in discrete spaces, efficiently sampling this residual in continuous space is non-trivial. Consequently, existing diffusion adaptations either use computationally inefficient sampling techniques or rely on an alternative scheme. In this work, we introduce a novel scheme that efficiently implements the original speculative sampling mechanism for diffusion models. Our approach offers a critical advantage over current methods: it enables us to adapt block verification from LLMs to diffusions -- which provably improves the acceptance rate of drafts. Furthermore, we formalize and analyze the Free Drafter, a heuristic self-speculative drafter for diffusions that requires no training. By enabling block verification, our Free Drafter yields up to a 6.3% speedup over existing speculative methods with no additional training and negligible overhead beyond the existing parallel verification pass.

</details>


### [187] [Improving Crash Frequency Prediction from Simulated Traffic Conflicts Using Machine Learning Based Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12500)
*Xian Liu,Carlo G. Prato,Gustav Markkula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于规则的驾驶行为模型与机器学习(ML)行为模型在交通微观仿真中的表现，发现ML模型生成的冲突经极值理论(EVT)建模后能准确预测真实事故频率，而规则模型因缺乏地点特定校准则无法给出有意义的预测。但直接使用ML模型生成的模拟事故进行预测效果仍不佳，表明当前ML模型可真实再现冲突但尚无法生成逼真的事故。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则的微观仿真行为模型虽能合理复现交通流，但往往无法生成真实的冲突动态，限制了事故预测精度。随着大规模轨迹数据集的出现，ML行为模型提供了直接从人类驾驶数据中学习、进而提升仿真真实性和事故预测能力的潜在可能，有必要系统探究这一潜力。

Method: 选取英国利兹五个真实信号交叉口，分别使用标准规则模型和前沿ML模型进行交通微观仿真。采用二维TTC(碰撞时间)指标从模拟车辆轨迹中识别冲突，再应用极值理论(EVT)对冲突进行建模以预测事故频率，最后与真实事故数据对比验证。

Result: ML模型生成的冲突经EVT建模后的事故预测与真实事故数据一致；规则模型因缺乏对特定交叉口的校准未能给出有意义预测；直接使用ML模型生成的模拟事故预测真实事故频率效果较差。

Conclusion: ML行为模型在无需地点特定校准的情况下，能显著改善基于模拟冲突的事故预测，展现了良好前景；但当前ML模型仍无法生成逼真的模拟事故，这为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Traffic microsimulation combined with surrogate safety measures has increasingly been used as a proactive alternative to historical crash data for predicting crash frequency for current or planned road infrastructure designs. However, existing microsimulation-based safety studies have adopted simplified rule-based behaviour models, which reproduce traffic flow reasonably well but often fail to generate realistic conflict dynamics, limiting crash prediction accuracy. Recent advances in machine learning (ML)-based behaviour models offer a promising opportunity to potentially improve microsimulation realism and crash frequency predictions by learning human driving behaviour directly from large-scale trajectory datasets. To investigate this possibility, traffic microsimulation was conducted for five real-world signalised intersections in Leeds, UK, using both a standard rule-based model and a state-of-the-art ML model. Simulated vehicle trajectories were analysed using a two-dimensional Time-to-Collision metric to identify simulated conflicts, which were then modelled using Extreme Value Theory to predict crash frequency. Results show that conflicts from the ML model yielded crash predictions in line with the real-world crash data, whereas the rule-based model did not permit meaningful predictions, presumably due to a lack of model calibration to the specific simulated intersections. Directly using ML-generated simulated crashes to predict real-world crash frequency also yielded poor results, suggesting that while current ML models can realistically reproduce conflicts, they are not yet able to generate realistic crashes. Overall, the findings demonstrate that ML-based behaviour models are promising for improving crash prediction from simulated conflicts, without a need for location-specific model calibration, and suggest clear future directions for ML-based traffic microsimulation.

</details>


### [188] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可模拟过程"框架，允许学习者通过模拟器近似任意复杂且强依赖的数据生成分布，从而恢复经典独立数据下的VC维学习保证，并展示条件采样在此框架下的统计与计算优势，最终给出一个统一算法，其遗憾由过程的时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制，显著扩展了经典PAC模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论中的泛化保证高度依赖数据独立性假设，而强依赖数据下的结果极为有限。作者旨在弥合这一差距，探索在数据具有任意复杂依赖结构时，恢复独立同分布情形下学习保证所需的最小假设条件。

Method: 引入"可模拟过程"（simulatable processes）框架：学习者可访问一个能近似数据生成分布的模拟器（simulator）。基于此，将经典VC维分析推广至任意依赖过程，并利用时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度来刻画过程的复杂度，设计出能同时学习所有可在有界多项式时间内采样的VC类的单一算法。

Result: （1）在有模拟器访问的条件下，可恢复与经典独立数据设置相同的VC维误差界；（2）条件采样在此框架下展现出严格的统计与计算优势；（3）给出单一算法，对所有可在有界多项式时间内采样的过程同时学习任意给定VC类，其遗憾由过程的时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制。

Conclusion: "可模拟过程"框架为强依赖数据下的学习理论提供了统一视角，将PAC学习的适用范围从独立数据显著扩展至任意可模拟的依赖过程，揭示了模拟器访问与条件采样作为核心假设的强大能力。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [189] [Policy-driven Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy QoT Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12501)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Conformal QoT框架，将统计保证的QoT估计与操作决策策略相结合，在域偏移下实现可靠的光路可行性预测，在开放数据集上准确率从92%提升至99.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QoT（Quality of Transmission）估计方法在面临域偏移（domain shift）时缺乏统计可靠性保证，难以满足光网络运维对光路可行性预测的可靠性需求。

Method: 提出Conformal QoT框架，采用共形预测（Conformal Prediction）技术，将统计保证机制与操作决策策略相结合，为QoT估计提供概率意义上的可靠性保证。

Result: 在开放数据集上，将光路可行性预测准确率从92%显著提升至99.6%。

Conclusion: Conformal QoT框架通过策略驱动的方式融合共形预测，有效应对域偏移问题，实现了高可靠性的光路可行性预测，为光网络自动化运维提供了具有统计保证的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose Conformal QoT, a policy-driven framework that combines statistically guaranteed QoT estimation with operational decision policies, enabling reliable lightpath-feasibility predictions under domain shift and improving accuracy from 92\% to 99.6\% on open datasets.

</details>


### [190] [Dolph2Vec: Self-Supervised Representations of Dolphin Vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12503)
*Chiara Semenzin,Faadil Mustun,Roberto Dessi,Pierre Orhan,Alexis Emanuelli,Yair Lakretz,Gonzalo de Polavieja,German Sumbre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dolph2Vec，一个基于Wav2Vec2.0架构、在五年海豚录音数据上训练的首个大规模物种特异性自监督学习模型。该模型在签名哨声分类和哨声检测任务上显著优于通用基线，且其学习的嵌入和码本结构能够捕捉可解释的声学单元，为动物交流研究提供了新的科学工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物声学领域的自监督学习模型追求跨物种的广泛泛化，忽视了针对单一物种交流系统细粒度结构的深入建模。海豚具有复杂的哨声交流系统，但缺乏针对性的无监督表征学习工具来揭示其内在结构。

Method: 收集并发布包含五只已知海豚、跨越五年的纵向录音数据集（半自然海洋环境）；将Wav2Vec2.0架构适配到该领域，仅使用该数据集训练Dolph2Vec模型；在签名哨声分类和哨声检测两个生物学相关任务上进行基准测试；分析学习到的嵌入空间和码本结构，将其与海豚哨声类别及亚哨声结构进行关联解释。

Result: Dolph2Vec在两个下游任务上均显著优于通用预训练基线模型；模型学到的嵌入表示和量化码本结构能够映射到具有生物学意义的声学单元，包括哨声类别甚至亚哨声级别的结构，从而实现对海豚交流模式的细粒度分析。

Conclusion: 自监督学习不仅可以作为提升性能的建模手段，更可作为科学发现工具，用于探索和检验动物交流研究中的假说。物种特异性的SSL训练策略相较于通用模型能更好地揭示单一物种交流系统的细粒度结构。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has opened new opportunities in bioacoustics by enabling scalable modeling of animal vocalizations without the need for expensive manual annotation. However, current SSL models in this domain prioritize broad generalization across species and are not optimized for uncovering the fine-grained structure of individual communication systems. In this work, we collect and release a novel dataset of over five years of longitudinal recordings, from five known dolphins in a semi-naturalistic marine environment, an unprecedented resource for studying dolphin communication. We adapt the Wav2Vec2.0 Baevski et al. (2020) architecture to this domain and introduce Dolph2Vec, the first large-scale, species-specific SSL model trained exclusively on this data. We benchmark our model on two biologically relevant tasks: signature whistle classification and whistle detection. Dolph2Vec significantly outperforms general-purpose baselines in both tasks. Beyond performance, we show that learned embeddings and codebook structure capture interpretable acoustic units aligned with dolphin whistle categories and possibly sub-whistle structure, enabling fine-grained analysis of communication patterns. Our findings demonstrate how SSL can serve as both a model and a scientific tool to explore hypotheses in animal communication research.

</details>


### [191] [Boosting Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12505)
*Pengwei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPOP 是对 DPO 的简单扩展，通过在参考模型贪心响应上施加门控惩罚项，利用被传统 DPO 忽略的参考模型生成信号，显著提升了偏好优化的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线偏好优化方法（如 DPO 及其变体）仅使用静态数据集中的 chosen 和 rejected 响应，忽略了参考模型自身对同一 prompt 生成的响应这一有用信号。作者希望利用这一被忽视的信号来提升偏好优化性能。

Method: 提出 DPOP（Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization），在 DPO 的基础偏好损失上增加一个对参考模型贪心响应（reference-greedy response）的门控惩罚项。该惩罚仅在当前策略对 chosen 响应的似然仍低于 rejected 响应时激活。同时探索了 SimNPO 风格的长度归一化惩罚、NPO 和 token-level unlikelihood 等变体。

Result: 在 AlpacaEval 2.0 上，DPOP 在 Llama-3-8b-it 和 Gemma-2-9b-it 两个模型上均优于 DPO、SimPO 和 AlphaDPO，长度控制胜率分别相对基线提升 5.3% 和 4.4%。消融实验表明 SimNPO 风格的长度归一化惩罚强于 NPO 和 token-level unlikelihood。

Conclusion: DPOP 通过门控惩罚机制有效利用了参考模型的生成信号，是一种简洁且高效的 DPO 改进方案，在多个模型上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Offline preference optimization has become a practical substitute for reinforcement learning from human feedback, but pairwise objectives such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants use only the chosen and rejected responses stored in a static dataset. This leaves a useful signal unused: the response that the reference model itself would generate for the same prompt. We propose Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization (DPOP), a simple extension of DPO that augments the base preference loss with a gated penalty on reference-greedy responses. DPOP activates this penalty only when the current policy still assigns a lower likelihood to the preferred response than to the rejected response. On AlpacaEval 2.0, DPOP improves length-controlled win rate over DPO, SimPO, and AlphaDPO on both Llama-3-8b-it and Gemma-2-9b-it, achieving relative gains of 5.3\% and 4.4\% over baselines on the two models, respectively. Ablations further show that a SimNPO-style length-normalized penalty is stronger than NPO and token-level unlikelihood in this setting.

</details>


### [192] [Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation: Post-Training Without Rubric Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12507)
*MohammadHossein Rezaei,Anas Mahmoud,Zihao Wang,Utkarsh Tyagi,Advait Gosai,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Aakash Sabharwal,Bing Liu,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RGSD提出一种无验证器的训练方法，通过将基于评分标准的条件策略自蒸馏至无条件学生模型，以逐token稠密信号替代稀疏轨迹级奖励，在保持评分标准满足度的同时消除了LLM评判器的训练开销和偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的训练方法依赖LLM验证器对每个rollout打分，存在三大痛点：(1)训练时验证器调用带来显著计算开销；(2)优化过程暴露于验证器特有的偏差；(3)评分标准反馈被压缩为稀疏的轨迹末端信号，信息利用率低。亟需一种无需验证器、能提供稠密学习信号的替代方案。

Method: 提出Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD)：以评分标准条件化的基础策略作为教师，蒸馏至无条件的学生策略。具体地，教师模型在给定评分标准（rubric）的条件下生成响应，其token级条件分布作为监督信号，逐token指导学生模型的输出分布，从而用稠密的逐token KL散度（或交叉熵）学习信号替代稀疏的轨迹级奖励，并完全移除训练循环中的LLM评判器。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5（3B, 7B）和Qwen3-Thinking（4B, 8B）模型上，于医学和科学领域的实验表明：RGSD在评分标准满足度上与基于评判器的GRPO方法相当，且每个prompt仅需一次on-policy rollout，训练时无需任何验证器调用。消融实验揭示：(1)原始评分标准作为教师条件信号比自生成的参考答案更强；(2)更强的GRPO评判器在某些设定下可超越RGSD，表明RGSD更适合作为验证器成本或可靠性成为瓶颈时的互补方案。

Conclusion: RGSD作为一种无验证器的评分标准引导训练方法，通过自蒸馏实现稠密token级监督，有效降低训练开销并规避验证器偏差。在验证器成本高昂或可靠性不足的场景中，RGSD是GRPO等依赖评判器方法的有力互补替代方案。

Abstract: Rubrics have emerged as an alternative to RLVR in open-ended domains where a single ground-truth final answer is not available. Existing rubric-based training methods rely on an LLM verifier that scores each rollout against rubrics. This introduces substantial training-time overhead, exposes optimization to verifier-specific biases, and reduces rubric feedback to a sparse end-of-trajectory signal. We propose Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD), a verifier-free training method in which the base policy, conditioned on the rubric, serves as the teacher for the unconditioned student. RGSD distills the rubric-conditioned teacher distribution into the student token-by-token, replacing sparse trajectory-level rewards with dense per-token learning signals and removing the LLM judge from the training loop entirely. Across Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B) and Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B) models on medical and science domains, RGSD achieves rubric satisfaction comparable to judge-based GRPO while using one on-policy rollout per prompt and no training-time verifier calls. Ablations show that raw rubrics provide a stronger teacher enrichment signal than self-generated reference responses, while a stronger GRPO judge can outperform RGSD in some settings, positioning RGSD as a complementary verifier-free alternative when verifier cost or reliability is the bottleneck.

</details>


### [193] [Crossing the Validation Crisis: Cross-Validation Reduces Benchmarking Variance Surprisingly Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12552)
*Célestin Eve,Gaël Varoquaux,Thomas Moreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了交叉验证（CV）中多折划分可显著提升学习算法性能评估的置信度，提出"样本增益"概念来量化通过多次划分实现的虚拟数据增广效果，并设计了基于早期折数动态早停CV的流程，在合成与真实数据集上验证了该方法能有效提升基准测试的可靠性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习依赖实证基准测试来评估方法优劣，但评估固有的统计变异性（尤其随机算法）加上有限测试样本，常导致性能估计不可靠，形成难以辨别真实进展的"验证危机"。作者旨在系统研究如何利用交叉验证的多重划分来缓解这一问题。

Method: 1. 提出"样本增益"（sample gain）概念，形式化量化通过K折交叉验证增加划分数量所等效的虚拟样本增量，从而降低基准测试方差。2. 在合成数据及真实数据集（病理组织扫描图像分类、NLP微调）上进行实验，分析不同折数与重复次数对性能估计稳定性的影响及收益递减规律。3. 提出动态早停策略：从最初几折的估计结果外推后续折数是否能带来显著的样本增益，若增益趋于饱和则提前终止交叉验证以节省计算开销。

Result: 1. 多折交叉验证能显著提升性能估计的可靠性和稳定性。2. 收益递减（diminishing returns）的出现时间往往晚于预期，意味着增加划分数量在较多情况下仍有实用价值。3. 所提出的早停流程能有效判断后续折数是否带来大幅增益，在不过度牺牲估计质量的前提下节约计算资源。

Conclusion: 在可用样本上充分推进交叉验证（增加折数与重复次数）是实现稳健、可靠基准测试的关键。样本增益框架为理解交叉验证降低方差的内在机制提供了新视角，动态早停策略则为实际应用中的计算效率与评估精度的权衡提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Modern machine learning progresses through empirical work, benchmarking new methods to evaluate relative performance. However, the statistical variability inherent to evaluation - exacerbated by the stochastic nature of many algorithms - often makes performance estimation unreliable due to the limited test samples available, leading to a validation crisis in which genuine advances are difficult to discern. In this work, we show that cross-validation improves markedly confidence when evaluating and comparing learning algorithm performances. We introduce the concept of sample gain, which quantifies the virtual data augmentation achieved by using multiple cross-validation splits to reduce benchmarking variance. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets (histopathologic scans and NLP fine-tuning) demonstrate that multiple splits can substantially improve the reliability and stability of performance estimates, with diminishing returns often setting in later than expected. We also introduce a procedure to dynamically early-stop cross-validation by estimating from the first few folds if subsequent folds will bring large sample gains. Our findings highlight the value of pushing cross-validation on available samples to achieve robust and reliable benchmarking.

</details>


### [194] [Emerging Flexible Designs for Geospatial Multimodal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12595)
*Philipe Dias,Waqwoya Abebe,Abhishek Potnis,Aristeidis Tsaris,Dan Lu,Xiao Wang,Dalton Lunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对面向地理空间多模态推理的主流基础模型架构进行了公平对比，统一自监督预训练目标和数据集，在GEOBench基准上评估分类与分割任务，揭示了模型灵活性、模态对齐与下游性能之间的设计权衡，为下一代地理空间基础模型构建提供实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前地球观测领域的基础模型架构多样（如仅编码器、编码器-解码器、掩码自编码器等），缺乏一致的性能对比框架，难以评估不同架构在多模态地理空间推理中的设计权衡，尤其在不同光谱波段配置下的灵活性尚不清楚。

Method: 采用"苹果对苹果"的比较方法：统一使用相同的自监督学习目标和训练数据集进行预训练，在一致的参数化设置下，于GEOBench基准上对所有模型进行评测，涵盖分类和语义分割任务，重点关注不同光谱波段配置下的灵活性表现。

Result: 揭示了模型架构灵活性、模态对齐能力与下游任务性能之间的关键设计权衡，不同架构在受控条件下展现出各自的优势与局限。

Conclusion: 通过受控条件下的系统性对比，明确了各架构的强项与短板，为构建具备鲁棒多模态推理能力的下一代地理空间基础模型提供了实用的设计指导。

Abstract: Foundation models are rapidly transforming Earth observation by enabling scalable pretraining across diverse unlabeled geospatial modalities. However, their architectural diversity ranging from encoder-only to encoder-decoder and masked autoencoding paradigms makes it challenging to assess performance trade offs in a consistent manner. In this work, we present an apples-to-apples comparison of leading FM architectures designed for geospatial multimodal reasoning, with a particular focus on flexibility across varied spectral band configurations. We standardize pretraining using identical self supervised learning objectives and training datasets, and evaluate all models under consistent parameterization on the GEOBench benchmark across classification and segmentation tasks. Our results offer new insights into the design trade-offs between model flexibility, modality alignment, and downstream task performance. By highlighting architectural strengths and limitations under controlled conditions, this study provides practical guidance for building next generation geospatial foundation models capable of robust multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [195] [Viral Proteins Reveal Geometry of Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12609)
*Arthur Bigot,Harmon Bhasin,Core Francisco Park,Eugene Shakhnovich,Dianzhuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 蛋白质语言模型在嵌入空间中存在一个主导的"天然性"轴，该轴与掩码重建困惑度对齐，将细胞蛋白、病毒蛋白、随机序列有序排列。缩放模型会不均匀地收缩不同病毒家族在此轴上的分布，但病毒蛋白仍保留线性可分的特异信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型（pLMs）在高度不平衡的数据集上训练，研究其如何表征代表性不足的生物序列（如病毒蛋白）是一个重要问题。理解这一点对于评估pLM在非典型序列上的泛化能力和潜在偏差至关重要。

Method: 以病毒蛋白为案例，在ESM模型家族中进行分析。通过嵌入空间分析和掩码重建困惑度（masked reconstruction perplexity）的对齐关系，考察细胞蛋白、病毒蛋白、打乱序列和随机序列在嵌入空间中的分布；并检验模型缩放对不同病毒家族的影响，以及病毒蛋白的线性可分性。

Result: （1）嵌入空间中存在一个主导的"天然性"轴，按细胞蛋白→病毒蛋白→打乱/随机序列有序排列，且与掩码重建困惑度对齐；（2）模型缩放会不均匀地收缩不同病毒家族在此轴上的分布；（3）尽管如此，病毒蛋白嵌入保留病毒特异性信号，线性可分性超越零样本困惑度及浅层序列特征。

Conclusion: pLM表征由一种通用的"天然性"概念所结构化，同时仍能保留特定生物类群的特异信息。这意味着即使训练数据不平衡，模型仍能捕获稀有类群的关键生物学信号。

Abstract: Protein language models are trained on highly imbalanced datasets, raising the question of how they represent underrepresented biological sequences. Using viral proteins as a case study across ESM model families, we identify a dominant nativeness axis in embedding space, aligned with masked reconstruction perplexity, that orders sequences from well-modeled cellular proteins through viral proteins to shuffled and random sequences. Scaling contracts this axis unevenly across viral families. Despite this, protein language model embeddings retain viral-specific signal: viral proteins remain linearly separable beyond zero-shot perplexity and shallow sequence features. Together, these results suggest that pLM representations are structured by a general notion of nativeness while preserving information specific to distinct biological groups.

</details>


### [196] [The Mathematics of AI Winters: The mathematical Taxonomy of Paradigm Fragility in AI Winter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12610)
*Miquel Noguer i Alonso,David Pacheco Aznar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个补充性论点：第一、二次AI寒冬不仅源于工程失败与商业失望，还因主导范式遭遇了表征、优化、计算复杂性、统计可学性和高维逼近等形式化瓶颈。文章综合分析了感知机不可能性结果、神经网络训练的复杂性下界、高维非参数估计的minimax速率、梯度消失分析及统计学习理论，并将这些障碍与后来缓解而非消除它们的突破联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，两次AI寒冬被归因于工程失败、商业失望和期望膨胀。作者认为这种解释不完整，意图补充一个更深层的论点：当时的主导AI范式在形式化层面遇到了真正的数学和理论瓶颈，这些瓶颈与AI寒冬中的核心失望高度一致。

Method: 采用综合性而非档案性的研究方法，不声称特定定理机械地导致寒冬，而是通过回顾和分析若干关键理论结果来展示形式化障碍的存在：Minsky与Papert的感知机不可能性定理、Blum与Rivest的精确神经网络训练复杂性、Stone的高维非参数估计minimax速率、Hochreiter及Bengio等人的梯度消失分析，以及Vapnik-Chervonenkis、Valiant、Blumer等人的统计学习理论。

Result: 分析表明，早期AI的若干核心失望与数学上精确的瓶颈高度一致：感知机表征能力受限、精确神经网络训练的计算难解性、高维估计的统计困难，以及梯度消失问题。后续突破（如深度学习的兴起）并未消除这些障碍，而是在一定程度上缓解了它们。

Conclusion: AI寒冬不仅是社会经济现象的产物，还反映了早期范式在形式化层面遭遇的深刻理论限制。理解这些瓶颈有助于更全面地把握AI发展的历史节律，并认识到深度学习等后续突破并非消灭了这些障碍，而是通过新范式绕开或缓解了它们。

Abstract: Two major periods of reduced funding and confidence in artificial intelligence research, commonly called the first and second AI winters, are usually explained through engineering failure, commercial disappointment, and inflated expectations. This article develops a complementary thesis: that the dominant paradigms of those periods also met genuine formal barriers, including limitations of representation, optimisation, computational complexity, statistical learnability, and high-dimensional approximation. The contribution is synthetic rather than archival. We do not claim that particular theorems mechanically caused the winters; rather, we show that several central disappointments of early AI were aligned with mathematically precise bottlenecks. We analyse these bottlenecks through the perceptron impossibility results of Minsky and Papert, the complexity-theoretic hardness of exact neural-network training established by Blum and Rivest, minimax rates for nonparametric estimation in high dimension due to Stone, vanishing-gradient analyses by Hochreiter and by Bengio and collaborators, and classical statistical learning theory in the tradition of Vapnik and Chervonenkis, Valiant, and Blumer and collaborators. We then relate these barriers to the later breakthroughs that mitigated, rather than eliminated, them.

</details>


### [197] [Evaluation of AutoML Frameworks for IDS under Imbalanced Data Conditions of the NSL-KDD Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12611)
*Wiliane Carolina Silva,Evandro César Vilas Boas,Felipe A. P. de Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在NSL-KDD数据集上评估了九种AutoML框架在严重多类别不平衡下的网络入侵检测性能，发现PyCaret以66% macro-F1表现最佳，揭示了仅优化准确率不足以应对稀有攻击类别检测，并呼吁将不平衡感知机制原生集成到自动化学习管道中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多通过二分类或删除少数类来简化问题，未能真实反映AutoML框架在严重多类别不平衡场景下的表现。本文旨在填补这一空白，在保留原始五类分布（含R2L、U2R等极度稀有攻击）的条件下，系统评估AutoML框架的 imbalance-sensitive 学习行为。

Method: 使用NSL-KDD数据集的原始五类分布，在统一的、可复现的实验协议下，对九个开源AutoML框架（PyCaret、AutoGluon等）进行分析。评估维度涵盖架构设计、集成策略、验证流程、超参数优化及不平衡处理机制，以macro-F1为主要评价指标。

Result: PyCaret取得最佳整体性能（macro-F1=66%），AutoGluon以55%位居第二。集成学习与不平衡感知优化显著提升了少数类判别能力，而缺乏原生平衡支持的框架在稀有攻击检测上退化严重。高准确率指标与稀有类别泛化能力之间存在脱节。

Conclusion: 仅依赖准确率优化的AutoML策略不足以应对高度不平衡的IDS场景；需将不平衡感知优化、重采样及分层评估策略原生集成到自动化学习管道中。本文建立了该领域的标准化基准，并公开了源代码。

Abstract: This work investigates the impact of severe class imbalance on the performance of automated machine learning (AutoML) frameworks for multiclass network intrusion detection using the NSL-KDD dataset. Unlike previous studies that simplify the problem through binary classification or minority-class removal, we preserve the original five-class distribution, including highly underrepresented attacks such as R2L and U2R, enabling a realistic evaluation of imbalance-sensitive learning behavior. Nine open-source AutoML frameworks were analyzed under a unified and reproducible experimental protocol, considering differences in architectural design, ensemble strategies, validation procedures, hyperparameter optimization, and imbalance-handling mechanisms. The results demonstrate that frameworks incorporating ensemble learning and imbalance-aware optimization achieve better minority-class discrimination. PyCaret obtained the best overall performance, reaching 66\% macro-F1, followed by AutoGluon with 55\%, whereas frameworks lacking native balancing support exhibited significant degradation in minority-class detection capability. The analysis further shows that accuracy-oriented optimization alone is insufficient for highly imbalanced IDS scenarios, since high-weighted metrics may coexist with poor generalization on rare attack categories. As a contribution, this work establishes a standardized benchmark for AutoML-based intrusion detection under severe multiclass imbalance, highlighting current architectural limitations and the need for native integration of imbalance-aware optimization, resampling, and stratified evaluation strategies into automated learning pipelines. The source code is publicly available.

</details>


### [198] [Towards Provably Fair Machine Learning: Bayesian Approaches For Consistent and Transparent Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12615)
*Owen O'Neill,Fintan Costello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fair Bayesian分类器，通过强制满足确定性（相同个体得相同预测）和统计一致性（预测不得显著偏离该子组的贝叶斯最优目标分布）两个要求，在所有子组上实现一致预测；对于无法一致预测的情况则主动弃权。该方法在三个基准数据集上实现了零一致性错误，同时准确率和多校准度均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域，ML分类器在不同子组（尤其由多特征交叉定义的细粒度子组）上的预测质量差异显著，常与观测数据相矛盾。正则化会通过将小子组合并到大组中来提升整体性能，但这不成比例地伤害了人口少数群体。而小子组恰恰是频率推断最不可靠之处，因此解决这一推断问题是实现算法公平的必要步骤。

Method: 作者定义了一致预测的两个核心要求：(1) 确定性——相同个体得到相同预测；(2) 统计一致性——在显著性水平α下，不能拒绝"子组预测来自该子组贝叶斯最优目标分布"这一假设。基于这两个要求，推导出Fair Bayesian分类器：对每个子组推断贝叶斯最优目标分布，检查是否存在确定性预测与该分布统计一致；若存在则输出该预测，若不存在则弃权。该方法在所有组和子组上同时强制执行上述两个要求。

Result: 在Adult、COMPAS和Bank Marketing三个基准数据集上，标准分类器在相当大比例的子组上产生了统计不一致的预测。Fair Bayesian分类器在构造上实现了零一致性错误，同时在所有测试数据集上超越了基线准确率和多校准度（multicalibration）。

Conclusion: 统计一致性为预测质量提供了原则性基础，并对算法公平性有直接启示。人口少数不成比例地集中在小子组中，恰是频率推断最不可靠之处；因此解决这一推断问题是迈向公平ML的必要步骤。Fair Bayesian分类器证明了在数据支持的最细粒度上强制执行贝叶斯一致性，并以原则性弃权方式实现穷举子组公平在实践中是可行的。

Abstract: ML classifiers deployed in high-stakes domains produce predictions whose quality varies systematically across subgroups. For granular subgroups defined by intersections of multiple features, predictions are often inconsistent with the observed data: the model's outputs contradict the evidence available for that subgroup. This problem is exacerbated by regularisation, which improves aggregate performance by collapsing small subgroups into larger groups, disproportionately affecting demographic minorities. We define two requirements for consistent prediction: determinism (identical individuals receive identical predictions) and statistical consistency (we cannot reject, at significance level alpha, the hypothesis that the predictions for a subgroup were drawn from the Bayesian optimal target distribution inferred for that subgroup). From these requirements we derive the Fair Bayesian classifier, which enforces both across every group and subgroup simultaneously and abstains whenever no consistent deterministic prediction is possible. On three benchmark datasets (Adult, COMPAS, and Bank Marketing), standard classifiers produce statistically inconsistent predictions for a substantial proportion of subgroups. Our classifier achieves zero consistency error by construction while exceeding baseline accuracy and multicalibration on every dataset tested. Statistical consistency provides a principled foundation for prediction quality with direct implications for algorithmic fairness. Minority demographics are disproportionately concentrated in small subgroups, precisely where frequentist inference is least reliable; addressing this inference problem is therefore a necessary step toward fair ML. By enforcing Bayesian consistency at the finest resolution the data supports, the our classifier demonstrates that exhaustive subgroup fairness with principled abstention is achievable in practice.

</details>


### [199] [Bag of Dims: Training-Free Mechanistic Interpretability via Dimension-Level Sign Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12629)
*Varun Reddy Nalagatla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Transformer隐藏状态的标准基底本身即构成无需训练、架构通用的特征基底：各维度通过符号编码语义内容、通过幅值编码置信度，功能上等价于独立的二值寄存器（Bag of Dims）。实验跨Qwen、Gemma、Mistral三个模型家族验证了该框架在读取、发现、写入全流程的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前解释Transformer表征的主流方法通常依赖训练探针、稀疏自编码器或优化过程，计算代价高昂。本文试图证明标准基底本身已蕴含丰富的可解释特征，无需任何额外训练或优化即可实现特征读写，从而提供一种更简单、更通用的表征理解范式。

Method: 提出"维度袋"(Bag of Dims, BoD)框架，核心思想是将隐藏状态的每个维度视为独立的二值寄存器。具体方法包括：(1) 符号-幅值解耦实验：将幅值统一置1仅保留符号，测试下一token预测精度和纯汉明距离排序能力；(2) 单token类别缓存：对词表中每个token做一次前向传播，基于各维度符号一致性从50个锚点发现175个语义类别（零训练）；(3) 探针对比：训练线性探针并对比其与轴对齐权重的性能差异；(4) FFN写入分析：静态检查FFN权重，将特征追溯到单个写入神经元；(5) 无监督特征发现：随机种子无标签扩展到1500个特征。实验覆盖Qwen 3.5-4B、Gemma 3-4B、Mistral 7B三个模型家族。

Result: （1）仅保留符号模式、幅值置1，通过LM head可实现72-93%的top-5下一token准确率；纯汉明距离排序（无解码器）达80-90% top-4096准确率。（2）单token缓存方法发现175个语义类别，平均AUC 0.80；训练探针仅增加+0.018 AUC且权重收敛到轴对齐方向。（3）全部175个类别在K和V投影中同样可发现。（4）20%特征可通过静态FFN权重追溯到单个写入神经元（一致性>0.70），top-200神经元联盟对99.9%原型达成>0.70一致性。（5）无监督发现扩展到1500个特征，100%产出率，99%稀疏性，维度间互信息仅0.0014比特。

Conclusion: 标准基底完全足以支撑Transformer计算通路中任意位置的特征读写，整个过程无需训练、无需优化、无需GPU天级别的计算（仅需对词表做一次前向传播），为模型可解释性提供了极其简洁高效的新范式。

Abstract: We show that the standard basis of transformer hidden states already provides a training-free, architecture-general feature basis. Individual dimensions encode semantic content via their signs and confidence via their magnitudes, functioning as independent binary registers. We validate this Bag of Dims framework across three model families (Qwen 3.5-4B, Gemma 3-4B, Mistral 7B) through four progressive experiments. Sign patterns alone carry predictive content: replacing all magnitudes with unity achieves 72-93% top-5 next-token accuracy through the LM head, and pure Hamming scoring without any decoder reaches 80-90% top-4096. These sign patterns organize into semantic features: using a single-token type cache (one forward pass per vocabulary token, no context), we discover 175 categories via per-dimension sign consistency (mean AUC 0.80) from 50 anchors with zero training. A trained probe adds only +0.018 AUC and converges to axis-aligned weights, confirming negligible cross-dimension structure. This structure extends to attention: all 175 categories remain discoverable in K and V projections. On the write side, static FFN weight inspection links 20% of features to individual writer neurons (>0.70 agreement; random controls: 0%), with top-200 neuron coalitions achieving >0.70 agreement on 99.9% of prototypes via majority vote. Fully unsupervised discovery (random seeds, no labels) scales to 1500 features at 100% yield and 99% sparsity across all three models, with pairwise MI of 0.0014 bits confirming low inter-dimension coupling. These results establish that the standard basis already suffices for feature reading throughout the transformer compute pathway, requiring no training, no optimization, and no GPU-days beyond a single forward pass per vocabulary token.

</details>


### [200] [Keep Policy Gradient in Charge: Sibling-Guided Credit Distillation for Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12634)
*Tianyu Ding,Jianhong Xin,Juan Pablo De la Cruz Weinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对长周期工具使用强化学习中奖励信号稀疏的问题，提出Sibling-Guided Credit Distillation (SGCD)方法，通过对比成功与失败的兄弟轨迹来生成密集的逐步信用参考信号，从而改进token级别的优势分配，避免直接自蒸馏对工具使用能力的破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期工具使用RL中，轨迹级别的奖励需要广播到大量token上，导致信用分配极其稀疏。自蒸馏虽然可以提供更密集的信号，但会不加区分地模仿教师行为——既放大有用技能也放大有害捷径，最终"沉默地"破坏工具使用能力。因此需要一种既能提供密集信号、又能正确进行信用分配的方法。

Method: 提出SGCD（Sibling-Guided Credit Distillation），核心是将蒸馏用于信用分配而非作为竞争性的actor损失。具体流程：(1) 动态采样生成成功与失败的"兄弟"轨迹对；(2) 外部LLM对比兄弟轨迹差异，生成仅用于训练的逐步信用参考；(3) 通过教师/学生分布散度驱动信用重分配；(4) 使用有界分离式信用权重重塑GRPO的token级优势。关键设计：部署时学生模型不使用外部LLM、兄弟证据或oracle。

Result: 在AppWorld和τ³-airline两个基准上，SGCD均优于匹配的GRPO对比方法。AppWorld test_normal上TGC从42.9提升至45.6，test_challenge上从24.7提升至27.0；τ³-airline上pass@1从0.583提升至0.602。

Conclusion: 直接token级自蒸馏会破坏工具使用策略，而SGCD通过对比兄弟轨迹将蒸馏重新定位为信用分配机制，有效提供了密集的逐步监督信号，同时避免了有害捷径的放大，在两个复杂工具使用环境中取得了一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Long-horizon tool-use reinforcement learning can learn from outcome verification, but its
  trajectory-level advantage is broadcast across many reasoning, API, and answer tokens.
  Self-distillation promises a denser signal by reusing a policy's own rollouts or a privileged
  teacher. We show, however, that direct token-level self-distillation can silently destroy tool use:
  it rehearses teacher behavior without knowing which actions the verifier rewards, so useful skills
  and harmful shortcuts are amplified together. We introduce Sibling-Guided Credit Distillation
  (SGCD), which uses distillation for credit assignment rather than as a competing actor loss.
  Dynamic sampling produces mixed successful and failed sibling rollouts; an external LLM summarizes
  their contrast into a training-only stepwise credit reference; dense teacher/student divergence
  drives credit reassignment; and bounded detached credit weights reshape GRPO token advantages. The
  deployed student sees no external LLM, sibling evidence, or oracle. Across AppWorld and
  $τ^3$-airline, SGCD improves over matched GRPO comparators: AppWorld TGC $42.9 \to 45.6$ on
  test_normal and $24.7 \to 27.0$ on test_challenge, and $τ^3$-airline pass@1 $0.583 \to 0.602$.

</details>


### [201] [The Metric Picks the Winner: Evaluation Choice Flips Model Rankings for Drug-Response Prediction in Unseen Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12639)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Riya Bisht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究药物转录组响应预测，发现在THP-1细胞DRUG-seq数据上，不同评估指标会导致模型排名完全反转：在逆方差代理指标下简单线性回归胜出，但在VCPI竞赛的真实活性集wMSE指标下，融合解码器深度模型显著优于所有基线，首次在真实化合物化学空间上证实了"指标校准效应"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物转录组响应预测中，复杂深度模型在化合物化学空间外推时往往无法击败简单基线（如未处理对照、均值响应）。本文旨在探索这一现象的根源，并验证评估指标选择是否会导致模型排名的系统性反转——即"指标校准效应"是否在真实药物化学空间中成立。

Method: 采用分阶段建模策略：(1) 简单基线（未处理对照、训练集化合物均值响应）；(2) 非参数检索（基于Tanimoto加权的最近邻训练化合物平均）；(3) 融合解码器，将冻结的化学嵌入与检索支持特征结合预测残差，配备不确定性头和基因程序。在VCPI THP-1 DRUG-seq数据（14,026个训练化合物）上，按Bemis-Murcko骨架划分，分别使用逆方差代理指标和VCPI官方活性集wMSE指标进行评估。

Result: 指标选择决定胜者：(1) 在逆方差每基因代理指标下，基于Morgan指纹的正则化线性回归击败所有深度模型、检索方法和ChemBERTa，呈现"简单基线获胜"的经典结果；(2) 在VCPI真实活性集wMSE指标下结果完全反转：融合解码器显著优于线性指纹基线（-0.012 wMSE，配对bootstrap p < 10⁻⁴），代理指标下的胜者变为化学感知预测器中最差的。均值基线wMSE为0.535，与主办方参考值0.507一致。

Conclusion: 首次在真实留出药物化学空间上证明了指标校准效应的存在——评估指标的选择直接决定了哪个模型获胜。这一现象此前主要在遗传扰动领域被建立，本研究将其拓展到药物响应预测领域。作者发布了可复现的pipeline，能生成有效的VCPI竞赛提交。

Abstract: Predicting how a cell's transcriptome responds to a drug it has never seen is a core, hard problem in computational cell biology: recent benchmarks show complex models often fail to beat trivial baselines once test compounds are held out by chemistry. We study one cell line and assay, THP-1 cells profiled by DRUG-seq, scored by the active-compound weighted MSE(wMSE) of the VCPI prediction contest. We propose a staged approach: dumb baselines (untreated control and mean training-compound response) that the field keeps failing to beat; non-parametric retrieval (a Tanimoto-weighted average of a held-out compound's nearest training compounds); and a fusion stage combining a frozen chemistry embedding with retrieval-support features to predict the residual over the mean, with an uncertainty head and gene programs. On the released VCPI THP-1 drug-seq data (14,026 training compounds), under a Bemis-Murcko scaffold split, the model ranking inverts depending on the metric. Under an inverse-variance per-gene proxy, a regularized linear regression on Morgan fingerprints appears to win over the deep models, retrieval, and ChemBERTa -- the textbook "simple baselines win" result. But under the contest's true active-set metric (per-(gene, compound) Mejia weights, validated against the official scorer; mean baseline 0.535 vs the organizers' 0.507 reference), that reverses: the deep models win, our fusion decoder significantly beats the linear fingerprint baseline (-0.012 wMSE, paired bootstrap p < 10^-4), and the proxy's winner becomes the worst chemistry-aware predictor. Picking the metric picks the winner -- to our knowledge the first demonstration on real held-out drug chemistry of the metric-calibration effect established largely on genetic perturbation. We release a reproducible pipeline
  wired to the official scorer that emits a valid submission over the real 1064 x 12,995 grid.

</details>


### [202] [Individual Control Barrier Functions-Guided Diffusion Model for Safe Offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12640)
*Qingyun Guo,Junyi Shi,Jianuo Huang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全离线多智能体强化学习算法，将神经个体控制屏障函数嵌入扩散模型中，在轨迹生成过程中增强安全性，并通过逆动力学恢复控制策略。实验表明该算法在保持竞争性奖励的同时显著提升了安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的离线强化学习方法主要关注单智能体场景，多智能体环境中的安全性挑战尚未被充分探索。离线多智能体强化学习需要在不与环境在线交互的前提下，同时处理多智能体协调和安全性约束，这构成了核心研究动机。

Method: 提出一种安全离线多智能体强化学习算法：(1) 将神经个体控制屏障函数（Neural Individual Control Barrier Functions）嵌入扩散模型，在扩散模型的去噪/生成过程中引入安全约束；(2) 通过逆动力学模型从生成的轨迹中恢复控制策略；(3) 最终实现在离线数据驱动下生成满足安全性要求的多智能体轨迹与策略。

Result: 在多个基准测试上进行了评估，实验结果表明：所提算法在保持与竞争方法相当的奖励水平的同时，实现了显著的安全性提升。

Conclusion: 将控制屏障函数与扩散模型结合是解决安全离线多智能体强化学习的有效途径，能够在保证策略性能的前提下大幅提升安全性，为安全关键场景中的多智能体离线学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning allows control policies to be learned directly from data without online interaction, making it suitable for safety-critical tasks. Recent studies have applied diffusion models to offline reinforcement learning to leverage their strong capacity for modeling complex data distributions. However, existing approaches primarily focus on single-agent settings, leaving the safety challenges in multi-agent environments largely unexplored. In this work, we propose a safe offline multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithm that embeds neural individual control barrier functions into the diffusion model to enhance safety during trajectory generation, with control policies recovered through inverse dynamics. We evaluate our algorithm across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating substantial safety improvements while maintaining competitive rewards.

</details>


### [203] [TEDD: Robust Detection of Unstable Temporal Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12643)
*Ricardo Ribeiro Pereira,Bruno Casal Laraña,Nádia Soares,Miguel Araújo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TEDD方法，利用回归模型预测实例时间戳，通过分析各特征对时间预测的贡献度，自动检测数据集中随时间变化的特征，从而识别导致机器学习模型不稳定的漂移特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时序数据中，特征分布常随时间变化，导致机器学习模型性能快速退化。亟需一种能自动识别哪些特征正在发生时间漂移的方法，以便从业者及时采取数据变换等缓解措施。

Method: 提出TEDD（Temporal Drift Detection），核心思想是训练一个回归模型来预测每条实例的时间戳，然后分析各特征对该预测任务的贡献度——对时间预测贡献大的特征即为发生时间漂移的特征。该方法可检测数值型和类别型特征，能识别单变量和多变量漂移。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，TEDD能够检测所有基本类型的时序变化模式（包括数值型和类别型特征），可检测多变量漂移，能输出各特征变化量的可比较度量值，无需参数调优，且在特征数量和实例数量上均具有可扩展性。

Conclusion: TEDD是一种有效的时序漂移特征检测方法，相比其他方法覆盖更全面的变化模式，兼具易用性和可扩展性，能帮助数据科学家构建更鲁棒的时序机器学习模型。

Abstract: When working with real-world temporal data, it is common to encounter features whose distribution is changing over time. The naive employment of Machine Learning models on this unstable data might lead to rapidly degrading performance, especially if the new distribution is much different from what was previously seen during training. In order to cope with this problem, it is critical to automatically identify features that are changing over time. With these features detected, data scientists and other practitioners will be able to mitigate the issue (for instance, by applying data transformations), deploying more robust models that retain high performance for longer periods of time. In this paper, we describe which temporal changes a feature should not suffer from, and propose TEDD, a technique to a) identify when a dataset might lead to an unstable Machine Learning model and b) automatically detect which features cause such lack of robustness. In order to achieve it, we leverage a regression model to highlight which features contribute to a good prediction of an instance's timestamp. We compare our approach to other methods in real and synthetic data, testing their detection capability on all simple change patterns. We show that our method: detects all types of basic changes, both for numerical and categorical features; can detect multivariate drifts; returns a comparable value measuring the amount of change of each feature; requires no parameter tuning; and is scalable both on number of features and instances of the dataset.

</details>


### [204] [Physics-Aware Auxiliary Losses Improve Out-of-Distribution Generalization of a GNN Synthesizability Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12651)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了在药物发现中，将廉价物理先验（拓扑复杂度和应变能）作为图神经网络辅助监督信号，能否提升分子合成性过滤器在分布外（OOD）场景下的泛化能力。实验表明，加入物理先验的变体在OOD测试上带来虽小但统计显著的提升，且多种子评估对得出可靠结论至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子合成性过滤器（SAScore、SCScore、RAscore、DeepSA）均为纯统计模型，在生成模型产生远离训练分布的分子时性能退化严重。作者希望探索引入廉价的闭式物理先验作为辅助监督，是否能改善GNN在OOD场景下的泛化能力。

Method: 以GINE为骨干网络，添加两个辅助损失：(1) 用Bertz指数监督的拓扑复杂度回归损失；(2) 用MMFF94力场能量监督的应变能软惩罚损失。在65,177个分子（来自HIV、Tox21、COCONUT数据集）上以SAScore阈值标记。实验采用四路消融（基线/+复杂度/+应变/+两者），在单源OOD切分（HIV+Tox21训练，COCONUT天然产物测试）上评估，重复5个随机种子，使用配对bootstrap置信区间。

Result: 三种物理感知变体均比基线（平均OOD AUC 0.9774）有统计显著提升：+复杂度变体Δ=+0.0060 (95% CI [+0.0023, +0.0102])，+应变变体Δ=+0.0032 ([+0.0008, +0.0052])，+两者组合Δ=+0.0066 ([+0.0038, +0.0093])，组合效果最佳。分布内各变体无显著差异。单一随机种子版本产生了定性不同（非单调）的结果，未能经受多种子评估的验证。

Conclusion: 廉价物理先验作为辅助监督可带来虽小但稳健的OOD泛化改进。物理先验的组合效果优于单独使用。研究同时警示：OOD评估中单一随机种子的结果可能产生误导，多种子重复实验对于获得可靠结论至关重要。

Abstract: Machine-learning drug-discovery pipelines increasingly rely on generative models that propose molecules far from the data used to train downstream synthesizability filters. Existing filters (SAScore, SCScore, RAscore, DeepSA) are purely statistical and degrade in exactly this out-of-distribution (OOD) regime. We ask whether cheap, closed-form physical priors, used as auxiliary supervision on a graph neural network (GNN), improve OOD generalization. We add two auxiliary losses to a GINE backbone: a topological complexity regression supervised by the Bertz index, and a strain-energy soft penalty supervised by MMFF94 force-field energy. On a 65,177-molecule corpus (HIV, Tox21, COCONUT) labeled by SAScore thresholds we reproduce a strong in-distribution baseline, then evaluate a 4-way ablation (baseline / +complexity / +strain / +both) on a single-source OOD split (train on drug-like HIV+Tox21, test on COCONUT natural products), repeated over 5 seeds with paired bootstrap confidence intervals. All three physics-aware variants give a small but statistically significant OOD improvement over the baseline (mean OOD AUC 0.9774): +complexity Delta = +0.0060 (95% CI [+0.0023, +0.0102]), +strain Delta = +0.0032 ([+0.0008, +0.0052]), +both Delta = +0.0066 ([+0.0038, +0.0093]); every interval excludes zero, and the combination is best. The variants are indistinguishable in-distribution, so the effect is visible only under OOD evaluation. We are explicit that the effects are modest, and we report a cautionary methodological finding: a single-seed version of this experiment produced a qualitatively different (non-monotone) story that did not survive multi-seed evaluation.

</details>


### [205] [A Zero-shot Generalized Graph Anomaly Detection Framework via Node Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12673)
*Phan Nguyen,Dat Cao,Hien Chu,Khue Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AlignGAD，一个面向跨域图异常检测的零样本泛化框架，通过全局统一模块、聚类模块和节点差异评分模块实现跨域异常节点识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法高度依赖特定数据集的特征语义和结构模式，难以跨域泛化；现实应用中异构数据普遍存在，急需能零样本迁移的通用检测框架。

Method: AlignGAD由三部分组成：(1) 全局统一模块，对齐异构节点特征并在谱域归一化图信号；(2) 聚类模块，构建聚类感知的图视图以捕获群组级异常模式；(3) 节点差异评分模块，衡量重建差异并聚合来自不同图视图的异常证据。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，AlignGAD在零样本图异常检测设定下取得了有效的检测性能。

Conclusion: AlignGAD通过特征对齐、聚类感知视图和差异评分聚合，成功实现了跨域零样本图异常检测，为异构场景下的GAD提供了可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain graph anomaly detection (GAD) aims to identify abnormal nodes in unseen target graphs, showing strong potential in real-world applications with heterogeneous graph data. However, existing methods often depend on dataset-specific feature semantics and structural patterns, which limits their ability to generalize across different domains. To address this challenge, we propose AlignGAD, a zero-shot generalized graph anomaly detection framework. Our framework is built upon three key components: a Global Unification Module that aligns heterogeneous node features and normalizes graph signals in the spectral domain; a Clustering Module that constructs cluster-aware graph views to capture group-level abnormal patterns; and a Node Discrepancy Scoring Module that measures reconstruction discrepancy and aggregates anomaly evidence from different graph views. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of AlignGAD under the zero-shot GAD setting.

</details>


### [206] [Fed-FBD: Federated Functional Block Diversification for Isolation, Privacy, and Surgical Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12679)
*Weijie Chen,Alan B. McMillan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fed-FBD 提出一种模块化联邦学习架构，将 ResNet 分解为六个功能块并维护多色变体仓库，实现块级隔离、隐私保护和亚秒级机器遗忘，在适度精度损失下获得 FedAvg 所不具备的三项安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦学习（如 FedAvg）将每个客户端视为黑盒，缺乏三项关键能力：无法隔离对抗性参与者、无法审计单客户端影响、无法满足"被遗忘权"要求。这些问题在医疗等隐私敏感场景中尤为突出。

Method: 将 ResNet 骨干分解为六个功能块（stem、四个残差组、分类头），维护一个包含 N 个颜色变体的仓库，每个颜色变体由独立追踪且带有贡献者标记的块组装而成。通过架构层面的块级隔离实现安全保证，无需额外隐私机制即可使成员推理攻击优势降至随机猜测水平，并支持对退出参与者的贡献进行手术式机器遗忘，无需重新训练。

Result: 在六个 MedMNIST-2D 数据集、224×224 PathMNIST 和 CIFAR-10 上的实验表明：在充分大小的数据集上，IID 精度损失仅为 0.3%–3.1%；在 Dirichlet α=1.0 的非同分布设置下，四个数据集中的三个与 FedAvg 的差距保持在 0.8%–4.0%；六种对抗攻击均被限制在投毒客户端自身的块内，干净颜色变体的 AUC 漂移不超过 ±0.01。

Conclusion: Fed-FBD 以可接受的精度代价，在联邦学习中首次同时实现架构级别的对抗隔离、原生隐私保护和极低成本的机器遗忘，为医疗等高风险领域的联邦学习部署提供了更可靠的安全基础。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw patient data, but standard approaches such as FedAvg treat each client as a black box and provide no mechanism for isolating an adversarial contributor, auditing per-client influence, or honoring a departed participant's right to be forgotten. We present Fed-FBD (Federated Functional Block Diversification), a modular federated architecture that decomposes a ResNet backbone into six functional blocks (the stem, four residual groups, and the classification head) and maintains a warehouse of N color variants, each assembled from independently tracked and contributor-stamped blocks. Fed-FBD provides three capabilities absent in FedAvg: (i) architecturally guaranteed block-level isolation, so that an adversarial or mislabelled client cannot contaminate the clean colous; (ii) privacy-by-design, where membership inference advantage is already indistinguishable from chance before any privacy mechanism is applied; and (iii) surgical machine unlearning of a departed participant's contribution at sub-second cost and without retraining. Experiments on six MedMNIST-2D datasets, PathMNIST at 224x224, and CIFAR-10 show that Fed-FBD trades a modest 0.3%-3.1% IID accuracy gap on the adequately sized datasets for these guarantees, remains within 0.8%-4.0% of FedAvg at Dirichlet alpha=1.0 on three of four datasets, and confines all six adversarial attacks we study to the poisoned client's own blocks with at most +/-0.01 AUC drift on the clean colors.

</details>


### [207] [Forecasting Is Not Attribution: Localizing Decoder Bypass in Graph-Based Neural Marketing Mix Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12687)
*Yunbo Wang,Bolbi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示基于图的神经营销组合模型（MMM）中存在"归因绕过"（attribution bypass）现象：高容量解码器可通过目标自回归、共动等捷径实现低预测误差，却未能将反事实敏感性真正路由通过用于归因的图结构。作者提出 DICE-MMM 框架，将图恢复、预测精度、归因对齐三者解耦，并通过实验证明低 MSE 并不能保证归因质量，真正的瓶颈在图支持选择而非预测或解码器容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销组合模型同时服务于预测与归因两个目标，但二者并不等价。现有基于图的神经 MMM 存在关键失败模式：高容量解码器可通过多种捷径获得低预测误差，使得低 MSE 被误认为归因可信的证据（即"归因绕过"），需要诊断与定位该瓶颈。

Method: 提出 DICE-MMM（三阶段诊断与训练框架）：Stage 1 使用受限的图介导解码器训练图编码器；Stage 2 冻结编码器，训练图安全潜在解码器（跨节点通信必须经过给定的图）；通过 CIG、AR-CIG 和图交换测试评估解码器使用图的程度。实验涵盖受控的 R/d/T 交换和外部多图 rawlog 压力测试。

Result: 在稀疏目标基准中，无图和全图解码器 MSE@7 ≈ 0.004，但 AR-CIG nAUPRC 接近或低于零；而 oracle 图在相当 MSE 下 nAUPRC 达 0.807±0.129。冻结图交换实验显示：同一 DICE-hard-trained 解码器在学习图输入下 nAUPRC 为 -0.044±0.006，切换为 oracle 图后升至 0.894±0.027，精准定位瓶颈在图支持选择。

Conclusion: 低 MSE 可能掩盖归因绕过，预测精度不能作为归因质量的认证。当前核心瓶颈是图支持选择，而非预测能力或解码器容量。DICE-MMM 为神经 MMM 提供了有效的压力测试与故障定位框架。

Abstract: Marketing mix models are used to forecast business outcomes and to attribute those outcomes to marketing channels, but these goals are not equivalent. We study a failure mode in graph-based neural MMM called attribution bypass: a high-capacity decoder can obtain low forecasting error through target autoregression, dense communication, co-movement, context, or latent memory while failing to route counterfactual sensitivity through the graph used as the attribution object. We introduce DICE-MMM as a bounded diagnostic and training framework. We do not claim that observational neural MMM identifies causal effects. Instead, DICE separates three questions often conflated in graph-based MMM: graph recovery, forecasting accuracy, and whether the trained decoder's perturbation-induced influence is graph aligned. Stage 1 trains a graph encoder with a restricted graph-mediated decoder. Stage 2 freezes the selected encoder and trains a graph-safe latent decoder whose cross-node communication must pass through the supplied graph. Decoder use is evaluated with CIG, AR-CIG, and graph-swap tests. Across controlled R/d/T swaps and an external multi-graph rawlog stress test, DICE improves stable graph recovery over CausalMMM. The experiments show that forecasting accuracy is not an attribution certificate: in a sparse-target benchmark, no-graph and full-graph decoders achieve MSE@7 around 0.004 while AR-CIG nAUPRC remains near or below zero, whereas an oracle graph reaches 0.807 +/- 0.129 at comparable MSE. Frozen graph-swap localizes the bottleneck: the same DICE-hard-trained decoder moves from nAUPRC -0.044 +/- 0.006 under learned graph inputs to 0.894 +/- 0.027 with the oracle graph. The contribution is a stress test and failure-localization framework showing that low MSE can hide attribution bypass and that the unresolved bottleneck is graph-support selection, not forecasting or decoder capacity.

</details>


### [208] [M*: A Modular, Extensible, Serving System for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12688)
*Atindra Jha,Naomi Sagan,Keisuke Kamahori,Irmak Sivgin,Rohan Sanda,Steven Gao,Mark Horowitz,Luke Zettlemoyer,Olivia Hsu,Jure Leskovec,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M* 是一个面向复合AI模型的通用推理服务系统，通过"Walk Graph"抽象将任意模型表示为数据流图，实现跨模态、跨任务的高效分布式推理，在文生图、文生语音和机器人规划等任务上分别比vLLM-Omni和专项基线取得显著延迟与吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型服务框架基于单一结构假设构建，难以适配由视觉编码器、语言骨干、扩散头、音频编解码器等多组件复合而成的新一代多模态模型（统一多模态模型、全模态模型、语音-语言模型、VLA策略、世界模型等），亟需一个通用、可组合的推理系统。

Method: 提出 Walk Graph 抽象——将复合模型表示为模块化数据流图，支持组件的任意组合、跨物理集群的灵活放置，以及在分布式运行时中实施与模型无关的全局优化。请求处理被建模为在图上的遍历过程。

Result: 在BAGEL文生图任务上，端到端延迟比vLLM-Omni平均降低20%；在Qwen3-Omni文生语音任务上，实时因子最高降低2.9倍，吞吐提升2.7倍；在机器人规划的V-JEPA 2-AC rollout任务上，性能提升最高达12.5倍。

Conclusion: M* 以极低的开发者接入成本，实现了复合模型的高效推理服务，为新一代多组件多模态模型的规模化部署铺平了道路。
