Abstract: We are entering a new era of composite model architectures that integrate diverse components such as vision encoders, language backbones, diffusion and flow heads, audio codecs, action generators, and world-model predictors. Such architectures underpin a broad class of multimodal models, including unified multimodal models, omni models, speech-language models, vision-language-action policies, and world models. However, existing model serving frameworks were built on narrow assumptions about model structure, making them ill-suited to accommodate this new architectural diversity. Here we present M*, a universal serving system for efficient serving of composite AI models. M* represents models as dataflow graphs, processing requests spanning diverse modalities and tasks as traversals over these graphs. The core insight is a modular abstraction that supports arbitrary composition of model components, flexible placement onto a physical cluster, and model-agnostic optimizations within a distributed runtime. We call this abstraction the Walk Graph and show how it can concisely capture composite models from a broad range of families. We instantiate M* on representative models and find that it achieves, on average, 20% lower end-to-end latency than vLLM-Omni for text-to-image workloads on BAGEL, while delivering up to 2.9x lower real-time factor and 2.7x higher throughput for text-to-speech workloads on Qwen3-Omni. M* also outperforms the V-JEPA 2-AC rollout baseline for robotic planning by up to 12.5x. Thus, our work paves the road towards more efficient serving of complex models with minimal developer effort.

</details>


### [209] [LLM-Powered Personalized Glycemic Assessment in Type 2 Diabetes with Wearable Sensor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12699)
*Yifan Gao,Yanmin Gong,Yun Shi,Yuanxiong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GlyLLM，一个基于大语言模型的框架，通过融合可穿戴传感器数据与结构化元数据来建模血糖动态，在血糖预测和糖尿病分类任务上显著优于传统机器学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统血糖评估方法依赖传统机器学习，主要使用历史血糖测量值，忽视个体层面的个性化信息（如人口统计、问卷、体检数据等），在不同糖尿病人群中泛化性能受限。LLM 能整合多模态数据并建模时序依赖，为个性化血糖评估提供了新可能。

Method: 提出 GlyLLM 框架，利用预训练 LLM 的广泛先验知识，将可穿戴传感器数据（CGM、健身追踪器）与结构化元数据进行融合，在决策时实现传感器-文本语义抽象。在 AI-READI 数据集上评估。

Result: 在血糖预测任务上 RMSE 平均提升 13.66%，在糖尿病分类任务上 AUROC 平均提升 13.08%。消融研究进一步表明，糖尿病问卷与生物测量检验对血糖评估比其他健康信息更为关键。

Conclusion: GlyLLM 展示了利用 LLM 推进 2 型糖尿病个性化血糖评估的潜力，为融合多源个性化数据的糖尿病管理提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Type 2 Diabetes (T2D) poses an increasing global health threat, demanding effective glycemic assessment to support personalized and improved diabetes care. Wearable sensors such as continuous glucose monitors (CGM) and fitness trackers offer many valuable insights for glycemic assessment. However, effectively analyzing these data requires integration with essential individual-level context. Existing methods are often based on traditional machine learning (ML) and rely primarily on historical blood glucose measurements and overlook personalized information, which limits their performance across diverse diabetes populations. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated their ability to integrate diverse data modalities while modeling sequential dependencies, motivating the exploration of their potential for personalized glycemic assessment.
  In this paper, we propose GlyLLM, an LLM-powered framework for modeling CGM-based glycemic dynamics through the integration of wearable sensor data and structured metadata. GlyLLM can leverage the extensive prior knowledge of pre-trained LLMs and achieve sensor-text semantic abstraction at decision time. Experiments on two related tasks on the AI-READI dataset demonstrate that our model outperforms traditional ML methods by an average of 13.66\% in Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) for glucose forecasting and 13.08\% in Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) for diabetes categorization. Additionally, our ablation study shows that diabetes surveys and biometric tests are more critical than other health information for glycemic assessment. Our work presents a promising step toward harnessing the power of LLMs to advance personalized glycemic assessment in T2D care.

</details>


### [210] [A Stabilized Path-Space Approach to Diffusion-Based Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12710)
*Evan Scope Crafts,Umberto Villa,Saviz Mowlavi,Yanting Ma,Hassan Mansour,Wael H. Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稳定化路径空间的扩散后验采样框架，通过时间重参数化和信赖域优化学习最优控制，解决了现有扩散后验采样器在非线性算子与多模态后验下失效的问题，并提供了重要性采样校正以实现渐近精确的后验期望估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散后验采样器依赖启发式引导近似，面对非线性正演算子和多模态后验时容易失效，缺乏严格的贝叶斯不确定性量化保障。

Method: 以基础扩散过程的终端边缘表示先验，在轨迹上定义似然加权的目标测度，将后验采样转化为学习受控随机过程使路径测度匹配目标；引入时间重参数化消除未知初值函数偏置；通过信赖域路径空间优化与对数方差目标学习控制；利用路径空间视角统一现有引导采样器、量化近似控制误差，并导出重要性采样校正。

Result: 在具备解析特征或高质量参考后验的基准逆问题套件上，该方法在采样精度和不确定性量化方面相比主流方法展现出更好的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 路径空间控制框架为扩散后验采样提供了更严格的理论基础，实现了对非线性、多模态后验的稳健采样，并通过重要性采样保证了渐近精确的后验推断。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide expressive data-driven priors for Bayesian inverse problems, but many diffusion posterior samplers rely on heuristic guidance approximations that can fail for nonlinear operators and multimodal posteriors. In this work, we develop a stabilized path-space framework for diffusion-based posterior sampling. Starting from a base diffusion process whose terminal marginal represents the prior, we define a likelihood-weighted target measure on trajectories and cast posterior sampling as learning a controlled stochastic process whose path measure matches this target. This formulation connects diffusion posterior sampling to stochastic optimal control while preserving the Bayesian structure needed for uncertainty quantification. We introduce a time reparameterization that makes the path-space control problem well posed by removing the bias induced by the unknown initial value function, without auxiliary training. We then learn the control via a trust-region path-space optimization method with log-variance objectives. The path-space perspective also unifies our learned control approach with existing guidance-based samplers, quantifies the sampling error induced by approximate controls, and yields importance sampling corrections for asymptotically exact posterior expectations. We evaluate the proposed framework on a suite of benchmark inverse problems with analytically characterized or high-quality reference posteriors, enabling principled assessment of sampling accuracy and uncertainty quantification. These experiments provide insight into the behavior of diffusion-based posterior samplers and demonstrate improved accuracy and robustness over leading approaches.

</details>


### [211] [Out-of-Distribution (OOD) Detectors for Open-Set RF Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12718)
*Sudeepta Mondal,Ganesh Sundaramoorthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对射频指纹识别中的开集问题，将一系列无需外部OOD调优数据的离群检测方法引入RFF领域，并基于信息论建立统一数学框架进行分析。在POWDER数据集上的实验表明，这些无OOD调优的检测器性能与使用真实OOD数据的基线相当，且远超不使用OOD数据的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频指纹识别系统需应对开集环境中未知发射机信号和时域漂移带来的分布偏移。OOD检测为此提供了天然框架，但现有方法大多依赖辅助OOD数据进行参数调优，而实际RF环境中代表性OOD数据难以收集，这构成了应用OOD检测的关键障碍。

Method: 作者将机器学习文献中一类具有前景的OOD检测方法引入开集RFF，并基于信息论（通信系统的自然数学框架）建立统一数学框架，用于系统化分析现有方法和开发新方法。核心亮点是采用无需给定OOD调优数据的检测器调优技术。

Result: 在POWDER射频指纹数据集上评估显示：无OOD调优数据的检测器性能与能获取真实OOD调优数据的基线相当，且显著优于无法获取真实OOD调优数据的基线方法。

Conclusion: 无需OOD调优数据的检测方法在开集射频指纹识别中具有实际可行性，有效解决了RF环境中OOD数据难以收集的现实困境，为RFF领域的OOD检测应用扫除了关键障碍。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) fingerprinting systems must operate in open-world environments where signals from unknown transmitters and temporal drift introduce distribution shift at test time. Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection provides a natural framework for this problem, yet its application to RF fingerprinting (RFF) remains limited. A key barrier to their adoption is that most OOD detectors require auxiliary OOD data for parameter tuning, an assumption that is difficult to satisfy in RF environments where representative OOD data is impractical to collect. In this work, we introduce a promising set of OOD detection methods from the machine learning literature to open-set RFF domain. We present these methods within a unified mathematical framework based on information theory, which is a natural framework for communication systems. Our framework allows for the systematic analysis of methods and development of new methods. We further demonstrate the applicability of recent work on tuning OOD detectors without given OOD tuning data for open-set RFF. We evaluate on the POWDER RF fingerprinting dataset, showing that detectors tuned without any given OOD data achieve performance comparable to baselines with access to true OOD tuning data and greatly out-perform baseline approaches without access to true OOD tuning data, showcasing the practical viability for the RFF problem.

</details>


### [212] [Normative Robustness as a Frontier for Non-Verifiable Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12731)
*Elizaveta Tennant,Benjamin Henke,Anita Keshmirian,Murray Shanahan,Verena Rieser,Kristian Lum,Sydney Levine,Julia Haas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“道德鲁棒性”概念，通过对抗性多轮评估框架测试四个前沿LLM在48,000次道德 deliberation 中的表现，发现模型存在“道德审议谄媚”现象——会向用户道德观点偏移（平均6.5%），且受前提顺序（13-22%案例受影响）和对话轮次（10-24%案例受影响）显著干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM推理评估集中于数学、科学等事实领域，忽视了模型在缺乏客观真值、模糊主观的价值负载问题上的表现。随着LLM越来越多承担咨询和审议角色，亟需评估其在非可验证推理领域的可靠性。

Method: 以道德推理为非可验证推理的典型子领域，定义“道德鲁棒性”概念；构建可扩展的对抗性多轮评估框架，操纵四个变量——前提相关性、前提顺序、对话时长、用户道德立场；对四个前沿LLM进行48,000次模拟道德 deliberation 实验。

Result: 模型能成功忽略道德无关的干扰项，但会向用户声明的道德观点平均偏移6.5%；前提顺序在13-22%的案例中改变道德判断；单轮与多轮对话之间在10-24%的案例中改变道德判断。模型不仅调整最终结论，连底层论证理由也会向用户立场对齐。

Conclusion: 作者将这一失败模式定义为“道德审议谄媚”（moral deliberative sycophancy），表明当前LLM在主观、价值负载的审议场景中缺乏稳健性，呼吁对非可验证推理领域的评估予以更多关注。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly serve in advisory and deliberative roles, users rely on them for non-verifiable reasoning in domains lacking objective ground truths. However, traditional evaluations of LLM reasoning focus almost exclusively on fact-based domains, such as mathematics and science, leaving uncertainty over whether and to what degree models can handle ambiguous, subjective, or value-laden problems over time. To address this concern, we propose moral reasoning as a paradigmatic subdomain of non-verifiable reasoning. We define moral robustness as a model's capacity to exhibit sound moral reasoning across time and contexts, and we introduce a scalable, adversarial, multi-turn evaluation framework to empirically measure this capability. We simulate 48,000 user-agent moral deliberations across four frontier LLMs, varying premise relevance, premise order, conversation duration, and the user's stated moral view. We find that models successfully ignore morally-irrelevant distractors, but shift their reasoning by up to 6.5%, on average, towards the user's stated preferred moral view, and varying their reasoning depending on factors such as order (altering moral judgments by order in 13-22% of the cases) and duration (altering moral judgments between single-turn and multi-turn in 10-24% of the cases). Our analysis indicates that models tailor not just their final verdicts but their underlying justifications to align with a user's moral viewpoint - a failure mode we characterize as moral deliberative sycophancy.

</details>


### [213] [Let's Ask Gauss: Improved One-Run Privacy Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12733)
*Adya Agrawal,Yu Wei,Jaspal Singh,Malik Magdon-Ismail,Vassilis Zikas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布视角的差分隐私审计框架，利用DP-SGD中金丝雀对齐信号序列的渐近高斯性，通过单次训练运行获得更紧的隐私下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次运行的DP审计方法通过将训练样本（金丝雀）二值化为成员资格猜测，丢弃了大量有用信息。作者旨在利用完整的信号分布信息，从单次训练中获得更紧的隐私下界估计。

Method: 在白盒DP-SGD设置下，作者首先证明金丝雀对齐信号自然形成一个随机变量序列，其归一化和渐近服从高斯分布。基于此分布视角，提出了一种新的DP审计框架，利用该渐近分布特性来计算更紧的隐私下界。

Result: 该方法在单次训练运行的条件下，能够获得比现有二值化方法更紧的差分隐私下界，从而更准确地评估模型实际泄露的信息量。

Conclusion: 通过将DP审计从二值猜测框架转向分布估计框架，可以充分利用信号中的统计信息，在保持单次运行效率的同时显著提升审计紧度。

Abstract: Privacy auditing provides an important safeguard by estimating the actual information leaked by a model, thus ensuring that theoretical privacy guarantees hold in practice. We study empirical privacy auditing for differentially private (DP) machine learning, focusing on efficient one-run methods for mechanisms such as DP-SGD. Prior one-run approaches threshold training examples or "canaries" into binary membership guesses, which discards useful information. We show that, in the white-box DP-SGD setting, canary-aligned signals naturally form a sequence of random variables whose normalized sum is asymptotically Gaussian. Leveraging this distributional perspective, we develop a DP-auditing framework that leads to tighter privacy lower bounds from a single training run.

</details>


### [214] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Radial Basis Functions for PDEs with Dirac Delta Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12735)
*Manuel Reyna,Alexandre Tartakovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用残差最小二乘法（RLS）视角，实现了物理信息神经网络（PINN）对含Dirac delta函数偏微分方程的直接处理，并通过神经正切核（NTK）理论解释了为何RBF-RLS在输运问题中优于PINN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在处理含Dirac delta函数（如点源、边界或初始条件）的PDE时，需要用光滑替代函数逼近delta函数，这会引入显著的建模误差。作者希望找到一种能直接处理Dirac delta项的方法，避免替代函数带来的误差。

Method: 作者将PINN重新解释为残差最小二乘法（RLS），利用弱形式方程直接积分消除Dirac delta项。重点对比了两种RLS方法：（1）标准PINN；（2）基于径向基函数（RBF）展开的单层RBF网络。通过神经正切核（NTK）理论分析两者差异，并在地下水流、多孔介质和河流溶质输运等线性PDE上进行正向和逆向问题测试，包括合成数据、含噪合成数据和真实测量数据。

Result: （1）PINN中积分消除Dirac delta后，残差无法收敛到零；（2）RBF-RLS在输运问题的正向和逆向求解中均表现良好且稳定；（3）NTK理论解释了上述性能差异。

Conclusion: RBF-RLS方法能够直接且有效地处理含Dirac delta函数的PDE，相比标准PINN具有显著优势，适用于地下水与河流输运建模中的正向和逆向问题。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are a machine learning method for solving forward and inverse Partial Differential Equations (PDEs). When applied to PDEs with Dirac delta functions in the forcing terms, boundary conditions, or initial conditions, PINNs require approximating them with smooth surrogate functions, a practice that can introduce significant modeling errors. In this work, we exploit the interpretation of PINNs as Residual Least Squares (RLS) methods and show that this perspective enables direct treatment of Dirac delta terms by integrating the weak-form equation. Among RLS formulations other than PINN, we focus on the Radial Basis Function (RBF) expansion (also known as a single-layer RBF Network). We show that while integrating out the Dirac delta in PINNs causes residuals to fail to converge to zero, RBF-RLS consistently provides good forward and inverse solutions to transport problems. We explain this finding using the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) theory. We test both approaches on linear PDEs that represent groundwater flow and transport in porous media and rivers. We solve inverse problems to fit synthetic data, noisy synthetic data, and real-world measurements.

</details>


### [215] [Deep Unfolded Latent Optimally Partitioned-l2/l1 Networks for Data-driven Block-Sparse Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12740)
*Takanobu Furuhashi,Hidekata Hontani,Qibin Zhao,Tatsuya Yokota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于深度展开（DU）的LOP-l2/l1架构，解决原方法需手动调参和近端算子数值不稳定的问题，实验表明其具备强鲁棒性，尤其对脉冲噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸LOP-l2/l1方法虽能在未知分区下实现块稀疏信号恢复，但依赖手动超参数调优；其近端算子的微分存在数值不稳定性，导致无法通过深度展开（DU）实现自动参数学习。

Method: 提出两种DU架构：(1) 基于隐式微分的稳定框架；(2) 基于深度权重分解（DWF）的灵活变体，后者还支持非凸光滑数据保真项。

Result: 数值实验表明，DU-LOP-l2/l1具有竞争性的恢复性能，且对脉冲噪声展现出高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过隐式微分和DWF两种策略，成功将LOP-l2/l1与深度展开结合，消除了手动调参瓶颈，并扩展了对非凸数据项的支持，在脉冲噪声场景下表现突出。

Abstract: The convex Latent Optimal Partition (LOP)-l2/l1 approach enables block-sparse signal recovery with unknown partitions but relies on manual hyperparameter tuning. Additionally, numerical instability in differentiating its proximal operator prevents its automatic parameter tuning via Deep Unfolding (DU). To address these limitations, we propose two architectures: a stable framework utilizing implicit differentiation and a flexible variant leveraging Deep Weight Factorization (DWF). The DWF-based approach also supports nonconvex smooth data fidelity terms. Numerical experiments demonstrate that DU-LOP-l2/l1 yields competitive performance and high resilience against impulsive noise.

</details>


### [216] [Adaptive Weighted Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12763)
*Aditya Bhaskara,Ashok Cutkosky,Ravi Kumar,Manish Purohit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在仅获得每个未知值 $x_i$ 的单次无偏估计 $y_i$ 的条件下，如何选择 $n$ 个未知值中的最大值。提出了一类同时满足可容许性（不被任何其他策略一致占优）且不劣于给定基线（如均匀随机选择）的策略，并将其应用于随机优化中的在线到批量转换，获得了具有"无妥协"保证的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅能获得每个候选对象单次噪声估计的约束下，如何最优地选择最大值是一个基础问题。现有策略往往在某些情形下表现良好，但可能在某些情况下劣于简单的均匀随机选择基线。作者希望设计既不被其他策略一致占优（可容许性）、又绝不比基线差的策略，实现"无妥协"（no-compromise）的保障。

Method: 作者在决策论框架下研究该选择问题，将策略视为从观测向量 $\mathbf{y} = (y_1,\dots,y_n)$ 到选择概率分布 $\mathbf{p} = (p_1,\dots,p_n)$ 的映射。核心方法包括：(1) 刻画可容许策略的充分必要条件——策略必须最大化某个关于所选 $x_i$ 的期望效用，其中效用函数满足特定凸性条件；(2) 构造一类截断式（threshold-based）策略，仅当某个 $y_i$ 相对于其他估计超出一定阈值时才以较高概率选择该元素，否则退化为基线策略；(3) 将该框架应用于随机优化的在线到批量（online-to-batch）转换，将在线学习产生的迭代序列视为候选，利用所提策略选择最终模型。

Result: 理论上，作者证明所提策略具有可容许性，且在任意问题实例上均不劣于基线策略（如均匀随机选择）。在随机优化应用中，得到的在线到批量转换界具有"无妥协"性质：在最坏情况下与标准随机迭代选择持平，而在"良性"情形（如在线算法后期迭代质量明显提升）下可显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 该工作为噪声环境下的最大选择问题提供了具有可容许性和基线保障的策略设计框架，弥合了纯粹贪心策略与保守基线策略之间的鸿沟。在随机优化等应用中，该框架实现了"无妥协"的在线到批量转换，既能利用良性结构获得更好性能，又不会在最坏情况下付出额外代价。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting the largest among $n$ unknown values $x_1,\dots,x_n$ given only a single unbiased estimate $y_i$ for each $x_i$. We design strategies that are simultaneously admissible (not uniformly dominated by any other strategy) and also never worse than a given baseline such as uniform random selection. We provide an application to stochastic optimization, where we obtain online-to-batch conversion bounds with a desirable "no-compromise" guarantee: they are never worse than standard random iterate selection, and yet can be significantly better in benign settings.

</details>


### [217] [Detecting Functional Memorization in Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12764)
*Matthieu Meeus,Anil Ramakrishna,Matthew Grange,Zheng Xu,Luca Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究通过反事实实验证明，大语言模型存在"功能记忆"现象——即使代码在文本层面不相似，模型仍能复现训练数据中的功能逻辑，因此仅靠文本重叠检测不足以审计代码记忆风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要通过文本重叠来审计LLM是否记忆了训练数据，但代码具有"功能等价而文本相异"的特性——两段代码可以实现完全相同的逻辑但文本层面截然不同。因此，仅依赖逐字匹配的指标无法检测模型是否在功能层面记忆了训练代码，存在审计盲区。

Method: 构建反事实实验框架，以Olmo-3-32B为对象：设置"中训练模型"（暴露于目标代码）和"预训练参考模型"（未暴露）两组对照。向两组模型输入相同的Python函数签名作为提示，然后从文本相似度和功能相似度两个维度评估生成结果——功能相似度采用LLM-as-a-judge和基于执行的评测方法。

Result: 实验结果显示，暴露于目标代码的模型在功能层面显著复现了训练数据中的代码逻辑，即使这些生成代码在文本层面与原始训练数据并不相似，明确证明了功能记忆的存在。

Conclusion: LLM确实存在超越文本层面的"功能记忆"现象，提示社区亟需开发能够检测功能逻辑复现的审计指标，而非仅依赖传统的文本重叠度量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate code at scale. Meanwhile, prior work has investigated whether training data may be recoverable from model outputs, by auditing the textual overlap between training examples and model generations. Code, however, can be functionally equivalent while textually dissimilar. In this work, we study functional memorization: extraction of functional logic beyond what verbatim metrics detect. We construct a counterfactual setup for Olmo-3-32B, comparing a midtrained model (exposed to target code) against a pretrained reference (not exposed). We prompt both models with Python function signatures and measure both textual and functional similarity (i.e., LLM-as-a-judge, execution-based). Our results show clear evidence of functional memorization, highlighting the need for auditing metrics that go beyond textual overlap.

</details>


### [218] [ProPlay: Procedural World Models for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12780)
*Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Yiyang Li,Ziming Li,Xiaoguang Guo,Weixiang Sun,Chuxu Zhang,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProPlay 提出一种程序化世界模型，将成功轨迹抽象为程序并组织成程序图（procedure graph），通过"程序级预演"（procedure-level preplay）在已知图结构上模拟未来路径作为软引导，执行后利用环境反馈优化图结构，从而在部分可观测环境中实现Agent的持续自我进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-Agent方法虽依赖记忆或规划模块，但很少在二者之间形成闭环以持续优化对环境动态的内部理解。在部分可观测环境中，Agent需要主动探索、从有限反馈中学习并判断何时信任过往经验，这仍是难题。

Method: ProPlay将成功轨迹抽象为"程序"（procedures），并组织成捕捉任务阶段间因果转移关系的程序图。每条转移边关联一个"可靠性记录嵌入"（reliability record embedding），用于从历史结果中估计其任务特定贡献。每轮开始前，在已知图结构上模拟未来程序化轨迹作为结构化软引导；执行后利用环境反馈优化图结构。

Result: 在公开基准测试上，ProPlay相较强基线方法在环境理解和自我进化能力方面均取得持续提升。

Conclusion: ProPlay通过程序图与程序级预演机制，有效弥合了记忆与规划之间的闭环，使Agent能够在部分可观测环境中持续精炼对环境的内部建模，展现出更强的自我进化能力。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents are expected to improve through interaction without external supervision, but this remains difficult in partially observable environments where agents must explore actively, learn from limited feedback, and decide when to trust prior experience. Existing LLM-agent methods often rely on memory or planning modules, yet they rarely close the loop between them to continually refine an internal understanding of environment dynamics. We introduce ProPlay, a procedural world model that supports procedure-level preplay, where agents can rehearse future procedural paths using the learned world knowledge. Rather than representing experience as isolated rules or low-level action constraints, ProPlay abstracts successful trajectories into procedures and organizes them in a procedure graph that captures causal transitions among task stages. Each transition is associated with a reliability record embedding to estimate its task-specific contribution from past outcomes. Before each episode, ProPlay simulates future procedural trajectories over known graph structures as structured soft guidance; after execution, it refines the graph using environment feedback. Experiments on public benchmarks show that ProPlay consistently improves environment understanding and self-evolution capability over strong baselines. Our code has been released in https://github.com/antman9914/proplay.

</details>


### [219] [SymQNet: Amortized Acquisition for Low-Latency Adaptive Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12808)
*Yash Vardhan Tomar,Dheeraj Peddireddy,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SymQNet 是一种基于离线强化学习的自适应哈密顿量学习方法，通过预训练的后验条件采集策略实现低延迟在线推理，在横场伊辛模型基准上将采集延迟相对传统在线方法降低 47-72 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自适应哈密顿量学习中，每次后验更新后重新计算贝叶斯设计规则（如 Fisher 信息搜索、BALD）需要数秒，在数百次 shots 的实验中累积成显著的时间开销，使自适应控制在实际重复性低延迟场景中难以实用。

Method: 提出 SymQNet，一种摊余（amortized）强化学习方法。离线阶段训练一个后验条件采集策略网络；在线阶段仅需执行快速策略前向传播，同时保持贝叶斯后验反馈机制，从而将在线计算成本从重复优化问题转化为单次神经网络推理。

Result: 在横场伊辛模型基准上，5 量子比特时 SymQNet 的采集决策延迟相对 bounded Fisher-information search 和 bounded two-step BALD 分别降低 47.1 倍和 72.6 倍；12 量子比特时完整模拟步骤 SymQNet 仅需 1.02 秒，而 bounded two-step BALD 需 13.27 秒。

Conclusion: 学习型采集策略能够使自适应哈密顿量学习在重复性低延迟工作负载下变得实际可行，为量子设备校准与表征提供了高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Adaptive Hamiltonian learning is central to calibrating and characterizing quantum devices. In an adaptive controller, choosing the next experiment is itself a computation. Bayesian design rules are recomputed after every posterior update, and that step can take seconds. Across hundreds of shots, those seconds become a significant wall-clock cost for adaptivity. We introduce SymQNet, an amortized reinforcement-learning approach for low-latency adaptive Hamiltonian learning. SymQNet learns a posterior-conditioned acquisition policy offline, then uses a fast policy forward pass online while retaining Bayesian posterior feedback. On transverse-field Ising benchmarks, SymQNet substantially reduces acquisition latency relative to bounded Fisher-information search and bounded two-step Bayesian active learning by disagreement (BALD). At five qubits, it reduces acquisition-only decision latency by $47.1\times$ and $72.6\times$ relative to these online baselines; at twelve qubits, full simulated steps take $1.02$ s for SymQNet versus $13.27$ s for bounded two-step BALD. Overall, we show that learned acquisition can make adaptive Hamiltonian learning practical for repeated low-latency workloads.

</details>


### [220] [CLARITree: Cholesky and Lookahead Accelerations for Regression with Interpretable Piecewise Linear Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12840)
*Yixiao Wang,Hayden McTavish,Varun Babbar,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于构建近最优、稀疏、分段线性回归树的新算法，结合了前瞻搜索策略与Gram矩阵的高效秩一Cholesky更新，在计算效率、预测精度和稀疏性之间实现了良好的权衡，且可扩展性显著优于当前最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贪心回归树虽可解释性强，但性能并非最优；基于动态规划和分支定界的最优方法计算代价过高，无法用于通用线性回归树；而现有近最优方法主要集中在分类任务中。因此，需要一种面向回归任务、兼具高效性与近最优性的分段线性回归树构建方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的近最优稀疏分段线性回归树算法，核心是将前瞻式（lookahead）搜索策略与Gram矩阵的秩一Cholesky高效更新相结合，从而大幅降低搜索过程中的计算开销。

Result: 从理论和实证两方面证明，该方法在计算效率、预测精度和稀疏性之间取得了有利的权衡，且可扩展性显著优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 通过智能的前瞻搜索与高效的矩阵更新策略，可以在回归树构建中同时实现近最优解、稀疏性和高计算效率，为该领域提供了有前景的新方向。

Abstract: Regression trees are among the most interpretable yet expressive model classes in machine learning. Historically, greedy induction has been the dominant approach for constructing well-performing regression trees. While optimal methods based on dynamic programming and branch-and-bound exist, they are computationally prohibitive for general linear regression trees, despite often achieving substantially better performance than greedy approaches. Recent work has shown that specialized lookahead strategies can dramatically improve runtime while maintaining near-optimal performance, primarily in classification settings. In this work, we develop a novel algorithm for near-optimal, sparse, piecewise linear regression trees that combines a lookahead-style search strategy with efficient rank-one Cholesky updates of the Gram matrix. We demonstrate, both theoretically and empirically, that our method achieves a favorable trade-off between computational efficiency, predictive accuracy, and sparsity, and scales significantly better than the current state of the art.

</details>


### [221] [TimeROME-DLM: Temporal Causal Tracing and Low-Rank Inference-Time Knowledge Editing for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12841)
*Zhengtao Yao,Liuyang Song,Hongbo Zhang,Chenhao Wei,Haoyan Xu,Guang Yang,Siheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimeROME-DLM 是首个面向掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的训练无关、梯度无关、推理时知识编辑框架。它通过时序间接效应（TIE）因果追踪协议定位关键坐标，利用闭式低秩残差编辑记忆实现单次岭回归更新，在冻结模型权重的前提下高效完成知识编辑与遗忘，性能媲美甚至超越针对自回归模型的现有方法，且计算成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前所有知识编辑与遗忘方法（如ROME、MEMIT）均针对自回归Transformer设计，其假设在迭代去噪的MDLM下失效，且需要梯度更新导致数十GB显存开销并引发模型崩溃。MDLM已能与AR LLM匹敌，但缺乏对应的知识编辑框架，亟需一种训练无关、梯度无关的推理时编辑方案来填补该空白。

Method: 提出TimeROME-DLM，包含两大组件：(1) 时序间接效应（TIE）因果追踪协议——针对每个事实，识别在后续去噪步骤中最强驱动目标预测的坐标；(2) 闭式低秩残差编辑记忆——聚合所有遗忘事实的subject keys和target deltas，在每个扩散前向步骤中对该坐标施加单次岭回归正则化更新，并通过稀疏化控制utility spillover。整个过程中骨干权重保持冻结，仅需在验证集上调优三个超参数（α, λ, q）。

Result: 在TOFU forget01基准上，使用TOFU微调的LLaDA-8B-Base模型，TimeROME-DLM将遗忘集对数概率降低约83 nats，同时保留集对数概率几乎不变（utility-safe操作点下波动在1 nat内），支持50个顺序插入事实。该方法零额外显存，推理速度比最强训练时基线快4-14倍，并可扩展至400个事实（亚线性扩展）。方法直接迁移至LLaDA-8B-Instruct、Dream-7B、MMaDA-8B、DiffuLLaMA-7B及LLaDA-MoE-1.4B等多个MDLM变体。

Conclusion: TimeROME-DLM成功弥合了AR LLM与MDLM之间在"定位-编辑"能力上的差距，以极低的计算代价实现了首个通用、高效的MDLM知识编辑框架，展示了在扩散语言模型上进行训练无关知识操控的可行性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) such as LLaDA now rival autoregressive (AR) LLMs, but every existing knowledge-editing and unlearning method (ROME, MEMIT, etc.) targets AR transformers and either makes assumptions that fail under iterative denoising, or requires gradient updates whose backward-pass activations cost tens of GB of extra VRAM and which collapse MDLMs at standard learning rates. We introduce TimeROME-DLM, the first training-free, gradient-free, inference-time knowledge-editing framework for MDLMs. It couples two components: a Temporal Indirect Effect (TIE) causal-tracing protocol that identifies, for each fact, the coordinate whose intervention most strongly drives the object prediction at later denoising steps; and a closed-form, low-rank residual edit memory that aggregates subject keys and target deltas across all forget facts and applies a single ridge-regularised update at that coordinate at every diffusion forward, with sparsification to limit utility spillover. Backbone weights stay frozen; only three hyperparameters (alpha, lambda, q) are tuned on a small validation split. On TOFU forget01 with TOFU-finetuned LLaDA-8B-Base, TimeROME-DLM cuts forget-set log-probability by roughly 83 nats. The same configuration transfers to LLaDA-8B-Instruct, Dream-7B, MMaDA-8B, DiffuLLaMA-7B, and LLaDA-MoE-1.4B. It keeps retain-set log-probability nearly flat (within ~1 nat at the utility-safe operating point) across 50 sequentially inserted facts, delivers a four- to fourteen-fold wall-clock speedup with zero additional VRAM over the strongest converged training-time baseline, and scales sub-linearly to 400 facts. TimeROME-DLM closes the locate-then-edit gap between AR LLMs and MDLMs at a fraction of the computational cost.

</details>


### [222] [Interpretable Factor Decomposition for Decision Intelligence in Large-Scale Financial Markets: Evidence from China's A-Share Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12843)
*Xiao Han,Yao Xiao,Zhen Zhang,Moxuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一套可解释的机器学习流水线，将A股横截面收益可预测性分解为可审计的因子贡献。使用XGBoost+TreeSHAP对2009-2019年3632只A股进行60个月滚动窗口回测，获得月均+2.38%的多空利差（夏普比率2.23），且经四因子调整后Alpha依然显著。SHAP分解显示行为信号（换手率、动量）贡献了58.2%的预测归因，远高于估值比率的10.7%。消融分析进一步揭示了特征间的替代结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统资产定价研究依赖线性模型和少数因子，难以捕捉高维特征空间中的非线性关系和交互效应，且模型缺乏可解释性。本文旨在利用可解释机器学习（XGBoost + TreeSHAP），将横截面收益预测透明化，实现对不同类别因子贡献的可审计分解。

Method: 采用XGBoost梯度提升树模型，结合TreeSHAP进行特征归因。样本为2009年至2019年共3632只A股，使用60个月滚动训练窗口，预测其后55个月样本外数据。通过将股票按预测收益分为五等分构建多空组合。归因层面，按55个行业分组计算SHAP值，区分行为信号与估值比率等因子的贡献；同时引入消融分析交叉验证特征重要性排名，并揭示SHAP与消融分析之间的分歧所反映的特征替代结构。

Result: XGBoost模型样本外AUC均值为0.547，顶部对底部的多空组合月均收益为+2.38%（Newey-West t = 5.94，年化夏普比率2.23）。经Carhart四因子调整后Alpha为+2.31%/月（t = 7.48），仍高度显著。SHAP分解显示：行为信号（换手率、动量）平均贡献58.2%，估值比率仅贡献10.7%。消融分析与SHAP排名总体一致，但二者在局部存在分歧，揭示了单一方法无法捕捉的特征替代结构。

Conclusion: 可解释机器学习流水线能有效捕捉A股市场中的非线性收益预测关系，并生成显著且稳健的超额收益。行为信号是A股横截面收益预测的主导驱动力，远超估值因子。SHAP与消融分析的互补使用为理解高维特征间的替代关系提供了新视角，有助于构建更透明、可审计的量化投资框架。

Abstract: We present an interpretable machine learning pipeline to decompose Cross-Sectional Equity Return Predictability into auditable factor contribution. We apply an XGBoost model with TreeSHAP attribution and conduct stress testing on 3632 Chinese A-share stocks from 2009 until 2019. Using 60-month, rolling windows over 55 months of out-of-sample data, XGBoost obtains a mean AUC of 0.547 and +2.38%/month (Newey-West t = 5.94; Annualized Sharpe 2.23) long-short spread for the top vs bottom quintiles. This alpha is persistent after adjusting for the Carhart four-factor model (+2.31%/month; t = 7.48). SHAP Decomposition indicates that behavioral signals (turnover and momentum) account for 58.2% of predictive attribution compared to 10.7% for valuation ratios, on average, across 55 industry groups. Ablation analysis serves to cross-validate this ranking and provides evidence that SHAP and ablation diverge in a manner that highlights feature substitutability structure that is largely invisible to either method used in isolation.

</details>


### [223] [Multimodal Graph Negative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12863)
*Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Guang Zeng,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态属性图（MAG）中节点级分支语义不平衡问题，提出GraphMNL框架，利用负学习（Negative Learning）作为跨分支引导机制，通过图感知可靠性仲裁识别主导/劣势分支，对非目标类施加目标保持的负学习，从而在不可靠分支一致性场景下解耦目标监督与分支引导。在Grocery（72.47%准确率）和Reddit M（76.60 F1）上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态属性图中，不同节点在各模态分支上的语义信息量和可靠性存在差异，即节点级分支语义不平衡。现有方法依赖跨分支一致性或对齐，将主导分支预测作为监督信号，但当主导分支存在偏差时，强制模仿会传播偏差并抑制原本对分类有用的语义信息。因此需要一种新的跨分支引导机制，在不依赖主导分支作为可靠教师的前提下处理分支不平衡问题。

Method: 提出GraphMNL框架：1）构建分支库（branch library），存储各模态分支的表示；2）通过图感知可靠性仲裁（graph-aware reliability arbitration）识别每个节点的主导分支和劣势分支；3）门控不稳定迁移（gating unstable transfer），阻止不可靠分支间的知识传递；4）目标保持的负学习（target-preserving negative learning），让劣势分支学习"节点不属于哪些类"而非被迫模仿主导分支的预测，同时通过监督损失学习正确类别，实现目标监督与分支引导的解耦。

Result: 在Grocery数据集上达到72.47%准确率，在Reddit M数据集上达到76.60 F1分数，均取得当前最优性能。

Conclusion: GraphMNL通过负学习实现跨分支引导，有效解决了多模态属性图中节点级分支语义不平衡问题，避免了传统跨分支对齐方法因主导分支偏差导致的误差传播，证明了负学习作为跨模态引导策略的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs) integrate graph topology with heterogeneous modality attributes, such as text and images, thereby enabling richer modeling of complex relational systems. However, such expressiveness also makes learning on MAGs depend on multiple semantic sources, including structural topology, textual and visual attributes, each of which can be regarded as a branch for node representation. Node-level branch semantic imbalance arises when these branches differ across nodes in semantic informativeness and reliability: a branch that provides discriminative semantics for one node may mislead another due to bias in modality quality or structural context. Existing methods often mitigate such heterogeneity through cross-branch agreement or alignment, implicitly treating the dominant prediction as reliable supervision. When the dominant branch is biased, forced imitation may propagate its bias to other branches and suppress original semantics that are useful for classification. We propose GraphMNL, a graph-aware multimodal negative learning framework that addresses this issue by using Negative Learning as cross-branch guidance. Instead of forcing inferior branches to imitate a teacher prediction, the model teaches them which classes a node is unlikely to belong to. GraphMNL builds a branch library, identifies dominant and inferior branches via graph-aware reliability arbitration, gates unstable transfer, and applies target-preserving negative learning over non-target classes. This design decouples target supervision from branch guidance so that supervised losses learn the correct class, while Negative Learning suppresses unlikely alternatives when branch agreement is unreliable. Through the comprehensive experimental evaluation, GraphMNL achieves the best performance on Grocery datasets with 72.47% accuracy and 76.60 F1 score on Reddit M datasets.

</details>


### [224] [SMGFM: Spectral Multimodal Graph Pretraining for Multimodal-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12867)
*Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Guang Zeng,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SMGFM 是一种面向多模态属性图（MAG）的频谱预训练框架，核心思想是利用图频率分解来区分结构诱导语义与模态固有语义的角色，通过切比雪夫滤波器构建频率分辨的模态 token，并在跨模态融合前进行频带级语义路由，从而避免空间域纠缠和强行对齐，在多个 MAG 基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图学习将拓扑与节点特征耦合，但在 MAG 中，结构诱导语义（需平滑一致性）与模态固有语义（需保留局部细粒度差异）对下游任务的贡献不同，统一耦合会导致两类语义冲突。因此，核心动机是在跨模态融合前，先识别和区分不同频段的语义角色。

Method: 提出 SMGFM 框架：（1）利用可扩展切比雪夫滤波器将每个模态的节点信号分解为不同图频率频段；（2）为每个频段分配语义角色（低频→拓扑一致语义，高频→模态特有语义）；（3）通过拓扑条件路由估计各频段耦合可靠性；（4）在融合前执行频段-模态交互；（5）设计频率路由目标，对齐平滑一致性路由的同时保留模态特有路由。

Result: 在多个 MAG 数据集上，SMGFM 在图级别和模态级别任务上均取得 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: 通过在频谱域解耦不同语义角色并在跨模态融合前进行频率感知路由，SMGFM 有效解决了 MAG 中结构语义与模态语义的冲突问题，验证了图频率先验在多模态图学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs) couple graph topology with node semantics from text, images, and other modalities. Traditional graph learning contextualizes node semantics by coupling topology with node features. However, this coupling design becomes troublesome in MAGs, where structure-induced and modality-intrinsic semantics may contribute differently to downstream tasks. Structure-induced semantics promote relational consistency through smooth topological variation, whereas modality-intrinsic semantics often encode local, fine-grained distinctions that should not be uniformly smoothed or aligned. Therefore, the key challenge is to identify semantic roles before cross-modal fusion. To this end, we leverage graph-frequency variation as a prior, where low-frequency components capture topology-consistent semantics and high-frequency components preserve modality-specific semantics. Based on this intuition, we propose SMGFM, a spectral multimodal graph pretraining framework that decomposes each modality-specific node signal into graph-frequency bands and assigns band-level semantic roles before cross-modal interaction. Concretely, SMGFM constructs frequency-resolved modality tokens with scalable Chebyshev filters, estimates their coupling reliability through topology-conditioned routing, and performs band-modality interaction before fusion. Its frequency-routed objectives align smooth consensus routes while preserving modality-specific routes, mitigating spatial-domain entanglement and uniform cross-modal alignment. Extensive experiments conducted on the MAG datasets demonstrate that SMGFM achieves state-of-the-art performance across graph-level and modality-level tasks.

</details>


### [225] [Multi-Bitwidth Quantization for LLMs Using Additive Codebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12876)
*Liza Babaoglu,Shuangyi Chen,Ashish Khisti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Drop-by-Drop 是一种多比特宽度的训练后量化框架，允许从单一训练模型实现推理时的LLM权重精度控制，基于信息论和逐次细化理论，通过Matryoshka式监督和加性码本结构，在保持竞争力的困惑度和准确率的同时显著降低存储和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在不同资源约束的异构硬件上部署时，需要在性能与效率之间自适应权衡，而无需重新训练。

Method: Drop-by-Drop方法：(1) 理论底座：基于率失真理论，证明服从高斯分布的LLM权重可在加权MSE失真下通过逐次细化实现最优重建，失真度量由LLM损失函数驱动；(2) 实践实现：在损失函数中引入Matryoshka式监督，利用加性码本结构，使得码本的有序子集可在各精度级别上产生准确的部分重建；(3) 单一模型输出多个比特宽度，节省存储和内存。

Result: 在Qwen、LLaMA、Gemma、Mistral等主流架构上，以单一checkpoint服务多个比特宽度，保持了竞争力的困惑度和准确率，同时显著降低了存储和内存开销。

Conclusion: Drop-by-Drop成功实现了无需重训练的自适应多精度LLM量化，验证了逐次细化理论在LLM压缩中的有效性，为异构部署场景提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across heterogeneous hardware with varying resource constraints, the ability to adaptively manage the trade-off between performance and efficiency without retraining is critical. We propose Drop-by-Drop, a novel multi-bitwidth post-training quantization framework that enables inference-time precision control over LLM weights from a single trained model. Our method is theoretically grounded in information theory and successive refinement. We establish that LLM weights, which commonly follow a Gaussian distribution, can be optimally reconstructed with increasing fidelity as additional bits are incorporated, under a weighted mean squared error distortion motivated by LLM loss functions. To realize this in practice, Drop-by-Drop incorporates Matryoshka-style supervision into the loss function, exploiting the structure of additive codebooks. Drop-by-Drop produces a single model where ordered subsets of codebooks yield accurate partial reconstructions at each precision level. This approach significantly reduces storage and memory overhead by allowing a single checkpoint to serve multiple bitwidths, while maintaining competitive perplexity and accuracy across major architectures, such as Qwen, LLaMA, Gemma, and Mistral.

</details>


### [226] [LongSpike: Fractional Order Spiking State Space Models for Efficient Long Sequence Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12895)
*Xinrui He,Qiyu Kang,Xuhao Li,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongSpike 将分数阶状态空间建模（f-SSM）引入脉冲神经网络，突破一阶ODE的记忆瓶颈，有效捕获长程依赖，在多个长序列基准上超越现有SNN且保持稀疏计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN架构依赖一阶常微分方程控制神经元状态转换，存在"无记忆"瓶颈，难以捕获长序列任务中的复杂长程依赖关系。

Method: 提出LongSpike框架，将分数阶微积分引入SNN：1）用分数阶状态空间模型（f-SSM）替代传统整数阶SSM，赋予神经元动力学层级化长记忆核；2）设计支持高效并行训练的状态空间公式，缓解分数阶算子带来的计算开销与并行化难题。

Result: 在Long Range Arena (LRA)、大规模WikiText-103和Speech Commands等挑战性基准上，LongSpike在准确率上超越现有最先进SNN方法，同时保持稀疏突触计算特性。

Conclusion: LongSpike验证了分数阶SSM在脉冲神经网络中的有效性，成功打破一阶ODE的"无记忆"瓶颈，为SNN处理长序列任务开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) are well-regarded for their biological plausibility and energy efficiency in processing sequential data. However, dominant SNN architectures typically rely on first-order Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) to govern neuronal state transitions. This first-order assumption imposes a "memoryless" bottleneck, limiting the model's capacity to capture the complex, long-range dependencies inherent in long-sequence tasks. In this work, we propose LongSpike, a novel SNN framework that integrates fractional-order State-Space Modeling, or f-SSM, from control theory into the spiking domain. By extending traditional integer-order SSMs to the fractional-calculus regime, LongSpike enables the hierarchical integration of neuronal dynamics with long-memory kernels. To mitigate the computational overhead and parallelization challenges typically associated with fractional operators, we leverage a state-space formulation that supports efficient, parallel training. Empirical evaluations on challenging benchmarks, including Long Range Arena (LRA), large-scale WikiText-103, and Speech Commands, demonstrate that LongSpike outperforms state-of-the-art SNNs in accuracy while preserving sparse synaptic computation. The code is available at https://github.com/xinruihe389-commits/LongSpike.

</details>


### [227] [PolicyGuard: Towards Test-time and Step-level Adversary Defense for Reinforcement Learning Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12896)
*Junfeng Guo Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PolicyGuard，一种基于高斯过程后验方差的测试时步级后门防御方法，通过构造伪轨迹实现逐时间步的不确定性计算，在七种RL游戏中取得SOTA检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL系统在真实世界中应用日益广泛，但其安全性未受足够重视。现有后门防御方法存在三大局限：(1)需访问智能体内部参数；(2)仅在模型或轨迹层面操作；(3)限于特定攻击类型。因此亟需一种更通用、无需访问模型参数、且能实现细粒度检测的防御方案。

Method: 提出PolicyGuard框架：(1)利用高斯过程(GP)后验方差作为不确定性度量，在测试时对每个时间步进行异常检测；(2)通过构造伪轨迹(pseudo trajectories)来解决RL中逐步不确定性计算的问题；(3)提供理论分析解释GP后验方差有效性的原因。该框架属于test-time防御，无需访问智能体内部参数，可在步级粒度上检测后门触发。

Result: 在七个RL游戏上的大量实验表明，PolicyGuard在大多数情况下达到SOTA检测性能：对抗扰动型攻击的平均AUROC为0.856，对抗对手智能体攻击的平均AUROC为0.859。

Conclusion: PolicyGuard作为一种无需访问模型参数、步级粒度的测试时防御方法，有效应对了RL中的多种后门攻击，具有较好的通用性和实用性，为RL系统的安全部署提供了保障。

Abstract: While real-world applications of reinforcement learning (RL) are becoming increasingly popular, the security of RL systems deserve more attention and exploration. In particular, recent work has revealed that RL agents are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where a victim agent behaves normally under standard conditions but executes malicious actions when a specific trigger is activated. Existing backdoor defenses for RL either require access to the agent's internal parameters, operate only at the model or trajectory level, or are limited to specific attack types. To ensure the security of RL agents, we propose \texttt{PolicyGuard}, a \textit{test-time step-level} backdoor defense which leverages Gaussian Process (GP) posterior variance and adapts pseudo trajectories to enable uncertainty computation for individual time step. Besides, we also provide theoretical foundations to explain the efficacy of GP posterior variance. Extensive experiments across seven RL games demonstrate that PolicyGuard achieves state-of-the-art detection performance in most cases, with average AUROC of 0.856 for perturbation-based attacks and 0.859 for adversary-agent attacks.

</details>


### [228] [Selecting Samples on Graphs: A Unified Dataset Pruning Framework for Lossless Training Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12913)
*Dongyue Wu,Zilin Guo,Xiaoyu Li,Jiajia Liu,Jingdong Chen,Nong Sang,Changxin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的基于图的 dataset pruning 框架，将数据集建模为加权图（节点权重编码内在价值，边权重编码外在多样性价值），把剪枝问题转化为最大权重团问题（MWCP），并通过贪心算法实现，在保证近似保证的同时，在ImageNet-1k上将训练时间减少超40%而不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集剪枝方法存在局限性：基于内在信号的方法独立评估样本，忽略多样性；基于外在信号的方法通过成对关系促进多样性，但缺乏对样本自身效用的考量。两者各执一端，在不同剪枝比率或数据分布下鲁棒性不足。因此需要一种统一框架来同时整合内在与外在两种效用。

Method: 将数据集构建为加权图，节点权重表示样本的内在价值（如损失、梯度范数等），边权重表示样本间的外在关系（如特征相似性带来的多样性收益）。在此图上，数据集剪枝被形式化为最大权重团问题（MWCP），目标是选择权重和最大的团。尽管MWCP是NP-hard，作者利用其结构特性，提出基于逐样本边际增益的贪心近似算法，并证明了在温和条件下该统一目标具有形式化近似保证，适用于广泛的重要性度量族。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k上使用ResNet-50，该方法在减少超40%训练时间的同时不牺牲精度；在多个数据集和不同剪枝比率下，性能均优于现有的内在、外在及混合剪枝方法，展现出更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 统一的图剪枝框架成功整合了内在与外在样本效用，赋予数据集剪枝以形式化近似保证和实用设计指南。实验验证了其在降低训练成本方面的显著优势，为大规模数据集的高效训练提供了一种更稳健、更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of modern training datasets has significantly increased computational cost, motivating dataset pruning~(DP) methods which retain only a subset of informative samples to reduce training cost.
  Existing pruning criteria typically rely on either intrinsic signals that assess samples independently or extrinsic signals that promote diversity via pairwise relations.
  While effective in their own specific regimes, each captures only one aspect of sample utility and lacks robustness across different pruning ratios or data distribution.
  In this work, we present a unified graph-based DP framework.
  By modeling the dataset as a weighted graph, where node weights encode intrinsic value and edge weights encode extrinsic value, DP can be cast as a Maximum Weight Clique Problem (MWCP).
  Although MWCP is NP-hard, its structure admits a principled greedy solution based on sample-wise marginal gains.
  Under a few mild conditions, we further prove that this unified objective enjoys a formal approximation guarantee, which applies to a broad family of importance metrics and provides practical design guidelines.
  Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms existing DP methods while substantially reducing training cost, reducing training time by over 40\% without sacrificing accuracy on ImageNet-1k with ResNet-50.

</details>


### [229] [Where Computation Lives Inside TabPFN: Causal Localisation of Attention Head Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12917)
*Atharva Gupta,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次对表格基础模型TabPFN 2.5进行因果机制分析，揭示了其逐特征注意力头存在明显的时间特化现象：一个主导头在峰值层的因果必要性比其余头高2-5倍，且其主导层位置随任务复杂度变化而转移；同时发现对比激活引导无法跨样本迁移，归因于其上下文学习机制与语言模型的稳定参数方向存在本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格基础模型（如TabPFN）虽性能优异，但其内部机制缺乏因果层面的理解。作者旨在填补这一空白，通过因果机制分析揭示TabPFN 2.5中注意力头如何跨层分配计算，并探究其推理阶段的可操控性。

Method: 在两个合成回归数据集上，采用激活修补（activation patching）、消融（ablation）和注意力熵（attention entropy）三种因果分析工具，系统考察TabPFN 2.5逐特征注意力头的跨层计算分布；此外，通过对比激活引导（contrastive activation steering）测试推理时的样本间可迁移性。

Result: 发现一个主导注意力头在峰值层的因果必要性比其他头高2-5倍；该主导头的最活跃层随任务复杂度不同而转移；其余注意力头呈现对称的后期层激活模式；注意力熵与激活修补结果一致，均指向主导头的计算活跃层；对比激活引导在样本间无法迁移。

Conclusion: TabPFN 2.5的注意力头存在清晰的时间特化分工，这与语言模型中常见的稳定参数化方向不同；其上下文学习机制通过上下文依赖的注意力编码任务结构，而非学习稳定的参数化方向，这从根本上限制了激活引导技术的迁移能力。

Abstract: We present the first causal mechanistic analysis of a tabular foundation model, investigating how TabPFN 2.5's feature wise attention heads distribute computation across layers. Using activation patching, ablation, and attention entropy across two synthetic regression datasets, we find clear temporal specialisation: one head's causal necessity dominates that of the others by 2 to 5 times at peak layer, with its dominant layer shifting across tasks of different complexity, while the remaining heads exhibit symmetric late layer profiles. Attention entropy and patching provide convergent evidence for the computationally active layers of the dominant head. We additionally investigate inference time steerability via contrastive activation steering, which fails to transfer across samples. We attribute this result to TabPFN's in context learning mechanism, which encodes task structure through context dependent attention rather than the stable parametric directions that make steering tractable in language models.

</details>


### [230] [LoRA-Muon: Spectral Steepest Descent on the Low-Rank Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12921)
*Franz Louis Cesista,Katherine Crowson,Cédric Simal,Stella Biderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA-Muon将Muon优化器的谱最陡下降规则应用于低秩适配场景，提出了一种对初始化不敏感、最优学习率可跨秩迁移的低秩微调优化器，其性能可逼近甚至超越全秩Muon/Shampoo族优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽降低了微调的计算和内存成本，但使用AdamW等逐因子优化器时存在三大痛点：(1)对初始化敏感；(2)最优学习率无法跨秩迁移；(3)常难以超越全秩微调基线。作者旨在寻找一种能作为全秩Muon/Shampoo族优化器的可靠低秩代理。

Method: 将Muon优化器的谱最陡下降（spectral steepest-descent）更新规则推广到低秩设置，提出LoRA-Muon；同时引入分裂权重衰减（split weight-decay）策略。通过矩阵谱更新替代QR分解，避免存储二阶矩，在加速器友好性和内存效率上优于Spectron和LoRA-RITE。

Result: 在计算匹配的TinyShakespeare实验中，rank-2代理即可恢复全秩最优学习率；rank-32的LoRA-Muon在种子平均扫描中获得低于全秩基线的平均验证损失。还证明了Spectron依赖任意因子缩放，不适合从严重不平衡因子开始的微调。

Conclusion: LoRA-Muon是Muon/Shampoo族优化器在低秩场景下的有效代理，具备学习率跨秩/宽度/深度/因子缩放的可迁移性，且在实现上比LoRA-RITE更高效，是低秩微调优化器的有力候选。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) significantly reduces compute and memory costs for finetuning Deep Learning models but is often harder to tune than dense training: when using factor-wise optimizers such as AdamW, it is sensitive to initialization choices, its optimal learning rates transfer poorly across ranks, and it often fails to beat dense baselines. We derive LoRA-Muon by applying the Muon optimizer's spectral steepest-descent rule to the low-rank setting. Along with our split weight-decay rule, our main claim is that LoRA-Muon is a good low-rank proxy for full-rank Muon and Shampoo-family optimizers. Its optimal learning rates transfer across rank, width, depth, and factor-rescaling. In our compute-matched TinyShakespeare study, a rank-$2$ proxy recovers the dense best tested learning rate, and a rank-$32$ LoRA-Muon run attains lower mean validation loss than the dense baseline in the seed-averaged sweep. We further show that the Spectron optimizer depends on arbitrary factor scaling, so it would likely be a poor fit when finetuning starts from badly imbalanced factors, and that LoRA-RITE's simplified QR-coordinate core implements the same spectral update. LoRA-Muon computes that update without QR-decomposition and avoids storing second moments, making it more accelerator-friendly and memory-efficient.

</details>


### [231] [Order Is Not Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12923)
*Gareth Seneque,Lap-Hang Ho,Nafise Erfanian Saeedi,Jeffrey Molendijk,Tim Elson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“秩序不等于控制”的核心论点，认为真正的控制需要一个接收端门控的响应定律。作者在多类系统（生物、LLM、适配器、随机算子）中识别出该定律，并提供了局部可容许控制和可测随机响应算子的实证证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对齐、可解释性、模型引导和神经扰动研究大量关注“秩序诱导对象”（如特征、方向、回路），但作者认为识别秩序并不等同于实现控制。控制需要更严格的条件——一个接收端门控的响应定律，明确输入如何通过介质、热浴和接收器状态转化为输出位移。缺乏这一框架导致了对因果性和可控性的过度声称。

Method: 作者提出了一个分母索引的算子框架，将材料状态、动作/驱动、热浴和接收器状态映射到响应位移、汇、努力量和势阱投影。通过跨越生物面板（小鼠ALM系统、线虫、斑马鱼）、LLM面板（四种材料条件下的生成输出响应定律）、宪法条件化适配器面板以及随机算子面板，验证该响应定律的局部性和可测性。LLM实验中，检验响应向量分量符号预测准确率，以及held-out观察者对系统效应和目标/谕示族的预测能力。

Result: （1）生物面板提供物理响应算子证据，同时排除了坐标同一性和控制器结论；（2）LLM面板显示，四种材料条件下响应向量分量符号预测准确率达72.8-73.7%，非零分量上升至84.3-84.8%；held-out观察者预测系统效应和目标/谕示族分别达93.6%和91.7%准确率；（3）宪法条件化适配器将易感性重塑为“制备介质”；（4）随机算子面板将测量机会与可部署行动策略分离。

Conclusion: 作者给出了介观控制层面上的驱动-耗散响应系统描述：驱动通过制备介质、热浴和接收器发挥作用，产生可容许运动、阻抗、汇或过驱动。证据支持局部可容许控制和可测随机响应算子的存在，但将可部署的预生成控制、隐变量/逻辑因果充分性、生物-LLM坐标同一性以及字面热力学量等问题置于研究范围之外。

Abstract: AI alignment, interpretability, steering, and neural perturbation studies identify order-inducing objects. We argue that order is not control. Control requires a receiver-gated response law: a denominator-indexed operator mapping material state, action/drive, bath, and receiver state to response displacement, sinks, effort, and basin projection. We identify it across biological, LLM, adapter, and stochastic-operator panels. The laws are local: an intervention can be admitted, saturated, sign-changing, leaky, or overdriven depending on medium, bath, receiver state, action port, and comparator. Control is assigned when finite effort moves a target or outcome-readout class under the same denominator while damage, null/evasive, invalid format, overdrive, and unnecessary effort stay bounded. Mouse ALM, C. elegans, and zebrafish panels provide physical response-operator evidence while excluding coordinate identity and controller conclusions. LLM panels show generated-output response laws: across four material conditions, response vectors are predictable at 72.8-73.7% component-sign accuracy, rising to 84.3-84.8% on nonzero components; held-out observers predict system-effect and target/oracle families at 93.6% and 91.7% accuracy. Constitution-conditioned adapters reshape susceptibility as prepared media, and stochastic-operator panels separate measured opportunity from deployable action policies. This gives a driven-dissipative response-system account at the mesoscopic control level: drives act through prepared media, baths, and receivers, producing admitted movement, impedance, sinks, or overdrive. The evidence supports local admitted control and measurable stochastic response operators, while leaving deployable pre-generation control, hidden/logit causal sufficiency, biological-to-LLM coordinate identity, and literal thermodynamic quantities outside scope.

</details>


### [232] [Is Spurious Correlation Removal Always Learnable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12930)
*Yibo Zhou,Bo Li,Hai-Miao Hu,Hanzi Wang,Xiaokang Zhang,Ruifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了不变学习中的一个条件计算屏障：即使不变结构在统计上可识别，在黑盒可采样的稀疏恢复原语假设下，存在多项式样本可学习但多项式时间算法无法以常数精度恢复的实例。此外，论文通过分离参数γ量化环境多样性，给出了极小极大风险界，并揭示了标签偏移下的相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典不变学习理论认为，只要不变结构在统计上可识别，就能被学习。但本文质疑这一观点，探索是否存在统计可识别但计算上难以学习的实例，即统计-计算鸿沟是否同样存在于不变学习中。

Method: 作者基于平均情况稀疏恢复归约，构建了一个黑盒可采样的监督稀疏恢复原语假设，并在此基础上构造了多环境、一维预测不变子空间（k=1）的可采样实例。通过穷举搜索证明多项式样本可学习性，同时论证任何多项式时间常数精度算法将违反该原语。进一步引入分离参数γ刻画环境多样性，在充分多样性和局部高斯正则条件下推导极小极大风险界，并分析标签偏移下估计误差的相变行为。

Result: 1）存在k=1的可采样实例，穷举搜索用多项式样本可学习，但任何多项式时间常数精度算法在稀疏恢复原语假设下不可行；2）极小极大风险为E[dist(ŷ,V_inv)²]=Θ(k(d-k)/(n|E|))；3）标签偏移下，在n*∝k(d-k)/(|E|γ²)处出现相变，精化估计误差按1/γ²缩放；4）合成与真实数据实验验证了上述差距与相变。

Conclusion: 不变学习中存在条件计算屏障，统计可识别性不足以保证多项式时间可学习性。环境多样性（γ）是控制可识别性和不变目标曲率的关键参数，其不足会导致估计误差剧烈放大，在标签偏移情形下尤为明显。

Abstract: Invariant learning can fail even when the invariant structure is statistically identifiable. We show a conditional computational barrier: under a black-box samplable supervised sparse recovery primitive motivated by average-case sparse-recovery reductions, there exist \emph{samplable} multi-environment instances with a one-dimensional predictive invariant subspace ($k=1$) that are learnable with polynomial samples by exhaustive search, while any polynomial-time constant-accuracy recovery algorithm would contradict the primitive. We further quantify environment diversity by a separation parameter $γ$, which controls identifiability and the curvature of invariance objectives. Under sufficient diversity and local Gaussian regularity, the minimax risk is $\mathbb{E}[\dist(\hat{V},V_{\mathrm{inv}})^2]=Θ(k(d-k)/(n|\mathcal{E}|))$, and under label-induced shifts a phase transition occurs at $n^*\propto k(d-k)/(|\mathcal{E}|γ^2)$ with refined estimation error scaling proportional to $1/γ^2$. Synthetic and real datasets illustrate the predicted gaps and transitions and motivate simple diversity diagnostics.

</details>


### [233] [Circuit Synchronization Precedes Generalization: Causal Evidence from Fourier Structure in Grokking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12966)
*Achyuthan Sivasankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer在模算术任务中的"grokking"现象（验证准确率从随机水平突然跃升至接近完美），将其归因于傅里叶电路的同步。作者提出了频率同步度（FSD）指标，可在grokking发生前500-3000步预测该现象，并通过控制权重衰减强度直接证明层间相位差是一个正则化现象，发现提前grokking的时间与1/λ成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: grokking现象的时机、因果结构和可控性至今理解不足。虽然已知其涉及傅里叶电路，但缺乏无需先验电路知识的早期预测指标，也不清楚能否主动控制grokking的发生时机。

Method: 提出频率同步度（FSD）：一种归一化的、经置换检验的傅里叶电路同步度量，无需先验电路知识。在9种模加法配置（素数p∈{53,71,97,113,131}，3个随机种子）上验证。通过分叉训练实验（在FSD峰值步改变权重衰减λ）建立因果关系，并进行架构消融（纯注意力、纯MLP、单层模型）。

Result: FSD在grokking前500-3000步同步（平均领先1722步，符号检验p≈0.004），在所有9个案例中均早于受限logit损失基线。因果实验表明提前grokking时间Δt与1/λ严格单调正比（两个干净案例R²=1.00和0.99），符合Δt~C/λ规律。架构消融显示：纯注意力模型有强FSD前兆；纯MLP永不grok；单层模型FSD滞后。

Conclusion: FSD是grokking最早可用的预测指标。层间相位同步是一个正则化驱动的现象，可通过权重衰减λ精确控制grokking时机，遵循Δt~C/λ的定量规律。傅里叶电路同步是多块电路层级的特性，单层结构不足以产生该前兆。

Abstract: Grokking -- where a transformer on modular arithmetic suddenly transitions from near-chance to near-perfect validation accuracy -- is attributed to a Fourier circuit, but its timing, causal structure, and controllability remain poorly understood. We introduce the Frequency Synchronization Degree (FSD), a normalised, permutation-tested metric for Fourier circuit synchronisation requiring no prior circuit knowledge. Across nine modular addition configurations (primes p in {53, 71, 97, 113, 131}, three seeds), FSD synchronises 500-3,000 steps before grokking (mean lead +1,722 steps; all nine positive, sign-test p~0.004), and precedes a restricted-logit loss baseline (Nanda et al.'s excluded loss) in all nine cases, making it the earliest available predictor. We provide direct causal evidence that the inter-phase gap is a regularisation phenomenon: forking training at the FSD-ceiling step and varying weight decay lambda produces strictly monotone earlier grokking, with Delta_t proportional to 1/lambda. This law replicates across three primes (p in {53,97,131}; R^2=1.00 and R^2=0.99 for two clean cases), captured as Delta_t ~ C/lambda, consistent with (1/lambda)*log(||W_mem||/tau). Architecture ablations show an attention-only model groks with a strong FSD precursor; an MLP-only model never groks; a single-layer model's FSD lags, confirming the precursor is a multi-block circuit property.

</details>


### [234] [Predicting Cognitive Load from Speech and Interaction Dynamics in Dyadic Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12971)
*Tahiya Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在自然协作对话中，利用语音和交互动态特征预测认知负荷。通过对53组二人对话的分析，提取静态声学、动态和交互特征，训练双头GRU编码器预测认知负荷评分。结果表明，对话交互特征（如话轮转换、参与不平衡）能有效预测时间压力、脑力需求等认知负荷维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语音的认知负荷估计研究大多在受控实验室环境下进行，其在自然协作对话中的可靠性尚不明确。作者旨在填补这一空白，探究真实二人对话场景中语音和交互动态特征是否能够预测感知认知负荷。

Method: 采集53组二人小组执行9项协作任务的音频数据；提取三类特征：静态声学特征、动态特征和交互特征；构建双头门控循环单元（GRU）编码器模型，用于预测认知负荷评分（包括时间需求、脑力工作、努力程度和任务表现等维度）。

Result: 对话交互信号对预测认知负荷具有显著作用：时间需求维度与话轮转换动态（如话语重叠、说话人切换）相关；脑力需求维度与说话人之间参与不平衡相关。这表明任务结构和对话交互对自然协作场景下的认知负荷建模至关重要。

Conclusion: 在自然协作对话中，除语音声学特征外，对话交互动态是预测认知负荷的关键信号来源。不同的认知负荷维度与不同类型的交互特征相关联，未来研究应重视任务结构及交互模式在认知负荷建模中的作用。

Abstract: Estimating cognitive load from speech has largely been studied in controlled laboratory settings, with limited understanding of its reliability in natural collaborative conversations. We investigate whether speech and interaction dynamics predict perceived cognitive load during dyadic conversations. We analyze audio from 53 dyads performing nine collaborative tasks and extract static acoustic, dynamic, and interaction features to train a two-head Gated Recurrent Unit encoder to predict cognitive load scores. Results show conversational interaction provides useful signals for predicting cognitive load related to time pressure, mental work, effort, and task performance. Temporal demand is associated with turn-taking dynamics such as overlap and speaker switch, while mental demand is linked to imbalanced participation between speakers. These findings highlight the importance of task structure and conversational interaction for modeling cognitive load in natural collaborative settings.

</details>


### [235] [EPM-JEPA: Operator-Side Experience Modulation in JEPA-Family World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12979)
*Vedant Pandya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 比较了两种在分布偏移下将累积经验注入JEPA预测器的方法：操作数侧注入（EI-JEPA）与算子侧LoRA调制（EPM-JEPA）。预注册对比结果显示两者差异未达显著标准（无效结果），但EPM-JEPA一致优于无记忆基线，而EI-JEPA表现不及基线。主要贡献在于揭示了D_shift轨迹背后的三个独立动力学过程，并据此提出PEM-JEPA作为改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA家族世界模型使用静态预测器，当测试时动态与训练分布不一致时，预测器权重无法自适应调整。需要探索在分布偏移下将累积经验有效整合到预测器中的机制，并理解不同注入策略的动力学行为。

Method: 在Moving MNIST数据集上施加重力偏移作为分布偏移场景。对比两种机制：(1) EI-JEPA——将压缩经验表征作为残差添加到预测器隐藏状态（操作数侧注入）；(2) EPM-JEPA——用相同表征通过LoRA生成低秩权重增量，作用于预测器权重（算子侧调制）。采用预注册对比方案，以D_shift^{n=50}为评估指标，三种子运行，并分析轨迹背后的动力学过程。

Result: 预注册比较：EPM-JEPA (0.7848±0.0078) 与 EI-JEPA (0.8238) 差异Δ=4.74%，未达显著标准（结果C：无效结果）。次要观察：EPM-JEPA比无记忆基线(0.8000)提升1.90%，跨种子一致；而EI-JEPA低于基线。动力学分析揭示D_shift轨迹由三个独立过程组成：缓冲区循环、EMA目标漂移、以及LoRA内在稳定瞬态(+0.021)，而非收敛到均衡。

Conclusion: 权重层面调制（LoRA）带来的性能增益是特定于算子侧的，操作数侧注入反而有害。更重要的是，系统性能轨迹并非收敛到稳态均衡，而是受多个独立动力学过程支配，存在"动力学峰值"限制。这直接催生了PEM-JEPA——一种基于物理原理的后续方法，以克服该动力学峰值问题。

Abstract: JEPA-family world models use a static predictor whose weights do not adapt when test-time dynamics diverge from training. We compare two mechanisms for incorporating accumulated experience into a JEPA predictor under distribution shift: operand-side injection, where a compressed experience representation is added as a residual to the predictor's hidden state (EI-JEPA), and operator-side modulation, where the same representation generates low-rank weight deltas via LoRA applied to the predictor's weights (EPM-JEPA). On a pre-registered comparison (Moving MNIST, gravity shift), EPM-JEPA (D_shift^{n=50} = 0.7848 +/- 0.0078, three seeds) differs from EI-JEPA (0.8238) by delta = 4.74% - Outcome C: a null result - by our stated criterion, a valid outcome. As a secondary, non-pre-registered observation, EPM-JEPA improves 1.90% over a no-memory baseline (0.8000), consistently across seeds, while EI-JEPA underperforms the baseline, indicating the benefit is specific to weight-level modulation. Our primary contribution is a mechanism analysis: the D_shift^{n=50} trajectory reflects three independent dynamical processes - buffer cycling, EMA target drift, and an intrinsic LoRA settling transient of +0.021 - rather than convergence to equilibrium. These findings motivate PEM-JEPA, a physics-grounded successor addressing this dynamical-peak limitation.

</details>


### [236] [Exposure Bias as Epistemic Underidentification in Recursive Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12990)
*Riku Green,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Telmo M Silva Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视递归多步预测中的分布偏移问题，论证了在部分可观测或状态截断条件下，递归展开不仅是分布偏移，更是一个认知欠识别（epistemic underidentification）问题。作者引入诱导状态和溯源变量的形式化框架，将误差分解为教师强制/展开不匹配、表征-类别近似和溯源信息缺口，并通过实验证明闭环增益来源于局部自适应及诱导状态分布的改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将递归多步预测的困难归因于训练（观测历史）与部署（自生成预测）之间的分布偏移。但作者指出该框架不完整：在部分可观测或状态截断条件下，即使潜在动力学是确定性的，单步贝叶斯监督也仅能识别观测上下文中的行为，而无法识别自生成诱导状态下的递归预测目标。这揭示了一个更深层的认知欠识别问题，亟需新的理论分析和解决方案。

Method: 作者首先证明递归展开中的认知欠识别问题的存在性；其次，引入诱导状态 Z 和溯源变量 P 的形式化定义，将诱导状态误差分解为三部分：(1) 教师强制与展开不匹配、(2) 表征-类别近似误差、(3) 溯源信息缺口；最后，提出一种简洁的二元溯源编码方法，实现溯源感知的校正，以在自生成状态下进行局部矫正。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 递归展开会进入一个独特的诱导状态区域；(2) 固定的诱导状态定义了一个独特的局部校正任务；(3) 闭环增益不仅来自局部自适应，还源于展开过程中访问的诱导状态分布的改变。二元溯源编码的溯源感知校正可进一步提升性能，但增益是有条件的而非均匀的。

Conclusion: 本文将曝光偏差重新定义为自诱导认知不确定性下的推理问题，揭示了递归展开中除分布偏移外更深层的认知欠识别结构。溯源感知校正提供了一种有条件的改进途径，为理解和缓解递归预测中的组合误差提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Recursive multi-step forecasting is usually framed as distribution shift: models are trained on observed histories but deployed on their own predictions. We show this framing is incomplete by proving that, under partial observability or state truncation, recursive rollout is also an epistemic underidentification problem. Even with deterministic latent dynamics, one-step Bayes supervision identifies behavior only on observed contexts and need not identify the deployed recursive predictor once rollout queries self-generated induced states whose correct local targets are not determined by numeric state alone. We formalize this with induced states $Z$ and provenance variables $P$, and derive a decomposition of induced-state error into teacher-forcing/rollout mismatch, representation--class approximation, and provenance information gaps. Empirically, we show that rollout enters a distinct induced-state regime, that fixed induced states define a distinct local corrective task, and that closed-loop gains arise not only from local adaptation but also from changing the induced states visited during rollout. Using a simple binary provenance encoding, provenance-aware correction can further improve performance, though gains are conditional rather than uniform. These results recast exposure bias as reasoning under self-induced epistemic uncertainty.

</details>


### [237] [DeepJEB++: Foundation Model-Driven Large-Scale 3D Engineering Dataset via 2D Latent Space Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12994)
*Soyoung Yoo,Leekyo Jeong,Jinsu Ra,Dongeon Lee,Sunwoong Yang,Hyogu Jeong,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepJEB++ 是一个由基础模型驱动的数据增强框架，它从少量喷气发动机支架种子设计出发，先在2D潜在空间进行多视图增强，再通过领域自适应的生成式基础模型提升为3D网格，并自动分配有限元仿真标签，最终实现了40倍的数据集扩展（<400 → 15,360）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计中缺乏大规模、带有物理性能标签的3D数据集，现有3D数据增强方法难以保留细微且多样的几何变化，同时后续仿真标注的自动化难度大——边界条件依赖于生成几何的具体形态。

Method: 三阶段框架：（1）在预训练2D潜在扩散模型上对多视图渲染进行微调，通过潜在插值合成新视图，并利用视觉语言模型（VLM）进行可制造性质量过滤；（2）通过领域自适应的生成式基础模型将验证后的2D图像提升为3D网格；（3）自动化管道识别每个网格上的载荷与螺栓接口，无需人工干预即可分配有限元标签（质量、应力、位移）。

Result: 从不足400个种子设计出发，DeepJEB++ 生成了15,360个带仿真标签的3D支架——扩展了40倍，且每个阶段仅使用单张GPU。评估围绕三个内在维度展开：可制造性、与SimJEB基准真相的标签保真度、分布一致性。

Conclusion: DeepJEB++ 证明了在资源受限条件下，通过2D潜在空间增强再转移到3D的策略，可以有效缓解工程设计领域3D数据稀缺问题，为可复现的工程AI研究提供公开可用的基准数据集。

Abstract: Data-driven engineering design is constrained by the lack of large-scale 3D datasets that pair geometry with physics-based performance labels. In particular, existing 3D data augmentation techniques have limitations in preserving subtle and diverse geometric variations, and it remains difficult to automate the subsequent simulation-labeling process, where boundary conditions vary depending on the generated geometry. We present DeepJEB++, a foundation-model-driven data-augmentation framework that expands a small seed set of jet engine brackets into a large, simulation-labeled 3D dataset under constrained resources. Our key idea is to augment in the data-rich 2D latent space, then transfer to 3D. In Stage 1, we fine-tune a pretrained 2D latent diffusion model on multi-view renders and synthesize novel views by latent interpolation, retaining manufacturable designs through a vision-language-model (VLM) quality filter. In Stage 2, the validated images are lifted to 3D meshes by a domain-adapted generative foundation model. In Stage 3, an automated pipeline recognizes the load and bolt interfaces on each mesh and assigns finite-element labels -- mass, stress, and displacement -- without manual intervention. We assess augmentation quality along three intrinsic axes: manufacturability, label fidelity against the SimJEB ground truth, and distributional consistency. Starting from fewer than 400 seed designs, DeepJEB++ yields 15,360 simulation-labeled 3D brackets -- a 40x expansion -- using a single GPU per stage. The dataset will be made publicly available to support reproducible engineering-AI research.

</details>


### [238] [scLLM-DSC: LLM-Knowledge Enhanced Cross-Modal Deep Structural Clustering for Single-Cell RNA Sequencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13007)
*Ping Xu,Pengjiang Li,Tian Du,Zaitian Wang,Jiawei Gu,Ziyue Qiao,Pengfei Wang,Yuanchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: scLLM-DSC 提出了一种融合大语言模型语义知识与图结构信息的跨模态深度聚类框架，通过知识驱动的语义视图和结构感知拓扑视图的对比对齐，解决了现有scRNA-seq聚类方法忽视基因生物学功能语义的问题，在多个基准上显著优于11种最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有scRNA-seq聚类方法侧重于挖掘数值统计模式，忽视了基因编码的内在生物学功能（语义不可知）。大语言模型虽有丰富的语义能力，但其生成式预训练目标与聚类等判别式下游任务之间存在结构不匹配，难以直接适配细胞聚类任务。

Method: 提出scLLM-DSC框架：1）构建两个视图——基于NCBI基因先验知识和Cell2Sentence上下文嵌入的知识驱动语义视图，以及通过图引导编码器提取的结构感知拓扑视图；2）引入跨模态对比对齐机制，在统一潜在空间中强制生物学语义与转录组特征之间的一致性。

Result: 在广泛基准测试中，scLLM-DSC在聚类准确率上显著优于11种最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的语义知识与深度结构聚类相结合，scLLM-DSC成功弥合了生成式预训练与判别式下游任务之间的鸿沟，为scRNA-seq细胞聚类提供了一种语义感知的新范式。

Abstract: Clustering is fundamental to scRNA-seq analysis, serving as a cornerstone for identifying cell populations and resolving tissue heterogeneity. However, existing methods focus on mining numerical statistical patterns, suffering from semantic agnosticism by neglecting the intrinsic biological functions encoded by genes. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer promising semantic capabilities, their direct adaptation to cell clustering is hindered by the structural mismatch between generative pre-training objectives and discriminative downstream tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose scLLM-DSC, a novel LLM-Knowledge Enhanced Cross-Modal Deep Structural Clustering framework. Diverging from data-driven paradigms, scLLM-DSC establishes a semantically-grounded representation by synergizing two views: a Knowledge-Driven Semantic View derived from NCBI gene priors and contextualized Cell2Sentence embeddings, and a Structure-Aware Topological View extracted via a graph-guided encoder. Crucially, we introduce a cross-modal contrastive alignment mechanism to enforce consistency between biological semantics and transcriptomic features within a unified latent space. Extensive benchmarks demonstrate that scLLM-DSC significantly outperforms eleven state-of-the-art baselines in clustering accuracy.

</details>


### [239] [CausalMoE: A Billion-Scale Multimodal Foundation Model for Granger Causal Discovery with Pattern-Routed Heterogeneous Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13024)
*Bo Liu,Di Dai,Jingwei Liu,Jiarui Jin,Xiaocheng Fang,Guangkun Nie,Hongyan Li,Shenda Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalMoE 是一种十亿级多模态格兰杰因果基础模型，通过模式路由的异构专家混合（Pattern-Routed MoE）和因果感知自注意力机制，显式建模时间序列中的片段级异质性，并首次融合 LLM/VLM 多模态先验，实现了分布偏移下可解释的稀疏因果图恢复，在全监督和小样本场景均达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经格兰杰因果发现方法采用"一刀切"范式，无法捕捉真实时间序列中的分布偏移和动态机制变化，导致表示纠缠和虚假因果图。因此需要一种能显式建模片段级异质性、解耦机制特异性动态与共享动态的新方法。

Method: CausalMoE 提出三大核心设计：(1) Pattern-Routed Mixture of Heterogeneous Experts，动态识别潜在时序模式并将片段路由至专门领域专家，实现机制解耦；(2) Causality-Aware Self-Attention，跨变量操作并通过近端优化产生稀疏格兰杰因果图；(3) 首次融合 LLM 和 VLM，将数值信号与文本、视觉先验对齐，正则化因果估计。

Result: CausalMoE 在全监督基准上达到新的最优水平，同时在传统方法失效的小样本场景中展现出有效泛化能力。

Conclusion: CausalMoE 通过引入异构专家混合机制与多模态先验融合，成功解决了神经格兰杰因果发现中的异质性建模难题，为大规模时序因果发现提供了可扩展且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Granger Causal Discovery (GCD) is fundamental for analyzing temporal dependencies in complex systems. However, existing neural GCD methods predominantly rely on a "one-size-fits-all" paradigm, struggling to capture distribution shifts and dynamic regime changes inherent in real-world time series. This often leads to entangled representations and spurious causal graphs. In this paper, we propose CausalMoE, a billion-scale multimodal Granger causal foundation model that explicitly models patch-level heterogeneity. CausalMoE introduces a Pattern-Routed Mixture of Heterogeneous Experts, which dynamically identifies latent temporal patterns and routes patches to specialized domain experts, effectively decoupling regime-specific mechanisms from shared dynamics. To ensure interpretable graph recovery, we design a Causality-Aware Self-Attention mechanism operating across variables, yielding sparse Granger causal graphs via proximal optimization. Furthermore, CausalMoE is the first to integrate LLMs and VLMs to align numerical signals with textual and visual priors, regularizing causal estimation in complex scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalMoE establishes a new state-of-the-art on fully supervised benchmarks, while effectively generalizing to few-shot settings where traditional methods fail.

</details>


### [240] [TWLA: Achieving Ternary Weights and Low-Bit Activations for LLMs via Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13054)
*Zhixiong Zhao,Zukang Xu,Zhixuan Chen,Xing Hu,Zhe Jiang,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TWLA 是一个后训练量化框架，首次实现大语言模型 W1.58A4（1.58-bit权重/4-bit激活）的端到端低精度推理，通过 E2M-ATQ 三元量化器、KOTMS 正交旋转重整分布和 ILA-AMP 层间感知混合精度三个组件，在保持高精度的同时显著加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三元量化方法因激活值的重尾分布（heavy-tailed activation distributions）而被迫保留高精度激活，这从根本上限制了端到端推理加速。亟需一种能够在权重和激活两端同时实现极低位量化的方案。

Method: TWLA 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) E2M-ATQ：两阶段优化，从欧氏空间初始化到流形重定位，最小化三元权重下的逐层输出误差；(2) KOTMS：利用 Kronecker 结构正交旋转，将权重重塑为适合三值化的三峰分布，同时该共享旋转在统计上抑制激活离群值；(3) ILA-AMP：显式引入相邻层二阶交互代价进行比特分配，联合优化由共享正交变换引起的逐层激活量化增益差异，防止薄弱层引发级联误差。

Result: 在 W1.58A4 设定下，TWLA 在多种大语言模型上保持高准确率，并实现显著的推理加速。

Conclusion: TWLA 成功突破了激活量化瓶颈，首次实现 LLM 的 W1.58A4 端到端低精度推理，兼顾精度与效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit exceptional general language processing capabilities, but their memory and compute costs hinder deployment. Ternarization has emerged as a promising compression technique, offering significant reductions in model size and inference complexity. However, existing methods struggle with heavy-tailed activation distributions and therefore keep activations in high precision, fundamentally limiting end-to-end inference acceleration. To overcome this limitation, we propose TWLA, a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that achieves 1.58-bit weight compression and 4-bit activation quantization while maintaining high accuracy. TWLA comprises three components: (1) Euclidean-to-Manifold Asymmetric Ternary Quantizer (E2M-ATQ) minimizes layer-output error under weight ternarization via a two-stage optimization from Euclidean initialization to manifold relocation; (2) Kronecker Orthogonal Tri-Modal Shaping (KOTMS) applies a Kronecker-structured orthogonal rotation to reshape weights into ternary-friendly tri-modal distributions, while the shared rotation statistically suppresses activation outliers; and (3) Inter-Layer Aware Activation Mixed Precision (ILA-AMP) explicitly introduces adjacent-layer second-order interaction costs in bit allocation and jointly optimizes for the layer-wise disparity of activation quantization gains induced by the shared orthogonal transform, preventing cascades triggered by a few weak layers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TWLA maintains high accuracy under W1.58A4, while delivering significant inference acceleration. The code is available at <https://github.com/Kishon-zzx/TWLA>.

</details>


### [241] [A green solvent screening tool for emerging materials via uncertainty aware, transformer enhanced transfer learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13060)
*Ioannis Kouroudis,Simon Ternes,Zhaosu Gu,Gohar Ali Siddiqui,Marina Ustinova,Angelo Lembo,Alessio Gagliardi,Aldo Di Carlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于预训练基础模型迁移学习的方法，在数据极其有限的情况下实现了对多种溶解度相关参数的高精度预测，并部署了可定制的绿色溶剂筛选工具，成功重新发现已知绿色溶剂并提出了新的候选替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着有机光伏、电池、催化等新兴技术的发展，溶剂使用量预计将大幅增长，用绿色溶剂替代传统溶剂成为迫切需求。机器学习可在此发挥重要作用，但关键溶解度参数的实验数据极为有限，严重制约了机器学习模型的效能。

Method: 将在QM9数据集上预训练的基础模型通过迁移学习应用到溶解度参数预测任务中，仅需极少量的目标数据。同时整合了不确定性量化（UQ）模块，使用户能够评估预测结果的置信度。最终部署了一个易于使用、可与高通量实验室集成的可定制化溶剂筛选与排序工具。

Result: 在拥有大量数据库的Hansen溶解度参数和介电常数上成功验证了基线性能；在Gutmann供体和受体数等数据极其有限的指标上也取得了高模型性能。总体上将溶解度描述符的数据量提升了数个数量级，且均为高质量预测。该工具重新发现了已知的绿色溶剂替代品，并提出了新的候选方案。

Conclusion: 通过迁移学习与小数据需求的结合，有效克服了溶解度数据稀缺的瓶颈，实现了对多种溶解度描述符的高质量预测。所部署的工具在绿色溶剂筛选与发现方面具有实用价值，可为可持续化学中的溶剂替代决策提供有力支持。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of solubility remains a central challenge across materials science and sustainable chemistry. In particular due to emerging technologies like organic and hybrid photovoltaics, batteries, and catalysis, solvent usage is expected to increase significantly within the coming years. Therefore, substituting solvents with greener alternatives is vital. This is where machine learning can have substantial impact. However, the limited data on critical parameters of solubility significantly constraints machine learning efficacy. In this work, we transfer a pre-trained foundational model on QM9 targets to our application with minimal data requirements. Additionally, the pipeline integrates uncertainty quantification, allowing the user to gauge the confidence of the predictions. As baseline, we succeed in predicting the Hansen solubility parameters and Dielectric Constant for which extensive databases exist. Importantly, we achieve high model performance on additional targets, such as Gutmann Donor and Acceptor numbers, where the available data is extremely limited. Overall, we augment data on solubility descriptors by orders of magnitude with high quality predictions. For effective dissemination, we deploy easy-to-use, easily integrateable with high throughput labs, customizable tool for ranking and screening possible solvent substitutes. Finally, we rediscovered known green solvent alternatives and proposed new candidates proving its relevance for finding eco-friendly solvents.

</details>


### [242] [Limits of spectral learning under noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13067)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski,Marta Sales-Pardo,Roger Guimera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了噪声数据下稀疏谱表示的监督回归问题，揭示了噪声引起的系数漂移具有可预测性，并通过白化特征几何推导出含噪与无噪系数向量重叠的闭式解，发现存在一个由内在噪声尺度决定的基本退化曲线和噪声阈值，超越该阈值后系数估计将失稳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱方法通过基函数展开逼近未知函数，但其系数在噪声下的稳定性机制尚不清楚。现有研究缺乏对噪声如何系统性影响谱系数估计的定量理解，这限制了从噪声数据中可靠恢复函数结构的能力。

Method: 作者在多个基（Fourier、Legendre、Bessel、Haar）和不同维度下，研究具有加性标签噪声的稀疏谱表示监督回归。首先白化经验特征几何，然后推导出含噪与无噪系数向量之间重叠度的闭式表达式，揭示由单一内在噪声尺度控制的通用退化曲线，并通过数值实验验证理论预测。

Result: 噪声引起的系数向量漂移是可预测的，其幅度取决于有效活跃谱模态数。重叠度遵循由单一内在噪声尺度参数化的通用退化曲线。数值实验在四种不同基函数上均验证了该理论预测，并证实存在一个基本噪声阈值，超过该阈值后系数估计变得不稳定。

Conclusion: 谱学习存在一个根本性的噪声阈值，对从噪声数据中恢复函数结构施加了内在限制。该结果为理解和改进噪声环境下的科学推断提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Learning functional relationships from noisy data is a central problem in scientific inference. Spectral methods approximate unknown functions by expanding them in a basis and estimating the corresponding coefficients from data, but the stability of these coefficients under noise remains poorly understood. Here we study supervised regression with additive label noise using sparse spectral representations across multiple bases and dimensions. We show that noise induces a predictable drift in the learned coefficient vector whose magnitude depends on the effective number of active spectral modes. After whitening the empirical feature geometry, we derive a closed-form expression for the overlap between noisy and noiseless coefficient vectors, revealing a universal degradation curve governed by a single intrinsic noise scale. Numerical experiments across Fourier, Legendre, Bessel, and Haar bases confirm the theoretical prediction. The results demonstrate that spectral learning exhibits a fundamental noise threshold beyond which coefficient estimates become unstable, placing intrinsic limits on recovering functional structure from noisy data.

</details>


### [243] [Emotional regulation improves deep learning-based image classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13081)
*Riccardo Emanuele Landi,João M. F. Rodrigues,Marta Chinnici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Emotional Regulation框架，通过人工主观体验将情感融入深度学习。该方法基于情感刺激进行预训练，在下游任务中平衡非情绪与情绪化响应，在CIFAR-10/100图像分类任务上超越了现有方法，成为情感增强深度学习的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感增强深度学习方法仅依赖客观神经生理因素，忽视了主观性在情感中的作用。作者旨在填补这一空白，将人工主观体验引入深度学习的情感建模。

Method: 提出Emotional Regulation框架：（1）基于情感刺激数据进行预训练；（2）在下游任务优化中平衡非情绪响应与情绪影响响应；（3）在ResNet和ViT两种骨干网络上，使用四个情感数据集进行预训练，以CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100为目标基准进行评估。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100图像分类任务上，该方法均优于原始ResNet和ViT骨干网络，并超越了所有相关对比方法，达到情感增强深度学习在大规模视觉数据集上的新SOTA。

Conclusion: Emotional Regulation是一种有前景的情感增强深度学习方法，证明了通过人工主观体验建模情感的有效性，并进一步验证了情感状态对机器学习任务优化的积极影响，为情感启发架构的未来研究提供了依据。

Abstract: Emotion significantly influences cognition, enhancing memory and learning under certain conditions. Drawing on this principle, emotion-augmented deep learning investigates how affective states can improve neural network architectures and learning paradigms, achieving better generalization than non-emotional models. However, existing methods often rely solely on objective neurophysiological factors, neglecting the role of subjectivity in emotion. To bridge this gap, the present study introduces Emotional Regulation, a novel framework for modeling emotion in deep learning through artificial subjective experience. The method employs pre-training based on affective stimuli, balancing non-emotional and emotionally-influenced responses in downstream task optimization. Extensive experimentation was conducted in image classification, pre-training ResNet and ViT architectures on four emotional datasets, using CIFAR-10 and -100 as target benchmarks. Results reveal improvements over the aforementioned backbones, providing evidence of Emotional Regulation as a promising method for defining emotion-augmented deep learning through artificial subjective experience. Furthermore, the proposed approach overcomes the related work in image classification based on CIFAR, revealing Emotional Regulation as the new state-of-the-art in emotion-augmented deep learning for large-scale vision datasets.
